  1570	            # The creation is handed over to the caller: the original
  1571	            # handles are the property of the caller now and they leave
  1572	            # the holding table.  The handover is **one** decision of
  1573	            # the table: the process and the thread are reserved
  1574	            # together, and a handle that the recovery of the façade
  1575	            # claimed is never delivered and never forgotten here.
  1576	            reserved, refused = self._decide_entries(
  1577	                [(process, 'process'), (thread, 'thread')],
  1578	                self.TRANSFER_PENDING)
  1579	            if not refused:
  1580	                self._commit_entries(reserved)
  1581	                return CreatedProcess(process, thread, pid, creation, failures)
  1582	            # The recovery of the façade owns one of the original
  1583	            # handles: the creation may not deliver a stale handle to
  1584	            # the core.  The creation failed and the direct cleanup
  1585	            # below claims the entries that it may claim.
  1586	            self._restore_entries(reserved, self.API_OWNED)
  1587	            primary = BrokerError(
  1588	                'BROKER_PROCESS_HANDOVER_REFUSED',
  1589	                ','.join('%s:%s' % (kind, reason)
  1590	                         for _handle, kind, reason in refused))
  1591	        # The creation failed.  A process that was created is released
  1592	        # through its own original handle and every failure is named.
  1593	        release_failures = []
  1594	        if process is not None:
  1595	            self._release_created(process, thread, release_failures, cancellations)
  1596	        failures.extend(release_failures)
  1597	        if cancellations and primary is None:
  1598	            # The cancellation of the release is the primary error of the
  1599	            # creation when nothing else failed.  Every safe step was
  1600	            # executed before it is raised.
  1601	            primary = cancellations[0]
  1602	            failures.append('creation_cancelled:' + type(primary).__name__)
  1603	        error = primary if primary is not None else cleanup_error
  1604	        detail = getattr(error, 'detail', None)
  1605	        if failures:
  1606	            error.detail = ((detail or '') + '|cleanup=' + ','.join(failures))
  1607	        if cancellations and error is not cancellations[0]:
  1608	            # The primary error and the cancellation coexist: the
  1609	            # cancellation object stays reachable next to the primary
  1610	            # error and it is never replaced by a copy of a message.
  1611	            try:
  1612	                error.lvw_cancelled = cancellations[0]
  1613	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1614	                pass
  1615	        raise error
  1616	
  1617	    def _close_quietly(self, handle, failures, cancellations=None):
  1618	        """Close one handle and name a failure of the close.
  1619	
  1620	        A cancellation in the middle of a close never skips the close
  1621	        that follows it: it is named in the failures and it is handed to
  1622	        the caller, which raises it after every safe step was executed.
  1623	        """
  1624	        try:
  1625	            if not self._api.kernel32.CloseHandle(handle):
  1626	                failures.append('CloseHandle:last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1627	                return False
  1628	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  1629	            failures.append('CloseHandle:CANCELLED')
  1630	            if cancellations is not None:
  1631	                cancellations.append(error)
  1632	            return False
  1633	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1634	            failures.append('CloseHandle:' + type(error).__name__)
  1635	            return False
  1636	        return True
  1637	
  1638	    def _release_created(self, process, thread, failures, cancellations=None):
  1639	        """Release a process that was created but never adopted.
  1640	
  1641	        The process is stopped through its own original handle, its exit
  1642	        is confirmed and both of its handles are closed.  A failure of
  1643	        one of the steps is named and the other steps still run: a handle
  1644	        is never left to the garbage collector.  A cancellation in the
  1645	        middle of the release never skips the close that follows it: it
  1646	        is named and the release continues.
  1647	
  1648	        The direct cleanup of a failed creation is **one** decision of
  1649	        the table, exactly like the recovery of the façade and exactly
  1650	        like the handover of a pipe: the original entries are claimed
  1651	        before they are touched, the decision covers the process and
  1652	        the thread together and a handle that the recovery of the
  1653	        façade already claimed is **never** closed here.  The claim is
  1654	        taken under the lock of the table and the blocking primitives
  1655	        of the release run **outside** of it.
  1656	
  1657	        The confirmation of the exit and the confirmation of the close of
  1658	        a handle are two separate facts and they are recorded separately:
  1659	        a wait that timed out is not an exit and a close that failed is
  1660	        not a close.  A handle that could not be closed stays in the
  1661	        holding table of the façade: the original reference is kept and
  1662	        the recovery of the façade can reach it.
  1663	
  1664	        The **control handle of the process** is the one exception: it
  1665	        is closed only when the exit was really confirmed.  A process
  1666	        whose exit was not confirmed may still be alive - it may be a
  1667	        suspended process that never ran - and its control handle is the
  1668	        only handle that may stop it.  The handle stays in the holding
  1669	        table of the façade with its owner, so that the recovery of the
  1670	        façade can reach it again.
  1671	
  1672	        The cancellations of the release are collected in the list of
  1673	        the caller: they are raised after every safe step was executed
  1674	        and they never replace the primary error of the creation.
  1675	        """
  1676	        if cancellations is None:
  1677	            cancellations = []
  1678	        entries = [(process, 'process')]
  1679	        if thread is not None:
  1680	            entries.append((thread, 'thread'))
  1681	        claimed, refused = self._decide_entries(entries, self.RECOVERY_CLAIMED)
  1682	        for _handle, kind, reason in refused:
  1683	            failures.append('create_release_refused:%s:%s' % (kind, reason))
  1684	        owned = {id(handle) for handle, _kind in claimed}
  1685	        closed = set()
  1686	        if id(process) in owned:
  1687	            try:
  1688	                if not self._api.kernel32.TerminateProcess(process, 1):
  1689	                    failures.append('TerminateProcess:last_error=%d'
  1690	                                    % self._last_error())
  1691	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  1692	                failures.append('TerminateProcess:CANCELLED')
  1693	                cancellations.append(error)
  1694	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1695	                failures.append('TerminateProcess:' + type(error).__name__)
  1696	            confirmed = self._confirm_process_exit(process, failures, cancellations)
  1697	            if not confirmed:
  1698	                # The process may still be alive.  Its control handle
  1699	                # stays with the façade: it is never closed and never
  1700	                # forgotten.
  1701	                failures.append('process_control_handle_retained:exit_not_confirmed')
  1702	                self._note(process, 'exit_not_confirmed:control_handle_retained')
  1703	            elif self._close_quietly(process, failures, cancellations):
  1704	                self._forget(process)
  1705	                closed.add(id(process))
  1706	            else:
  1707	                self._note(process, 'close_not_confirmed')
  1708	        # The thread handle is independent of the exit of the process: it
  1709	        # is closed on its own and it never waits for the process.
  1710	        if thread is not None and id(thread) in owned:
  1711	            if self._close_quietly(thread, failures, cancellations):
  1712	                self._forget(thread)
  1713	                closed.add(id(thread))
  1714	            else:
  1715	                self._note(thread, 'close_not_confirmed')
  1716	        # A claim that did not end with a confirmed close is given back:
  1717	        # a later recovery of the round may claim the entry again and it
  1718	        # may confirm the exit.
  1719	        for handle, _kind in claimed:
  1720	            if id(handle) not in closed:
  1721	                self._release_claim(id(handle))
  1722	        if cancellations:
  1723	            failures.append('release_cancelled:' + type(cancellations[0]).__name__)
  1724	        return failures
  1725	
  1726	    def _process_creation_time(self, process):
  1727	        """The birth time of one process, as the 100 nanosecond tick count.
  1728	
  1729	        The query is the identity of the process: a process whose birth
  1730	        time cannot be read has no proven identity and is not used.
  1731	        """
  1732	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1733	        creation = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
  1734	        exit_time = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
  1735	        kernel = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
  1736	        user = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
  1737	        if not self._api.kernel32.GetProcessTimes(
  1738	                process, ctypes.byref(creation), ctypes.byref(exit_time),
  1739	                ctypes.byref(kernel), ctypes.byref(user)):
  1740	            error = self._last_error()
  1741	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PROCESS_BIRTH_QUERY_FAILED',
  1742	                              'last_error=%d' % error)
  1743	        return (int(creation.dwHighDateTime) << 32) | int(creation.dwLowDateTime)
  1744	
  1745	    def assign_process_to_job(self, job, process):
  1746	        if not self._api.kernel32.AssignProcessToJobObject(job, process):
  1747	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_ASSIGN_FAILED',
  1748	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1749	        return True
  1750	
  1751	    def resume_thread(self, thread):
  1752	        result = self._api.kernel32.ResumeThread(thread)
  1753	        if result == 0xFFFFFFFF:
  1754	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_RESUME_FAILED',
  1755	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1756	        return int(result)
  1757	
  1758	    def terminate_process(self, process, exit_code):
  1759	        if not self._api.kernel32.TerminateProcess(process, exit_code):
  1760	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_TERMINATE_FAILED',
  1761	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1762	        return True
  1763	
  1764	    def wait_process(self, process, timeout_ms):
  1765	        result = self._api.kernel32.WaitForSingleObject(process, timeout_ms)
  1766	        if result == self.WAIT_OBJECT_0:
  1767	            return True
  1768	        if result == self.WAIT_TIMEOUT:
  1769	            return False
  1770	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_WAIT_FAILED', 'wait=%d' % int(result))
  1771	
  1772	    def exit_code(self, process):
  1773	        code = self._api.types['DWORD'](0)
  1774	        if not self._api.kernel32.GetExitCodeProcess(
  1775	                process, self._api.types['ctypes'].byref(code)):
  1776	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_EXIT_CODE_FAILED',
  1777	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1778	        return int(code.value)
  1779	
  1780	    def close_handle(self, handle):
  1781	        if not self._api.kernel32.CloseHandle(handle):
  1782	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED',
  1783	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1784	        # The close was confirmed: the handle leaves the holding table
  1785	        # of the façade.  A handle that could not be closed stays in it
  1786	        # with its owner and it is reachable by the recovery.
  1787	        self._forget(handle)
  1788	        return True
  1789	
  1790	    # -- the pipes ---------------------------------------------------------
  1791	    def create_pipe(self, name):
  1792	        """Create one pipe.  Only the write end is inheritable.
  1793	
  1794	        The security attributes of the pipe say that the ends are
  1795	        inheritable and the inherit flag of the *read* end is cleared
  1796	        right afterwards.  A pipe that was created with ``NULL``
  1797	        security attributes can never be inherited at all.
  1798	
  1799	        Both ends enter the holding table of this façade in the moment
  1800	        of their creation.  A pipe whose inherit flag could not be
  1801	        cleared therefore keeps its original ends with their owner: the
  1802	        core never received the pair and the round never loses the
  1803	        ownership of a handle that it created.
  1804	
  1805	        The failure of the inherit flag is cleaned up **independently**
  1806	        of the two ends.  A cancellation of the inherit flag and a
  1807	        cancellation of the close of one end never skip the close of the
  1808	        other end: the safe close of both ends always runs and the
  1809	        cancellation is raised afterwards.  The end that could not be
  1810	        closed keeps its original reference with the façade and the end
  1811	        that was closed with a confirmed close leaves the table.
  1812	        """
  1813	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1814	        read = self._api.types['HANDLE']()
  1815	        write = self._api.types['HANDLE']()
  1816	        attributes = self._security_attributes(True)
  1817	        if not self._api.kernel32.CreatePipe(ctypes.byref(read), ctypes.byref(write),
  1818	                                             ctypes.byref(attributes), 0):
  1819	            error = self._last_error()
  1820	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_FAILED', 'last_error=%d' % error)
  1821	        # The moment of the creation of the two ends.
  1822	        self._retain(read, 'pipe_read')
  1823	        self._retain(write, 'pipe_write')
  1824	        primary = None
  1825	        inherited = False
  1826	        try:
  1827	            inherited = bool(self._api.kernel32.SetHandleInformation(
  1828	                read, self.HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0))
  1829	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  1830	            # The cancellation of the inherit flag is the primary error
  1831	            # of the pipe and it never skips the safe close of the ends.
  1832	            primary = error
  1833	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1834	            primary = BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_INHERIT_FAILED',
  1835	                                  'last_error=%d:%s' % (self._last_error(),
  1836	                                                        type(error).__name__))
  1837	        if inherited:
  1838	            return read, write
  1839	        error = self._last_error()
  1840	        failures = []
  1841	        cancellations = []
  1842	        if self._close_quietly(read, failures, cancellations):
  1843	            self._forget(read)
  1844	        if self._close_quietly(write, failures, cancellations):
  1845	            self._forget(write)
  1846	        if primary is None:
  1847	            primary = BrokerError(
  1848	                'BROKER_PIPE_INHERIT_FAILED',
  1849	                'last_error=%d%s' % (error,
  1850	                                     ('|cleanup=' + ','.join(failures))
  1851	                                     if failures else ''))
  1852	        else:
  1853	            try:
  1854	                primary.detail = ((getattr(primary, 'detail', None) or '')
  1855	                                  + '|cleanup=' + ','.join(failures))
  1856	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1857	                pass
  1858	        if cancellations:
  1859	            # The cancellation of a close is the error that leaves the
  1860	            # method and the primary error stays reachable next to it:
  1861	            # a cancellation never becomes an ordinary evidence and it
  1862	            # never hides the error that caused the pipe.
  1863	            cancel = cancellations[0]
  1864	            try:
  1865	                cancel.lvw_primary = primary
  1866	                primary.lvw_cancelled = cancel
  1867	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1868	                pass
  1869	            raise cancel
  1870	        raise primary
  1871	
  1872	    def read_stream(self, handle, size):
  1873	        """Read one block of a child stream.
  1874	
  1875	        The end of a stream is an end and not an error, but only in the
  1876	        branches that Windows documents as the end of a pipe: a broken
  1877	        pipe, a pipe that is not connected any more, the end of the file
  1878	        and an operation that was aborted.  Every other failure of the
  1879	        read is a failure of the round and it is named.
  1880	        """
  1881	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1882	        buffer = ctypes.create_string_buffer(size)
  1883	        read = self._api.types['DWORD'](0)
  1884	        if not self._api.kernel32.ReadFile(handle, buffer, size,
  1885	                                           ctypes.byref(read), None):
  1886	            error = self._last_error()
  1887	            if error in (self.ERROR_BROKEN_PIPE, self.ERROR_PIPE_NOT_CONNECTED,
  1888	                         self.ERROR_HANDLE_EOF, self.ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED):
  1889	                return b''
  1890	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_READ_FAILED', 'last_error=%d' % error)
  1891	        return buffer.raw[:read.value]
  1892	
  1893	    def write_stream(self, handle, data):
  1894	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1895	        buffer = ctypes.create_string_buffer(bytes(data), len(data))
  1896	        written = self._api.types['DWORD'](0)
  1897	        if not self._api.kernel32.WriteFile(handle, buffer, len(data),
  1898	                                            ctypes.byref(written), None):
  1899	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_WRITE_FAILED',
  1900	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1901	        return int(written.value)
  1902	
  1903	    # -- the cancellation of a synchronous operation -----------------------
  1904	    def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
  1905	        """The original handle of the calling thread.
  1906	
  1907	        The handle is the handle of the thread itself and never a self
  1908	        reported identifier: ``CancelSynchronousIo`` cancels the
  1909	        operation of one thread and it needs the real handle of that
  1910	        thread.
  1911	        """
  1912	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1913	        thread = self._api.kernel32.GetCurrentThread()
  1914	        process = self._api.kernel32.GetCurrentProcess()
  1915	        duplicate = self._api.types['HANDLE']()
  1916	        if not self._api.kernel32.DuplicateHandle(process, thread, process,
  1917	                                                  ctypes.byref(duplicate), 0,
  1918	                                                  False, self.DUPLICATE_SAME_ACCESS):
  1919	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_THREAD_HANDLE_FAILED',
  1920	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1921	        return duplicate
  1922	
  1923	    def cancel_synchronous_io(self, thread_handle):
  1924	        """Ask for the cancellation of the synchronous operation of a thread.
  1925	
  1926	        The call is a **request** and not a completion.  It may report
  1927	        ``ERROR_NOT_FOUND`` because the operation had not started yet or
  1928	        because it had just finished: the caller has to read the real
  1929	        completion of the thread and to join it.  The return value of
  1930	        this method names the state of the request.
  1931	        """
  1932	        if not self._api.kernel32.CancelSynchronousIo(thread_handle):
  1933	            error = self._last_error()
  1934	            if error == self.ERROR_NOT_FOUND:
  1935	                return 'NOT_FOUND'
  1936	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_FAILED',
  1937	                              'last_error=%d' % error)
  1938	        return 'REQUESTED'
  1939	
  1940	
  1941	# -- the fixed command of a role ------------------------------------------
  1942	
  1943	def _base_environment():
  1944	    environment = {key: value for key, value in os.environ.items()
  1945	                   if key.upper() not in REMOVED_ENVIRONMENT}
  1946	    environment['PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE'] = '1'
  1947	    environment['PYTHONNOUSERSITE'] = '1'
  1948	    environment['NO_PROXY'] = '127.0.0.1,localhost'
  1949	    return environment
  1950	
  1951	
  1952	def _comfy_environment(root):
  1953	    environment = _base_environment()
  1954	    temporary = str(Path(root) / 'temp' / 'service-os')
  1955	    environment.update({
  1956	        'HF_HUB_OFFLINE': '1',
  1957	        'TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE': '1',
  1958	        'HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE': '1',
  1959	        'TEMP': temporary,
  1960	        'TMP': temporary,
  1961	        'TORCH_HOME': str(Path(temporary) / 'torch'),
  1962	        'HF_HOME': str(Path(temporary) / 'huggingface'),
  1963	        'XDG_CACHE_HOME': str(Path(temporary) / 'cache'),
  1964	    })
  1965	    return environment
  1966	
  1967	
  1968	def _ollama_environment():
  1969	    environment = _base_environment()
  1970	    environment['OLLAMA_HOST'] = '127.0.0.1:11434'
  1971	    environment['OLLAMA_NO_CLOUD'] = '1'
  1972	    return environment
  1973	
  1974	
  1975	def role_command(role, run_root, owned_pids):
  1976	    """Assemble the fixed command of one role.
  1977	
  1978	    The command form of a role is frozen in this module and it is
  1979	    assembled from the tools that the plan closed.  The plan never carries
  1980	    an argument vector, a shell, a script or an environment: it carries
  1981	    the tools and the closed data of the role, and this function is the
  1982	    only place where a command line of this package exists.
  1983	
  1984	    The forms are the ones of the already authorised launch of this pack:
  1985	    the ComfyUI launch of ``reports/v0-s1/wp-s1-03-phase-a/live_probe.py``
  1986	    and of ``tools/v0_s2_driver.py``, the Ollama launch of the same
  1987	    wrapper, and the sampler launch of ``tools/v0_s2_runtime.py``.
  1988	
  1989	    The private process ids of the sampler are passed as *separate*
  1990	    arguments: the parser of the sampler is ``type=int, nargs='+'`` and
  1991	    one argument that carries a space would not be an integer.
  1992	    """
  1993	    name = role['role']
  1994	    tools = role['tools']
  1995	    data = role['data']
  1996	    root = str(run_root)
  1997	    if name == 'COMFY':
  1998	        python = tools[0]['path']
  1999	        main = tools[1]['path']
  2000	        arguments = ['-s', '-B', main,
  2001	                     '--listen', '127.0.0.1', '--port', '8191',
  2002	                     '--base-directory', root,
  2003	                     '--user-directory', str(Path(root) / data['user_directory']),
  2004	                     '--disable-auto-launch', '--disable-api-nodes',
  2005	                     '--disable-all-custom-nodes',
  2006	                     '--whitelist-custom-nodes', 'lvw_v0_bridge']
  2007	        return {'executable': python, 'arguments': arguments,
  2008	                'working_directory': root, 'environment': _comfy_environment(root)}
  2009	    if name == 'OLLAMA':
  2010	        executable = tools[0]['path']
  2011	        return {'executable': executable, 'arguments': ['serve'],
  2012	                'working_directory': root, 'environment': _ollama_environment()}
  2013	    if name == 'SAMPLER':
  2014	        python = tools[0]['path']
  2015	        script = tools[1]['path']
  2016	        directory = str(Path(root) / 'temp' / 'resource-sampler')
  2017	        arguments = ['-I', '-B', script, '--execute-sampler',
  2018	                     '--run-root', directory,
  2019	                     '--nvidia-smi', data['nvidia_exe'],
  2020	                     '--binary-sha256', data['nvidia_sha256'],
  2021	                     '--private-pids'] + [str(pid) for pid in owned_pids] + \
  2022	                    ['--max-seconds', str(data['max_seconds'])]
  2023	        return {'executable': python, 'arguments': arguments,
  2024	                'working_directory': directory, 'environment': _base_environment()}
  2025	    raise BrokerError('BROKER_ROLE_UNKNOWN', name)
  2026	
  2027	
  2028	# -- the bounded reader of a child stream ----------------------------------
  2029	
  2030	class _BoundedStreamReader(threading.Thread):
  2031	    """One bounded background reader of one stream of a child.
  2032	
  2033	    The thread only reads.  It never writes, it never calls the broker and
  2034	    it never waits for the broker.  The broker takes the published counts
  2035	    out of it at its own checkpoints, so the deadline of the round is never
  2036	    held up by a silent child.
  2037	
  2038	    A read that failed for a reason that Windows does not document as the
  2039	    end of a pipe is a named error of the round: the reader keeps it and
  2040	    the round is not a release.
  2041	    """
  2042	
  2043	    def __init__(self, win32, handle, *, limit, name):
  2044	        super().__init__(daemon=True, name='lvw-native-custody-stream-' + name)
  2045	        self.win32 = win32
  2046	        self.handle = handle
  2047	        self.limit = limit
  2048	        self.observed = 0
  2049	        self.kept = 0
  2050	        self.overflow = False
  2051	        self.error = None
  2052	        self.finished = threading.Event()
  2053	
  2054	    def run(self):
  2055	        try:
  2056	            while True:
  2057	                block = self.win32.read_stream(self.handle, 65536)
  2058	                if not block:
  2059	                    break
  2060	                self.observed += len(block)
  2061	                if self.observed > self.limit:
  2062	                    self.overflow = True
  2063	                    break
  2064	                self.kept += len(block)
  2065	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2066	            self.error = getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__
  2067	        finally:
  2068	            self.finished.set()
  2069	
  2070	
  2071	# -- the pending resources of one round ------------------------------------
  2072	
  2073	def _json_fact(value):
  2074	    """One fact of the evidence of the round that is always serialisable.
  2075	
  2076	    A handle is an integer in the offline validation and an opaque
  2077	    object in a real run: the evidence of the round carries a printable
  2078	    name of it and it never carries the object itself.  A callable is
  2079	    never serialisable either and it is named instead.
  2080	    """
  2081	    if value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, int, float, str)):
  2082	        return value
  2083	    return repr(value)
  2084	
  2085	
  2086	class _PendingResources:
  2087	    """The pending resource table of one round.
  2088	
  2089	    Every handle that this round creates enters this table in the moment
  2090	    of its successful creation.  A handle **stays** in the table until it
  2091	    is closed with a confirmed close: the handover of a handle to its
  2092	    owner changes the owner and nothing else.  The handle is a resource
  2093	    that this round owns and it is named until it is really closed.
  2094	
  2095	    A handle that could not be closed stays in the table with its owner:
  2096	    it is a resource that this round still owns and it is named in the
  2097	    evidence of the round.
  2098	
  2099	    A handle of another owner is never in this table and it is never
  2100	    touched.
  2101	
  2102	    The table carries the **owner object** of a handle and not only its
  2103	    name: a handle that is owned by a live reader is protected from the
  2104	    pending pass by the fact that the reader is alive and not by a
  2105	    string that somebody may write.  The owner object is the reader
  2106	    itself and the table asks it.
  2107	    """
  2108	
  2109	    def __init__(self, win32):
  2110	        self._win32 = win32
  2111	        self._items = []
  2112	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
  2113	
  2114	    def add(self, handle, kind, *, role=None, owner=None, owner_ref=None,
  2115	            closeable=None):
  2116	        """Enter one handle into the table of the round.
  2117	
  2118	        The ``closeable`` predicate is the **close eligibility** of the
  2119	        handle.  It is asked by every close of the table and it is the
  2120	        authority of the question "may this handle be closed now?": a
  2121	        control handle of a process whose exit was not confirmed is
  2122	        never closed, and the predicate is the **owner** of that fact
  2123	        and not a name of it.
  2124	
  2125	        The ``owner_ref`` is the object of the owner of the handle: the
  2126	        pending pass asks it before it closes the handle.
  2127	        """
  2128	        item = {'handle': handle, 'kind': kind, 'role': role,
  2129	                'owner': owner or 'PENDING', 'owner_ref': owner_ref,
  2130	                'closeable': closeable, 'closed': False}
  2131	        with self._lock:
  2132	            self._items.append(item)
  2133	        return item
  2134	
  2135	    def take(self, handle):
  2136	        """Remove one handle from the table: its owner holds it now."""
  2137	        with self._lock:
  2138	            for index, item in enumerate(self._items):
  2139	                if item['handle'] == handle:
  2140	                    del self._items[index]
  2141	                    return item
  2142	        return None
  2143	
  2144	    def find(self, handle):
  2145	        """The entry of one handle, without removing it from the table.
  2146	
  2147	        The entry carries the **owner** of the handle and its close
  2148	        eligibility.  A close that is confirmed needs the owner of the
  2149	        handle in order to notify it: a notification that names a copy
  2150	        of the entry would notify nobody.
  2151	        """
  2152	        with self._lock:
  2153	            for item in self._items:
  2154	                if item['handle'] == handle:
  2155	                    return item
  2156	        return None
  2157	
  2158	    def transfer(self, handle, owner, *, owner_ref=None):
  2159	        """Hand one pending handle over to its owner.
  2160	
  2161	        The owner of the handle is only changed after the handover
  2162	        succeeded.  The handle stays in the table until it is closed: it
  2163	        is a resource that this round owns and it is named until then.
  2164	        A handle that could not be handed over keeps the owner
  2165	        ``PENDING`` and it is closed by the round.
  2166	
  2167	        The ``owner_ref`` is the **object** of the new owner.  It is
  2168	        what the pending pass asks before it closes a handle: a read end
  2169	        that a live reader still reads from is never closed under it.
  2170	        """
  2171	        with self._lock:
  2172	            for item in self._items:
  2173	                if item['handle'] == handle:
  2174	                    item['owner'] = owner
  2175	                    if owner_ref is not None:
  2176	                        item['owner_ref'] = owner_ref
  2177	                    return item
  2178	        return None
  2179	
  2180	    def pending(self):
  2181	        """The handles that this round still owns and has not closed."""
  2182	        with self._lock:
  2183	            return [dict(item) for item in self._items]
  2184	
  2185	    def pending_facts(self):
  2186	        """The pending handles of the round as **serialisable** facts.
  2187	
  2188	        The close eligibility of a handle is a **callable** over the
  2189	        owner of the handle and a callable is never serialised.  The
  2190	        facts name the eligibility as a name and they keep the
  2191	        **original** reference of the handle: a resource of the round is
  2192	        never replaced by a copy of a message.
  2193	        """
  2194	        facts = []
  2195	        for item in self.pending():
  2196	            predicate = item.get('closeable')
  2197	            if predicate is None:
  2198	                eligibility = 'NONE'
  2199	            elif callable(predicate):
  2200	                eligibility = 'CALLABLE'
  2201	            else:
  2202	                eligibility = 'FROZEN'
  2203	            facts.append({'handle': _json_fact(item.get('handle')),
  2204	                          'kind': item.get('kind'),
  2205	                          'role': item.get('role'),
  2206	                          'owner': item.get('owner'),
  2207	                          'closed': bool(item.get('closed')),
  2208	                          'eligibility': eligibility})
  2209	        return facts
  2210	
  2211	    def close_all(self, failures, on_closed=None, protect=None):
  2212	        """Close every handle that is still pending and name the failures.
  2213	
  2214	        A handle that could not be closed stays in the table: it is a
  2215	        resource that this round still owns and it is named in the
  2216	        evidence of the round.  It is never left to the garbage
  2217	        collector and it is never closed twice.
  2218	
  2219	        Every handle is closed on its own: a cancellation in the middle
  2220	        of the group never skips the handles that follow it.  The
  2221	        cancellation is raised after every handle was coordinated and it
  2222	        is never turned into an ordinary evidence.
  2223	
  2224	        A handle that was closed with a **confirmed** close is reported
  2225	        to the owner of the table through ``on_closed``: the core has to
  2226	        learn that the handle is really closed, so that it never names a
  2227	        closed handle any more.  The report happens before the
  2228	        cancellation is raised: the bookkeeping of the core is never
  2229	        left behind the error that left the cleanup.
  2230	
  2231	        The ``protect`` predicate is the **real** guard of the pass: a
  2232	        handle whose owner is still using it is never closed under it,
  2233	        whatever the branch that reached the pass did before.  The
  2234	        guard is asked about the owner and not about a name of an owner.
  2235	
  2236	        The **close eligibility** of a handle is asked here as well: a
  2237	        handle that may not be closed yet - the control handle of a
  2238	        process whose exit was not confirmed - is never closed by the
  2239	        pass.  The eligibility comes from the **same** owner that the
  2240	        release of the records asks: the pass may not decide the
  2241	        question on its own and it may not close behind the back of the
  2242	        owner of the handle.
  2243	        """
  2244	        remaining = []
  2245	        cancellations = []
  2246	        with self._lock:
  2247	            items = list(self._items)
  2248	        for item in items:
  2249	            if protect is not None and protect(item):
  2250	                # The handle is in use by a live owner: it is never
  2251	                # closed under it and it keeps its owner.
  2252	                remaining.append(item)
  2253	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2254	                                 'role': item['role'],
  2255	                                 'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_HANDLE_IN_USE'})
  2256	                continue
  2257	            if not self._is_closeable(item):
  2258	                # The handle may not be closed yet: it keeps its owner
  2259	                # and the round keeps the fact.
  2260	                remaining.append(item)
  2261	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2262	                                 'role': item['role'],
  2263	                                 'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_CLOSE_NOT_ELIGIBLE'})
  2264	                continue
  2265	            try:
  2266	                self._win32.close_handle(item['handle'])
  2267	            except BrokerError as error:
  2268	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2269	                                 'role': item['role'], 'code': error.code})
  2270	                remaining.append(item)
  2271	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  2272	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2273	                                 'role': item['role'],
  2274	                                 'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  2275	                                 'cancellation': True})
  2276	                remaining.append(item)
  2277	                cancellations.append(error)
  2278	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2279	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2280	                                 'role': item['role'],
  2281	                                 'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  2282	                remaining.append(item)
  2283	            else:
  2284	                item['closed'] = True
  2285	                if on_closed is not None:
  2286	                    on_closed(item)
  2287	        with self._lock:
  2288	            self._items = remaining
  2289	        if cancellations:
  2290	            raise cancellations[0]
  2291	        return remaining
  2292	
  2293	    @staticmethod
  2294	    def _is_closeable(item):
  2295	        """Whether one pending handle may be closed now.
  2296	
  2297	        The predicate of the entry is the authority of the question.  A
  2298	        predicate that cannot answer protects the handle: an unknown
  2299	        fact is never a permission to close.
  2300	
  2301	        The predicate is a **callable** over the original owner of the
  2302	        handle.  A frozen answer - a boolean that was taken at the
  2303	        moment of the creation of the handle - is **not** an
  2304	        eligibility: it protects the handle, because the current fact
  2305	        of the owner is the only authority of the question.
  2306	        """
  2307	        predicate = item.get('closeable')
  2308	        if predicate is None:
  2309	            return True
  2310	        if not callable(predicate):
  2311	            # A frozen answer is not an eligibility.
  2312	            return False
  2313	        try:
  2314	            return bool(predicate())
  2315	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2316	            return False
  2317	
  2318	
  2319	# -- the core of the broker ------------------------------------------------
  2320	
  2321	class BrokerCore:
  2322	    """The job and the handle custody of one native custody round.
  2323	
  2324	    The core owns exactly one job object and the original handles of the
  2325	    processes of the round.  Every process of the round is created
  2326	    suspended, it is assigned to the job of the round and only then it is
  2327	    resumed.
  2328	
  2329	    A failure never falls back to a weaker behaviour.  There is no retry
  2330	    without the job, there is no adoption of a process that was not
  2331	    created here and there is no stop of a process that this core does not
  2332	    hold a handle of.
  2333	    """
  2334	
  2335	    def __init__(self, *, win32, plan, run_root, clock=None, wall=None,
  2336	                 context=None):
  2337	        self.win32 = win32
  2338	        self.plan = plan
  2339	        self.run_root = Path(run_root)
  2340	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  2341	        self._wall = wall or time.time
  2342	        # The ONE context of the round.  The core, the loop, the
  2343	        # transport of the channel and the endpoints of the channel hold
  2344	        # this very object: a layer that only copied the number of a
  2345	        # deadline would be a layer with a deadline of its own.
  2346	        self.context = context if context is not None else _RoundContext(
  2347	            clock=self._clock, wall=self._wall)
  2348	        # The façade of the primitives reads the absolute deadline and
  2349	        # the published cleanup deadline of the round from the **same**
  2350	        # context: a recovery of the façade may not open a budget of
  2351	        # its own.
  2352	        binder = getattr(self.win32, 'bind_context', None)
  2353	        if callable(binder):
  2354	            try:
  2355	                binder(self.context)
  2356	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2357	                pass
  2358	        self.state = 'NEW'
  2359	        self.job = None
  2360	        self.job_kill_on_close = False
  2361	        self.job_closed = False
  2362	        self.job_terminated = False
  2363	        self.job_active = None
  2364	        self.job_active_known = False
  2365	        self.job_process_ids = []
  2366	        self.records = []
  2367	        self.failures = []
  2368	        self.readers = []
  2369	        self.reader_states = []
  2370	        self.new_text_bytes = 0
  2371	        self.stop_reason = None
  2372	        self._stream_failure_reported = False
  2373	        self._text_failure_reported = False
  2374	        self._pending = _PendingResources(win32)
  2375	        # The close eligibility of the control handle of every record of
  2376	        # the round.  The map is keyed by the identity of the record and
  2377	        # it holds the **callable** over that record: the pending table
  2378	        # and the release of the records ask the very same callable and
  2379	        # they never disagree about the current exit fact.
  2380	        self._process_eligibility = {}
  2381	        # The resources that this core could not release.  A resource
  2382	        # that could not be closed keeps its original reference and its
  2383	        # owner here: it is named in the evidence of the round and it is
  2384	        # never replaced by a copy of an error message.
  2385	        self._retained_resources = []
  2386	        # The cancellations that were observed during the coordination of
  2387	        # the readers.  They are raised after every reader was
  2388	        # coordinated and they never replace an ordinary failure.
  2389	        self._readers_cancelled = []
  2390	        # The cancellations that were observed during the wait of the
  2391	        # original processes.  They are raised after every process was
  2392	        # coordinated.
  2393	        self._processes_cancelled = []
  2394	        # The absolute deadline of the round.  It is bound by the loop
  2395	        # before the job of the round exists and it is the upper bound of
  2396	        # the one cleanup deadline of the round.
  2397	        self._absolute_deadline = None
  2398	        # The ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  It is generated at the
  2399	        # first stop or at the first failure of the cleanup and it is the
  2400	        # same value in every step of the cleanup.
  2401	        self._cleanup_deadline_value = None
  2402	        self._released = False
  2403	        self._abort_reported = False
  2404	        self.broker_identity = {
  2405	            'pid': os.getpid(),
  2406	            'executable': sys.executable,
  2407	            'reported_by_the_broker': True,
  2408	            'identity_source': 'CHILD_CLAIM',
  2409	        }
  2410	
  2411	    # -- the deadlines of the round ---------------------------------------
  2412	    def bind_clock(self, clock):
  2413	        """Bind the clock of the round to this core.
  2414	
  2415	        The core, the loop and the context of the round read the same
  2416	        clock: the absolute deadline of the round and the ONE cleanup
  2417	        deadline are in one domain and a deadline of one domain is never
  2418	        compared with a clock of another one.
  2419	        """
  2420	        if clock is not None:
  2421	            self._clock = clock
  2422	            self.context.clock = clock
  2423	        return self._clock
  2424	
  2425	    def bind_absolute_deadline(self, deadline):
  2426	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to this core.
  2427	
  2428	        The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
  2429	        bound.  It is the upper bound of the one cleanup deadline of the
  2430	        round: the cleanup never outlives the round itself.
  2431	        """
  2432	        value = self.context.bind_absolute_deadline(deadline)
  2433	        self._absolute_deadline = value
  2434	        # The façade of the primitives is bound to the same deadline: its
  2435	        # waits are bounded by the deadline of the round and they never
  2436	        # start a wait with a fresh timeout of their own.
  2437	        binder = getattr(self.win32, 'bind_deadline', None)
  2438	        if callable(binder):
  2439	            try:
  2440	                binder(value, self._clock)
  2441	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2442	                pass
  2443	        return value
  2444	
  2445	    def cleanup_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  2446	        """The ONE absolute deadline of the cleanup of this round.
  2447	
  2448	        The deadline is generated once, at the first stop or at the first
  2449	        failure of the cleanup, and it is published **atomically** by the
  2450	        context of the round.  Two callers that entered the cleanup at
  2451	        the same moment therefore see the very same value and the
  2452	        deadline is never generated twice.
  2453	
  2454	        It is the earlier of the absolute deadline of the round and the
  2455	        fixed cleanup budget, measured from the moment of the first
  2456	        cleanup.  Every later call returns the very same value: no layer
  2457	        of the cleanup adds a budget of its own and no layer starts a
  2458	        new wait with a fresh timeout.
  2459	        """
  2460	        value = self.context.cleanup_deadline(deadline)
  2461	        self._cleanup_deadline_value = value
  2462	        return value
  2463	
  2464	    def remaining_cleanup_seconds(self, deadline=None):
  2465	        """The seconds that are left until the cleanup deadline.
  2466	
  2467	        The value is recomputed on every call: a wait that already
  2468	        consumed part of the budget never gets the whole budget again.
  2469	        """
  2470	        return self.context.remaining_cleanup(deadline)
  2471	
  2472	    def check_startup_budget(self, stage):
  2473	        """Check the shared budget of the startup **before** a step.
  2474	
  2475	        A step of the startup of the round - the creation of a process,
  2476	        the assignment of a process to the job, the resume of its
  2477	        thread, the start of a reader and the creation of the next role
  2478	        - checks the shared cancel and the shared deadline of the round
  2479	        before it happens and after it happened.
  2480	
  2481	        A step that runs after the deadline arrived may only clean up:
  2482	        it never resumes a thread and it never creates the next role.
  2483	        """
  2484	        context = self.context
  2485	        if context.cancel.is_set() or context.is_unknown():
  2486	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', stage)
  2487	        deadline = context.absolute_deadline
  2488	        if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
  2489	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', stage)
  2490	        return True
  2491	
  2492	    def _retain_resource(self, kind, handle, code=None, owner='CORE_RETAINED'):
  2493	        """Keep the original reference of a resource that was not released."""
  2494	        for item in self._retained_resources:
  2495	            if item['kind'] == kind and item['handle'] is handle:
  2496	                return item
  2497	        item = {'kind': kind, 'handle': handle, 'owner': owner, 'code': code}
  2498	        self._retained_resources.append(item)
  2499	        return item
  2500	
  2501	    def _release_resource(self, kind, handle):
  2502	        """Forget a resource that was released with a confirmed close.
  2503	
  2504	        The **original reference** of a resource is its identity: a
  2505	        handle that was really closed is forgotten whatever name it was
  2506	        retained under.  A resource that was not closed keeps its owner
  2507	        and it stays named: the retained list is never cleared to make a
  2508	        check green.
  2509	        """
  2510	        self._retained_resources = [item for item in self._retained_resources
  2511	                                   if item['handle'] is not handle]
  2512	
  2513	    def _adopt_from_api(self, entries, role):
  2514	        """Take the named handles over from the façade of the primitives.
  2515	
  2516	        The receiver registers the handles and the façade forgets them
  2517	        in **one** atomic step.  A handle that both of them names would
  2518	        have two independent owners and the recovery of the façade could
  2519	        close a handle that a live reader of the round is reading from.
  2520	        A handle that left the façade before its receiver registered it
  2521	        would have no owner at all.
  2522	
  2523	        The façade that does not offer the atomic handover is asked to
  2524	        forget the handles after the registration: the receiver is
  2525	        always registered first, so that a failure in between leaves the
  2526	        handle with **two** owners and never with none.
  2527	        """
  2528	        items = list(entries)
  2529	
  2530	        def register(handles):
  2531	            for handle, kind in handles:
  2532	                self._pending.add(handle, kind, role=role)
  2533	
  2534	        handover = getattr(self.win32, 'handover', None)
  2535	        if callable(handover):
  2536	            handover(items, owner='CORE', register=register)
  2537	            return
  2538	        register(items)
  2539	        forget = getattr(self.win32, 'forget_handle', None)
  2540	        if callable(forget):
  2541	            for handle, _kind in items:
  2542	                forget(handle)
  2543	
  2544	    def _api_retained(self):
  2545	        """The original handles that the Windows façade still holds.
  2546	
  2547	        The façade is the owner of a handle that it could not hand over
  2548	        and it is the owner of the recovery of that handle.  The core
  2549	        keeps the façade, so the core can always reach these handles.
  2550	
  2551	        The names of the handles are the printable ones: the evidence of
  2552	        a round is a document and it never carries a raw handle value.
  2553	        """
  2554	        getter = getattr(self.win32, 'retained_handles', None)
  2555	        if not callable(getter):
  2556	            return []
  2557	        try:
  2558	            entries = list(getter())
  2559	        except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2560	            return []
  2561	        return [{'kind': entry.get('kind'), 'pid': entry.get('pid'),
  2562	                 'stage': entry.get('stage'), 'owner': entry.get('owner'),
  2563	                 'failures': list(entry.get('failures', ())),
  2564	                 'handle_repr': repr(entry.get('handle'))}
  2565	                for entry in entries]
  2566	
  2567	    # -- the arming of the round ------------------------------------------
  2568	    def arm(self):
  2569	        """Create the job object of the round.
  2570	
  2571	        The job is created before any process of the round exists, so that
  2572	        every process of the round can be assigned to it before it runs.
  2573	
  2574	        The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** the
  2575	        creation of the job and **after** it: a job that was created
  2576	        after the deadline of the round arrived is only cleaned up and it
  2577	        is never armed.
  2578	        """
  2579	        if self.state != 'NEW':
  2580	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STATE_INVALID', self.state)
  2581	        self.state = 'VALIDATED'
  2582	        self.check_startup_budget('ARM:JOB')
  2583	        try:
  2584	            self.job = self.win32.create_job()
  2585	        except BrokerError as error:
  2586	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_job', 'code': error.code})
  2587	            self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
  2588	            raise
  2589	        self._pending.add(self.job, 'job')
  2590	        self._pending.transfer(self.job, 'CORE')
  2591	        try:
  2592	            self.win32.set_job_kill_on_close(self.job)
  2593	        except BrokerError as error:
  2594	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'set_job_limits', 'code': error.code})
  2595	            closed = self._close_handle('job', self.job)
  2596	            if closed:
  2597	                # The job handle was closed with a confirmed close: it may
  2598	                # be forgotten.
  2599	                self.job = None
  2600	            else:
  2601	                # The job handle was not closed.  The original reference
  2602	                # stays with its owner and it is named: a handle that may
  2603	                # not be closed is never forgotten and it is never
  2604	                # replaced by a copy of an error message.
  2605	                self._retain_resource('job', self.job, error.code)
  2606	            self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
  2607	            raise
  2608	        self.job_kill_on_close = True
  2609	        self.state = 'ARMED'
  2610	        # The budget is checked after the creation of the job: a job that
  2611	        # was created after the deadline arrived is only cleaned up.
  2612	        self.check_startup_budget('ARM:DONE')
  2613	        return self.evidence()
  2614	
  2615	    # -- the launch of the roles ------------------------------------------
  2616	    def launch(self):
  2617	        """Create the roles of the round, one after the other.
  2618	
  2619	        The launch has an independent ``finally``: a failure of a role,
  2620	        a refusal of the broker and a cancellation all release the
  2621	        resources of the round before the failure leaves this method.
  2622	        The cancellation itself is not swallowed: it continues to
  2623	        propagate after the cleanup.
  2624	        """
  2625	        if self.state != 'ARMED':
  2626	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STATE_INVALID', self.state)
  2627	        try:
  2628	            for role in self.plan.roles:
  2629	                # The shared budget of the startup is checked before
  2630	                # every role: a role that is created after the deadline
  2631	                # of the round arrived is a role that nobody may stop.
  2632	                self.check_startup_budget('ROLE:' + role['role'])
  2633	                self._launch_role(role)
  2634	        except BaseException:
  2635	            # Any failure of the launch, a cancellation included, ends
  2636	            # the round closed: the job, the pipes and the original
  2637	            # handles of this round are released here.
  2638	            self._abort_round('LAUNCH_ABORTED')
  2639	            raise
  2640	        self.state = 'RUNNING'
  2641	        return self.evidence()
  2642	
  2643	    def _launch_role(self, role):
  2644	        name = role['role']
  2645	        owned = [record['pid'] for record in self.records if record['pid']]
  2646	        command = role_command(role, self.run_root, owned)
  2647	        record = {'role': name,
  2648	                  'executable': command['executable'],
  2649	                  'executable_sha256': role['tools'][0]['sha256'],
  2650	                  'arguments': list(command['arguments']),
  2651	                  'working_directory': command['working_directory'],
  2652	                  'log': role['log'],
  2653	                  'created': False, 'assigned': False, 'resumed': False,
  2654	                  'pid': None, 'creation_time': None,
  2655	                  'terminated': False, 'exited': False, 'exit_code': None,
  2656	                  'process_handle_closed': False, 'thread_handle_closed': False,
  2657	                  'stdout_bytes': 0, 'stderr_bytes': 0,
  2658	                  'stdout_overflow': False, 'stderr_overflow': False,
  2659	                  'failure': None,
  2660	                  'pending_handles': []}
  2661	        self.records.append(record)
  2662	        try:
  2663	            self._prepare_directories(role)
  2664	        except OSError as error:
  2665	            record['failure'] = 'BROKER_ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED'
  2666	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'role_directory', 'role': name,
  2667	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  2668	            self._abort_round('ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED')
  2669	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED', name)
  2670	        try:
  2671	            # The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** every
  2672	            # pipe: a pipe that is created after the deadline of the round
  2673	            # arrived is a pipe that nobody may close.
  2674	            self.check_startup_budget('PIPE:stdout:' + name)
  2675	            stdout_read, stdout_write = self.win32.create_pipe('stdout')
  2676	            # The two ends are handed over to the core in one step: the
  2677	            # façade forgets them and the core registers them, so that
  2678	            # the two tables never name the same handle.
  2679	            self._adopt_from_api([(stdout_read, 'pipe_read'),
  2680	                                  (stdout_write, 'pipe_write')], name)
  2681	            self.check_startup_budget('PIPE:stderr:' + name)
  2682	            stderr_read, stderr_write = self.win32.create_pipe('stderr')
  2683	            self._adopt_from_api([(stderr_read, 'pipe_read'),
  2684	                                  (stderr_write, 'pipe_write')], name)
  2685	            self.check_startup_budget('PIPE_DONE:' + name)
  2686	        except BrokerError as error:
  2687	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2688	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_pipe', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
  2689	            self._abort_round('PIPE_FAILED')
  2690	            raise
  2691	        try:
  2692	            # The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** the
  2693	            # creation: a role that is created after the deadline of the
  2694	            # round arrived would be a role that nobody may stop.
  2695	            self.check_startup_budget('CREATE:' + name)
  2696	            created = self.win32.create_process_suspended(
  2697	                executable=command['executable'], arguments=command['arguments'],
  2698	                working_directory=command['working_directory'],
  2699	                stdout_handle=stdout_write, stderr_handle=stderr_write,
  2700	                environment=command['environment'])
  2701	        except BrokerError as error:
  2702	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2703	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
  2704	                                  'code': error.code})
  2705	            # The façade keeps the original handles of a process that it
  2706	            # could not hand over.  They are named here: the core holds
  2707	            # the façade and the recovery of the façade can reach them.
  2708	            retained = self._api_retained()
  2709	            if retained:
  2710	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process_retained',
  2711	                                      'role': name, 'retained': retained})
  2712	            self._abort_round('CREATE_FAILED')
  2713	            raise
  2714	        # The original handles of the process enter the pending table of
  2715	        # the round in the moment of their creation and the core takes
  2716	        # them right away: it is their owner from now on and it closes
  2717	        # them when it releases the records.
  2718	        #
  2719	        # The **eligibility** of the control handle is a callable over
  2720	        # the ORIGINAL record: the pending pass asks it at the moment
  2721	        # of its close and it reads the current exit fact of the
  2722	        # record.  The release of the records asks the very same
  2723	        # callable, so that the record and the pending table never
  2724	        # disagree about the question "may this handle be closed now?".
  2725	        #
  2726	        # The registration happens **before** the check of the shared
  2727	        # budget that follows the creation and before every other step
  2728	        # that may raise.  A process that was created after the deadline
  2729	        # arrived is only cleaned up and it is never owned by nobody:
  2730	        # the lease of the round may end, the ownership of the handles
  2731	        # may not.
  2732	        eligibility = self._process_closeable(record)
  2733	        self._process_eligibility[id(record)] = eligibility
  2734	        self._pending.add(created.process, 'process', role=name,
  2735	                          closeable=eligibility)
  2736	        self._pending.add(created.thread, 'thread', role=name)
  2737	        self._pending.transfer(created.process, 'CORE')
  2738	        self._pending.transfer(created.thread, 'CORE')
  2739	        record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'process', 'owner': 'CORE'})
  2740	        record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'thread', 'owner': 'CORE'})
  2741	        record['created'] = True
  2742	        record['pid'] = created.pid
  2743	        record['creation_time'] = created.creation_time
  2744	        record['process_handle'] = created.process
  2745	        record['thread_handle'] = created.thread
  2746	        for failure in getattr(created, 'failures', ()):
  2747	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
  2748	                                  'code': failure})
  2749	        try:
  2750	            # The budget is checked again after the creation: a process
  2751	            # that was created after the deadline arrived is only
  2752	            # cleaned up, it is never assigned and it is never resumed.
  2753	            self.check_startup_budget('CREATED:' + name)
  2754	        except BrokerError as error:
  2755	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2756	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
  2757	                                  'code': error.code})
  2758	            # The façade keeps the original handles of a process that it
  2759	            # could not hand over.  They are named here: the core holds
  2760	            # the façade and the recovery of the façade can reach them.
  2761	            retained = self._api_retained()
  2762	            if retained:
  2763	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process_retained',
  2764	                                      'role': name, 'retained': retained})
  2765	            self._abort_round('CREATE_FAILED')
  2766	            raise
  2767	        # The write ends belong to the child now; the broker keeps the read
  2768	        # ends only.  The copies of the broker are closed and they leave
  2769	        # the pending table: they are not pending any more.
  2770	        self._release_pending(stdout_write)
  2771	        self._release_pending(stderr_write)
  2772	        try:
  2773	            # The shared budget is checked before the assignment and
  2774	            # before the resume: a thread that is resumed after the
  2775	            # deadline of the round arrived is a service that runs
  2776	            # outside of the round.
  2777	            self.check_startup_budget('ASSIGN:' + name)
  2778	            self.win32.assign_process_to_job(self.job, created.process)
  2779	        except BrokerError as error:
  2780	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2781	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'assign', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
  2782	            # The process is still suspended: it never ran.  It is stopped
  2783	            # through its own original handle and its exit is confirmed.
  2784	            self._terminate_owned(created.process, record, 'ASSIGN_FAILED')
  2785	            self._abort_round('ASSIGN_FAILED')
  2786	            raise
  2787	        record['assigned'] = True
  2788	        try:
  2789	            self.check_startup_budget('RESUME:' + name)
  2790	            self.win32.resume_thread(created.thread)
  2791	        except BrokerError as error:
  2792	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2793	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'resume', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
  2794	            self._abort_round('RESUME_FAILED')
  2795	            raise
  2796	        record['resumed'] = True
  2797	        try:
  2798	            self.check_startup_budget('READER:' + name)
  2799	            self._start_reader(record, stdout_read, 'stdout')
  2800	            self.check_startup_budget('READER:' + name)
  2801	            self._start_reader(record, stderr_read, 'stderr')
  2802	        except BrokerError as error:
  2803	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2804	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'reader', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
  2805	            self._abort_round('READER_FAILED')
  2806	            raise
  2807	
  2808	    def _release_pending(self, handle):
  2809	        """Release one pending handle that the child took over."""
  2810	        item = self._pending.take(handle)
  2811	        if item is None:
  2812	            return False
  2813	        try:
  2814	            self.win32.close_handle(handle)
  2815	        except BrokerError as error:
  2816	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2817	                                  'role': item['role'], 'code': error.code})
  2818	            self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
  2819	            return False
  2820	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  2821	            # A cancellation never becomes an ordinary evidence: it is
  2822	            # named and it is raised, so that the cleanups that follow
  2823	            # it still run.
  2824	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2825	                                  'role': item['role'],
  2826	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  2827	                                  'cancellation': True})
  2828	            self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
  2829	            raise
  2830	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2831	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2832	                                  'role': item['role'],
  2833	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  2834	            self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
  2835	            return False
  2836	        return True
  2837	
  2838	    def _prepare_directories(self, role):
  2839	        """Create the fresh directories of a role.
  2840	
  2841	        A directory that already exists is refused: a stale log, a stale
  2842	        pid lease or a stale sampler output of an earlier round may never
  2843	        be read as the output of this round.
  2844	
  2845	        The user directory of the ComfyUI role is the directory that the
  2846	        plan closed and not a fixed name: the round creates the
  2847	        directory that the command of the role really uses.
  2848	        """
  2849	        name = role['role']
  2850	        if name == 'COMFY':
  2851	            (Path(self.run_root) / 'temp' / 'service-os').mkdir(parents=True,
  2852	                                                                exist_ok=True)
  2853	            (Path(self.run_root) / role['data']['user_directory']).mkdir(
  2854	                parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  2855	        elif name == 'SAMPLER':
  2856	            (Path(self.run_root) / 'temp' / 'resource-sampler').mkdir(
  2857	                parents=True, exist_ok=False)
  2858	
  2859	    def _start_reader(self, record, handle, stream):
  2860	        """Hand the read end of a pipe over to its reader.
  2861	
  2862	        The reader is the owner of the read end from now on: the
  2863	        handover changes the owner of the handle and nothing else.  The
  2864	        handle stays in the pending table of the round until it is
  2865	        really closed, and the reader closes it when it is stopped.
  2866	
  2867	        The reader **object** is registered as the owner of the handle
  2868	        and not only its name: the pending pass asks the reader whether
  2869	        it is still alive before it closes the read end under it.
  2870	        """
  2871	        reader = _BoundedStreamReader(self.win32, handle, limit=MAX_STREAM_BYTES,
  2872	                                      name=record['role'] + '-' + stream)
  2873	        self.readers.append(reader)
  2874	        self._pending.transfer(handle, 'READER', owner_ref=reader)
  2875	        record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'pipe_read',
  2876	                                          'stream': stream, 'owner': 'READER'})
  2877	        reader.start()
  2878	
  2879	    @staticmethod
  2880	    def _process_closeable(record):
  2881	        """The close eligibility of the control handle of a process.
  2882	
  2883	        The control handle of a process is closed **only** when the exit
  2884	        of that process was really confirmed.  The fact comes from the
  2885	        **record** of the process and it is not a name of a state: a
  2886	        process whose exit was not confirmed may still be alive and its
  2887	        control handle is the only handle that may stop it.
  2888	
  2889	        The eligibility is a **callable** over the original record and
  2890	        never a frozen copy of a fact: the pending pass asks it at the
  2891	        moment of its close and it reads the **current** exit fact of
  2892	        the record.  A pending entry that froze the answer at the
  2893	        moment of the creation of the process would never close the
  2894	        handle of a process whose exit was confirmed later.
  2895	
  2896	        The same eligibility is asked by the release of the records and
  2897	        by the pending pass of the round: the pending pass may not
  2898	        close behind the back of the owner of the handle.
  2899	        """
  2900	        def eligible():
  2901	            return bool(record.get('exited'))
  2902	        return eligible
  2903	
  2904	    @staticmethod
  2905	    def _process_exit_confirmed(record):
  2906	        """Whether the exit of the process of a record was confirmed.
  2907	
  2908	        The fact is read from the **original** record and it is asked at
  2909	        the moment of the decision: a record whose exit was confirmed
  2910	        after the creation of the process is a record whose control
  2911	        handle may be closed.
  2912	        """
  2913	        return bool(record.get('exited'))
  2914	
  2915	    def _process_eligible(self, record):
  2916	        """Whether the control handle of the process of a record may be closed.
  2917	
  2918	        The **very same** callable that the pending table of the round
  2919	        holds is asked: the record and the pending table never disagree
  2920	        about the current exit fact of the process.  A record without
  2921	        an eligibility of its own is asked directly: the fact comes
  2922	        from the record and it is never a name of a state.
  2923	        """
  2924	        eligibility = self._process_eligibility.get(id(record))
  2925	        if eligibility is None:
  2926	            return self._process_exit_confirmed(record)
  2927	        return bool(eligibility())
  2928	
  2929	    def _close_handle(self, kind, handle):
  2930	        """Close one owned handle and name a failure of the close.
  2931	
  2932	        A handle that was closed leaves the pending table of the round
  2933	        and it leaves the retained resources of the round: a handle that
  2934	        is really closed is never named as a resource any more.  A
  2935	        handle that could not be closed stays pending: it is a resource
  2936	        that this round still owns and it is named.
  2937	
  2938	        A **confirmed** close is notified through the **one** notification
  2939	        of the round: the job of the round, the records of the round, the
  2940	        reader that owns the handle and the retained resources of the
  2941	        round learn the very same fact, and every owner of a handle that
  2942	        was really closed knows that it must never close it again.
  2943	
  2944	        A **cancellation** in the middle of a close is never turned into
  2945	        an ordinary evidence: it is named and it is raised, so that the
  2946	        finalizer that follows it still runs and the cancellation
  2947	        continues to propagate after the cleanup.
  2948	        """
  2949	        entry = self._pending.find(handle)
  2950	        try:
  2951	            self.win32.close_handle(handle)
  2952	        except BrokerError as error:
  2953	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
  2954	                                  'code': error.code})
  2955	            return False
  2956	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  2957	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
  2958	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  2959	                                  'cancellation': True})
  2960	            raise
  2961	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2962	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
  2963	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  2964	            return False
  2965	        self._pending.take(handle)
  2966	        # The handle is really closed: every owner of it is notified
  2967	        # through the one notification of the round.
  2968	        self._note_closed_handle(entry if entry is not None
  2969	                                 else {'handle': handle, 'kind': kind})
  2970	        return True
  2971	
  2972	    def _close_readers(self):
  2973	        """Stop the readers of the children and close their read handles.
  2974	
  2975	        The wait of a reader is bounded by the ONE cleanup deadline of the
  2976	        round and its state is recorded.  A reader that is still blocked
  2977	        when the wait is over is a *fact* of this round and it is
  2978	        reported as such: a daemon thread that is still running is not a
  2979	        finished thread, and the read end of such a reader is not closed
  2980	        under it.
  2981	
  2982	        Every reader is independent of the others: a reader that could
  2983	        not be joined never skips the coordination of the readers that
  2984	        follow it, and a reader whose thread never started is never
  2985	        joined at all.  A read end that was closed with a confirmed
  2986	        close is never closed a second time.
  2987	        """
  2988	        for reader in self.readers:
  2989	            try:
  2990	                self._close_one_reader(reader)
  2991	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  2992	                # A cancellation never skips the readers that follow it:
  2993	                # it is named and it is raised after every reader was
  2994	                # coordinated.
  2995	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_readers',
  2996	                                      'name': reader.name,
  2997	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  2998	                                      'cancellation': True})
  2999	                self._readers_cancelled.append(error)
  3000	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3001	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_readers',
  3002	                                      'name': reader.name,
  3003	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  3004	        if self._readers_cancelled:
  3005	            raise self._readers_cancelled[0]
  3006	
  3007	    def _close_one_reader(self, reader):
  3008	        """Coordinate one reader of the round on its own.
  3009	
  3010	        The read end of a reader is closed **only** when the reader
  3011	        really stopped.  Every branch of the coordination asks the
  3012	        reader whether it is still alive: a wait that was cancelled and
  3013	        a join that failed are not a proof that the reader ended, and
  3014	        the read end of a live reader is never closed under it.
  3015	
  3016	        A cancellation in the middle of the coordination never skips the
  3017	        readers that follow it and it never skips the protection of the
  3018	        read end of the reader that is still alive.
  3019	        """
  3020	        if reader.handle is None:
  3021	            # The read end of this reader was closed with a confirmed
  3022	            # close already.
  3023	            return
  3024	        state = {'name': reader.name,
  3025	                 'finished': reader.finished.is_set(),
  3026	                 'joined': False,
  3027	                 'alive': reader.is_alive(),
  3028	                 'error': reader.error}
  3029	        if reader.ident is None and not reader.is_alive():
  3030	            # The thread of this reader never started.  A thread that
  3031	            # never started is never joined: the join of a thread that
