  3032	            # was not started is an error of the caller and not a fact
  3033	            # of the round.  The handle of this reader is closed here,
  3034	            # because no thread of the round reads it.
  3035	            state['started'] = False
  3036	            state['alive'] = False
  3037	            state['finished'] = True
  3038	            self.reader_states.append(state)
  3039	            if self._close_handle('stream', reader.handle):
  3040	                reader.handle = None
  3041	            else:
  3042	                self._retain_resource('pipe_read', reader.handle,
  3043	                                      'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
  3044	            return
  3045	        state['started'] = True
  3046	        cancelled = None
  3047	        try:
  3048	            remaining = self.remaining_cleanup_seconds()
  3049	            wait = min(READER_JOIN_SECONDS, remaining)
  3050	            if wait > 0:
  3051	                reader.finished.wait(wait)
  3052	            # The remaining budget is recomputed before the join: a wait
  3053	            # that already consumed part of the budget never receives the
  3054	            # whole budget again.
  3055	            remaining = self.remaining_cleanup_seconds()
  3056	            reader.join(min(READER_JOIN_SECONDS, remaining))
  3057	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  3058	            # A cancellation never decides the fate of the read end: the
  3059	            # reader is asked again whether it is still alive.
  3060	            cancelled = error
  3061	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3062	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_reader',
  3063	                                  'name': reader.name,
  3064	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  3065	        alive = reader.is_alive()
  3066	        state['finished'] = reader.finished.is_set()
  3067	        state['joined'] = not alive
  3068	        state['alive'] = alive
  3069	        self.reader_states.append(state)
  3070	        if alive:
  3071	            # The reader still holds the read end.  The handle is not
  3072	            # closed under a running reader: it leaves the pending
  3073	            # table with its owner and the round keeps the fact.
  3074	            self._pending.take(reader.handle)
  3075	            self.reader_states[-1]['handle_retained'] = True
  3076	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'reader_not_stopped',
  3077	                                  'name': reader.name,
  3078	                                  'code': 'BROKER_READER_NOT_STOPPED'})
  3079	            if cancelled is not None:
  3080	                raise cancelled
  3081	            return
  3082	        if self._close_handle('stream', reader.handle):
  3083	            reader.handle = None
  3084	        else:
  3085	            self._retain_resource('pipe_read', reader.handle,
  3086	                                  'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
  3087	        if cancelled is not None:
  3088	            raise cancelled
  3089	
  3090	    def _terminate_owned(self, handle, record, reason):
  3091	        """Stop one owned process through its own original handle.
  3092	
  3093	        No pid is used and no name is used: only the handle that this core
  3094	        created is passed to the operating system.  The wait is bounded
  3095	        by the ONE cleanup deadline of the round: an expired deadline
  3096	        never starts a new wait.
  3097	
  3098	        The termination and the **confirmation of the exit** are two
  3099	        independent facts.  A termination that failed is named and it
  3100	        is preserved, and the **bounded wait** of the exit still runs
  3101	        when the original deadline of the round still permits it: a
  3102	        process whose termination failed may have exited anyway, and
  3103	        the exit is the only fact that may release its control handle.
  3104	        A failure of the termination never skips the wait and it never
  3105	        replaces it.
  3106	
  3107	        The control handle of a process is never closed because the
  3108	        termination was **requested**: only a confirmed exit may close
  3109	        it, and an expired cleanup keeps the control and it never opens
  3110	        a new budget of its own.
  3111	        """
  3112	        terminated = False
  3113	        try:
  3114	            self.win32.terminate_process(handle, 1)
  3115	            record['terminated'] = True
  3116	            terminated = True
  3117	        except BrokerError as error:
  3118	            # The original failure of the round is named and it stays
  3119	            # named: the wait of the exit below is independent of it.
  3120	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'terminate', 'reason': reason,
  3121	                                  'code': error.code})
  3122	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
  3123	            # A cancellation is never swallowed: it leaves the method
  3124	            # and the finalizers that follow it still run.
  3125	            raise
  3126	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3127	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'terminate', 'reason': reason,
  3128	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  3129	        timeout = self._wait_timeout_ms()
  3130	        if timeout <= 0:
  3131	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'reason': reason,
  3132	                                  'code': 'BROKER_CLEANUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED'})
  3133	            return terminated
  3134	        try:
  3135	            record['exited'] = bool(self.win32.wait_process(handle, timeout))
  3136	        except BrokerError as error:
  3137	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'reason': reason, 'code': error.code})
  3138	        if record['exited']:
  3139	            try:
  3140	                record['exit_code'] = self.win32.exit_code(handle)
  3141	            except BrokerError as error:
  3142	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'exit_code', 'reason': reason,
  3143	                                      'code': error.code})
  3144	        return terminated
  3145	
  3146	    # -- the stop of the round ---------------------------------------------
  3147	    def stop(self, reason='STOP', *, force_unknown=False, deadline=None):
  3148	        """Stop the round and release every handle that this core holds.
  3149	
  3150	        The stop is bounded and it is idempotent: a repeated stop does not
  3151	        terminate anything twice and it does not change the evidence of
  3152	        the round.  Every handle is reported on its own, so a handle that
  3153	        could not be closed stays visible and the round becomes unknown
  3154	        instead of a silent success.
  3155	
  3156	        The steps of the stop are **independent bounded finalizers**.  The
  3157	        termination of the job, the wait for the original processes, the
  3158	        stop of the readers, the query of the job, the release of the
  3159	        original handles, the release of the job, the release of the
  3160	        pending resources and the diagnosis of the round each run in
  3161	        their own guard: a failure of one of them never skips the others.
  3162	        The first failure of the round is the one that leaves the method
  3163	        and the later failures are named next to it.
  3164	
  3165	        A stop that did not release everything does **not** latch the
  3166	        round as released: the same owner may try the remaining work
  3167	        again, inside the ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  A handle
  3168	        that was closed with a confirmed close is never closed a second
  3169	        time.
  3170	        """
  3171	        if self._released:
  3172	            return self.evidence()
  3173	        if self.state == 'STOPPING' and self._cleanup_deadline_value is not None:
  3174	            # A stop that is already running: the owner of the round does
  3175	            # not start a second one.
  3176	            return self.evidence()
  3177	        self.state = 'STOPPING'
  3178	        if self.stop_reason is None:
  3179	            self.stop_reason = reason
  3180	        # The ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  It is generated here,
  3181	        # at the first stop, and every step below uses this very value.
  3182	        deadline = self.cleanup_deadline(deadline)
  3183	        primary = None
  3184	
  3185	        def step(stage, action):
  3186	            """Run one bounded finalizer of the stop.
  3187	
  3188	            A finalizer that fails never skips the finalizers that follow
  3189	            it.  A cancellation is named as a cancellation and it is
  3190	            never turned into an ordinary evidence: it becomes the
  3191	            primary failure of the round when nothing else failed, and
  3192	            it is raised after every finalizer was executed.
  3193	            """
  3194	            nonlocal primary
  3195	            try:
  3196	                action()
  3197	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the cleanup still runs
  3198	                cancelled = isinstance(error, (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit))
  3199	                if primary is None:
  3200	                    primary = error
  3201	                self.failures.append({'stage': stage,
  3202	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  3203	                                      'cancellation': cancelled})
  3204	
  3205	        step('diagnostic', self._collect_stream_counts)
  3206	        step('terminate_job', self._terminate_the_job)
  3207	        step('wait_processes', self._wait_the_processes)
  3208	        step('close_readers', self._close_readers)
  3209	        step('query_job', self._query_job)
  3210	        step('release_records', self._release_the_records)
  3211	        step('release_job', self._release_the_job)
  3212	        step('pending_resources', self._close_pending)
  3213	
  3214	        released = self._is_released()
  3215	        # The latch of a completed release is only set when the release
  3216	        # was really proven.  An unfinished cleanup never blocks the
  3217	        # recovery of the same owner.
  3218	        self._released = released
  3219	        self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED' if (force_unknown or not released) else 'RELEASED'
  3220	        if primary is not None:
  3221	            # The first failure of the round is not swallowed: the caller
  3222	            # sees it after the round was cleaned up.
  3223	            raise primary
  3224	        return self.evidence()
  3225	
  3226	    def _pending_handle_in_use(self, item):
  3227	        """Whether the owner of a pending handle is still using it.
  3228	
  3229	        The owner of a handle is an **object** and not a name: the
  3230	        reader that owns a read end is asked whether it is still alive.
  3231	        A read end that a live reader still reads from is never closed
  3232	        under it, whatever the branch that reached the pending pass did
  3233	        before.
  3234	        """
  3235	        owner = item.get('owner_ref')
  3236	        if owner is None:
  3237	            return False
  3238	        alive = getattr(owner, 'is_alive', None)
  3239	        if not callable(alive):
  3240	            return False
  3241	        try:
  3242	            return bool(alive())
  3243	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3244	            # A reader that cannot answer is a reader that may still be
  3245	            # alive: the handle is protected.
  3246	            return True
  3247	
  3248	    def _close_pending(self):
  3249	        """Close the handles that are still pending and name the failures.
  3250	
  3251	        The pending pass is the **last** close of the round and it may
  3252	        close a handle that the release of the records or the release of
  3253	        the job could not close.  Every handle that it really closes is
  3254	        reported to the core: the job of the round, the records of the
  3255	        round and the retained resources of the round never name a
  3256	        handle that is closed.
  3257	
  3258	        The pass never closes a handle whose owner is still using it:
  3259	        the guard is the fact of the owner and not the name of an
  3260	        owner.
  3261	        """
  3262	        retained = self._pending.close_all(self.failures,
  3263	                                           on_closed=self._note_closed_handle,
  3264	                                           protect=self._pending_handle_in_use)
  3265	        if retained:
  3266	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'pending_resources',
  3267	                                  'count': len(retained),
  3268	                                  'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_RESOURCES_RETAINED'})
  3269	
  3270	    def _note_closed_handle(self, item):
  3271	        """Learn that one handle was really closed.
  3272	
  3273	        The bookkeeping of the core has to agree with the fact of the
  3274	        close: a handle that is closed may never be named by the job of
  3275	        the round, by the records of the round or by the retained
  3276	        resources of the round.  Only the handle that was really closed
  3277	        is forgotten: a handle that could not be closed keeps its owner
  3278	        and it stays named.
  3279	
  3280	        The **owner object** of the handle is notified as well.  A
  3281	        reader whose read end was closed by the pending pass learns that
  3282	        the handle is gone: a later stop never closes the same original
  3283	        handle a second time and it never closes a handle that the
  3284	        operating system may have reused in the meantime.
  3285	        """
  3286	        handle = item['handle']
  3287	        kind = item.get('kind')
  3288	        if self.job is handle:
  3289	            self.job = None
  3290	            self.job_closed = True
  3291	        for record in self.records:
  3292	            if record.get('process_handle') is handle:
  3293	                record['process_handle_closed'] = True
  3294	                record['process_handle'] = None
  3295	            if record.get('thread_handle') is handle:
  3296	                record['thread_handle_closed'] = True
  3297	                record['thread_handle'] = None
  3298	        owner = item.get('owner_ref')
  3299	        if owner is not None and getattr(owner, 'handle', None) is handle:
  3300	            # The owner of the handle learns that it is closed.  The
  3301	            # handle of the owner is the **original** handle and it is
  3302	            # never closed twice.
  3303	            try:
  3304	                owner.handle = None
  3305	                owner.closed = True
  3306	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3307	                pass
  3308	        self._release_resource(kind, handle)
  3309	
  3310	    def _terminate_the_job(self):
  3311	        """Terminate the job of the round.
  3312	
  3313	        The termination happens once.  A repeated stop does not terminate
  3314	        the job of the round a second time.
  3315	        """
  3316	        if self.job is None or self.job_terminated:
  3317	            return
  3318	        try:
  3319	            self.win32.terminate_job(self.job, 1)
  3320	            self.job_terminated = True
  3321	        except BrokerError as error:
  3322	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'terminate_job', 'code': error.code})
  3323	
  3324	    def _wait_the_processes(self):
  3325	        """Wait for the original processes of the round.
  3326	
  3327	        Every process is independent of the others: a process that could
  3328	        not be waited for does not skip the wait of the next one, and an
  3329	        ordinary failure of the wait of one process does not interrupt
  3330	        the group.  The wait is bounded by the ONE cleanup deadline of
  3331	        the round and the remaining budget is recomputed before every
  3332	        single wait: an expired deadline never starts a new wait.
  3333	        """
  3334	        for record in self.records:
  3335	            try:
  3336	                self._wait_one_process(record)
  3337	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  3338	                # A cancellation never skips the processes that follow it:
  3339	                # it is named and it is raised after every process was
  3340	                # coordinated.
  3341	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
  3342	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  3343	                                      'cancellation': True})
  3344	                self._processes_cancelled.append(error)
  3345	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3346	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
  3347	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  3348	        if self._processes_cancelled:
  3349	            raise self._processes_cancelled[0]
  3350	
  3351	    def _wait_one_process(self, record):
  3352	        """Wait for one original process of the round, on its own."""
  3353	        handle = record.get('process_handle')
  3354	        if handle is None or record['exited']:
  3355	            return
  3356	        if not record['resumed']:
  3357	            # The process never ran: it is stopped through its own
  3358	            # suspended handle.
  3359	            self._terminate_owned(handle, record, 'SUSPENDED_AT_STOP')
  3360	            return
  3361	        timeout = self._wait_timeout_ms()
  3362	        if timeout <= 0:
  3363	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
  3364	                                  'code': 'BROKER_CLEANUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED'})
  3365	            return
  3366	        try:
  3367	            record['exited'] = bool(self.win32.wait_process(handle, timeout))
  3368	        except BrokerError as error:
  3369	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
  3370	                                  'code': error.code})
  3371	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
  3372	            # A cancellation is never turned into an ordinary evidence:
  3373	            # it leaves the wait of this process and it is raised after
  3374	            # every other process was coordinated.
  3375	            raise
  3376	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3377	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'wait', 'role': record['role'],
  3378	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  3379	            return
  3380	        if record['exited']:
  3381	            try:
  3382	                record['exit_code'] = self.win32.exit_code(handle)
  3383	            except BrokerError as error:
  3384	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'exit_code', 'role': record['role'],
  3385	                                      'code': error.code})
  3386	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
  3387	                # The cancellation of the exit code is a cancellation of
  3388	                # the round and it is never swallowed by the evidence.
  3389	                raise
  3390	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3391	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'exit_code', 'role': record['role'],
  3392	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  3393	
  3394	    def _wait_timeout_ms(self):
  3395	        """The milliseconds that are left until the cleanup deadline."""
  3396	        remaining = self.remaining_cleanup_seconds()
  3397	        if remaining <= 0:
  3398	            return 0
  3399	        return max(1, min(WAIT_MILLISECONDS, int(remaining * 1000)))
  3400	
  3401	    def _release_the_records(self):
  3402	        """Close the original handles of the processes of the round.
  3403	
  3404	        A handle that was closed with a confirmed close is forgotten and
  3405	        it is never closed a second time.  A handle that could not be
  3406	        closed keeps its original reference and its owner.
  3407	
  3408	        The **control handle of a process** is closed **only** when the
  3409	        exit of that process was really confirmed.  A process whose exit
  3410	        was not confirmed - a suspended child that was never assigned to
  3411	        the job of the round, a process whose wait timed out or whose
  3412	        wait failed - may still be alive, and its control handle is the
  3413	        only handle that may stop it.  The handle therefore stays with
  3414	        the record of the process and with the pending table of the
  3415	        round, and the same owner may confirm the exit and close it
  3416	        later, inside the same cleanup deadline of the round.
  3417	
  3418	        The thread handle and the pipe handles are independent of the
  3419	        exit of the process: they are closed on their own and they never
  3420	        wait for the process.
  3421	
  3422	        Every handle of every record is closed on its own: a failure of
  3423	        one of them never skips the handles that follow it, and a
  3424	        cancellation is raised after every handle was coordinated.
  3425	        """
  3426	        cancellations = []
  3427	        for record in self.records:
  3428	            for key, kind in (('thread_handle', 'thread'),
  3429	                              ('process_handle', 'process')):
  3430	                handle = record.get(key)
  3431	                if handle is None:
  3432	                    continue
  3433	                if kind == 'process' and not self._process_eligible(record):
  3434	                    # The exit of the process was not confirmed: the
  3435	                    # control handle stays with its owner and the round
  3436	                    # keeps the fact.  The handle is never closed under
  3437	                    # a process that nobody may stop any more.
  3438	                    record[kind + '_handle_closed'] = False
  3439	                    self._retain_resource(
  3440	                        kind, handle, 'BROKER_PROCESS_EXIT_NOT_CONFIRMED')
  3441	                    self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle',
  3442	                                          'kind': kind,
  3443	                                          'role': record.get('role'),
  3444	                                          'code': 'BROKER_PROCESS_EXIT_NOT_CONFIRMED'})
  3445	                    continue
  3446	                try:
  3447	                    closed = self._close_handle(kind, handle)
  3448	                except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  3449	                    # A cancellation never skips the handles that follow
  3450	                    # it: it is named and it is raised after every
  3451	                    # handle was coordinated.
  3452	                    record[kind + '_handle_closed'] = False
  3453	                    self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
  3454	                                          'role': record.get('role'),
  3455	                                          'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  3456	                                          'cancellation': True})
  3457	                    self._retain_resource(kind, handle, 'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
  3458	                    cancellations.append(error)
  3459	                    continue
  3460	                record[kind + '_handle_closed'] = closed
  3461	                if closed:
  3462	                    record[key] = None
  3463	                else:
  3464	                    self._retain_resource(kind, handle, 'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
  3465	        if cancellations:
  3466	            raise cancellations[0]
  3467	
  3468	    def _release_the_job(self):
  3469	        """Close the original handle of the job of the round.
  3470	
  3471	        A job handle that could not be closed is **not** forgotten: the
  3472	        core keeps the original reference and the round stays unknown.
  3473	        """
  3474	        if self.job is None:
  3475	            return
  3476	        handle = self.job
  3477	        closed = self._close_handle('job', handle)
  3478	        self.job_closed = closed
  3479	        if closed:
  3480	            self.job = None
  3481	            self._release_resource('job', handle)
  3482	        else:
  3483	            self._retain_resource('job', handle, 'BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED')
  3484	
  3485	    def _is_released(self):
  3486	        """Whether the round is a proven release.
  3487	
  3488	        A release needs every fact and not only the absence of a failure:
  3489	        every owned process has to have exited, every handle has to have
  3490	        been closed, every reader has to have been joined without a read
  3491	        error and the job has to have been queried and found empty.
  3492	        """
  3493	        if self.failures:
  3494	            return False
  3495	        if not all(record['exited'] for record in self.records if record['created']):
  3496	            return False
  3497	        if not all(record['process_handle_closed'] and record['thread_handle_closed']
  3498	                   for record in self.records):
  3499	            return False
  3500	        if not self.job_closed:
  3501	            return False
  3502	        if not (self.job_active_known and self.job_active == 0):
  3503	            return False
  3504	        if not self.reader_states:
  3505	            return False
  3506	        for state in self.reader_states:
  3507	            if not state['finished'] or state['alive'] or state['error']:
  3508	                return False
  3509	        if self._pending.pending():
  3510	            return False
  3511	        return True
  3512	
  3513	    def _query_job(self):
  3514	        """Ask the job how many processes it still holds.
  3515	
  3516	        The answer is bounded.  An unknown answer and an answer that is
  3517	        over the cap are both *unknown*: neither of them is a zero.
  3518	
  3519	        The query only runs while the job handle is still open.  A
  3520	        handle that was already closed keeps the answer that it produced:
  3521	        a second stop never destroys the proof of the first one.
  3522	        """
  3523	        if self.job is None:
  3524	            return
  3525	        try:
  3526	            identifiers = self.win32.job_process_ids(self.job)
  3527	        except BrokerError as error:
  3528	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'query_job', 'code': error.code})
  3529	            self.job_active_known = False
  3530	            self.job_active = None
  3531	            return
  3532	        if len(identifiers) > MAX_DESCENDANTS:
  3533	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'query_job', 'code': 'BROKER_DESCENDANT_LIMIT'})
  3534	            self.job_active_known = False
  3535	            self.job_active = None
  3536	            return
  3537	        self.job_active_known = True
  3538	        self.job_active = len(identifiers)
  3539	        self.job_process_ids = list(identifiers)
  3540	
  3541	    def _collect_stream_counts(self):
  3542	        total = 0
  3543	        for record in self.records:
  3544	            for stream, key in (('stdout', 'stdout_bytes'), ('stderr', 'stderr_bytes')):
  3545	                for reader in self.readers:
  3546	                    if reader.name.endswith(record['role'] + '-' + stream):
  3547	                        record[key] = reader.observed
  3548	                        record[stream + '_overflow'] = reader.overflow
  3549	                        if reader.overflow and not self._stream_failure_reported:
  3550	                            self._stream_failure_reported = True
  3551	                            self.failures.append({'stage': 'stream_limit',
  3552	                                                  'role': record['role'],
  3553	                                                  'stream': stream,
  3554	                                                  'code': 'BROKER_STREAM_LIMIT_EXCEEDED'})
  3555	        for reader in self.readers:
  3556	            total += reader.kept
  3557	        self.new_text_bytes = total
  3558	        if total > MAX_NEW_TEXT_BYTES and not self._text_failure_reported:
  3559	            self._text_failure_reported = True
  3560	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'new_text_limit',
  3561	                                  'code': 'BROKER_NEW_TEXT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED'})
  3562	
  3563	    def overflowed(self):
  3564	        """Whether a bound of the text of the children was exceeded."""
  3565	        self._collect_stream_counts()
  3566	        if self.new_text_bytes > MAX_NEW_TEXT_BYTES:
  3567	            return True
  3568	        return any(record['stdout_overflow'] or record['stderr_overflow']
  3569	                   for record in self.records)
  3570	
  3571	    def _abort_round(self, reason):
  3572	        """Fail the round closed.
  3573	
  3574	        The stop of the round is bounded and it only touches the handles
  3575	        and the job of this round.  The state of the round is unknown
  3576	        afterwards, whatever the stop itself proved.  The abort is
  3577	        idempotent: a failure that already aborted the round does not
  3578	        abort it a second time and it does not report it twice.
  3579	        """
  3580	        if not self._abort_reported:
  3581	            self._abort_reported = True
  3582	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'round', 'code': reason})
  3583	        try:
  3584	            self.stop(reason, force_unknown=True)
  3585	        except BrokerError as error:
  3586	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'abort', 'code': error.code})
  3587	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3588	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'abort', 'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  3589	        self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
  3590	
  3591	    # -- the evidence ------------------------------------------------------
  3592	    def summary(self):
  3593	        """The bounded summary of the round that fits into one frame."""
  3594	        return {'state': self.state,
  3595	                'roles': [{'role': record['role'], 'pid': record['pid'],
  3596	                           'created': record['created'], 'assigned': record['assigned'],
  3597	                           'resumed': record['resumed'], 'exited': record['exited']}
  3598	                          for record in self.records],
  3599	                'job_active_known': self.job_active_known,
  3600	                'job_active': self.job_active,
  3601	                'failures': len(self.failures)}
  3602	
  3603	    def evidence(self):
  3604	        return {
  3605	            'kind': 'lvw.s3-native-broker-evidence/1',
  3606	            'state': self.state,
  3607	            'run_id': self.plan.run_id,
  3608	            'candidate_sha': self.plan.candidate_sha,
  3609	            'authorization_sha256': self.plan.authorization_sha256,
  3610	            'grant_authority': 'NOT_VERIFIED',
  3611	            'stop_reason': self.stop_reason,
  3612	            'broker': self.broker_identity,
  3613	            'job': {'created': self.job is not None or self.job_closed,
  3614	                    'kill_on_job_close': self.job_kill_on_close,
  3615	                    'breakaway_allowed': False,
  3616	                    'terminated': self.job_terminated,
  3617	                    'active_process_count': self.job_active,
  3618	                    'active_process_count_known': self.job_active_known,
  3619	                    'process_ids': list(self.job_process_ids),
  3620	                    'handle_closed': self.job_closed},
  3621	            'roles': [{key: value for key, value in record.items()
  3622	                       if key not in ('process_handle', 'thread_handle')}
  3623	                      for record in self.records],
  3624	            'new_text_bytes': self.new_text_bytes,
  3625	            'readers': list(self.reader_states),
  3626	            'pending_resources': self._pending.pending_facts(),
  3627	            # The resources that this round could not release and the
  3628	            # original handles that the Windows façade still holds.  They
  3629	            # are named with their original references: a resource that
  3630	            # was not released is never replaced by a copy of a message.
  3631	            'retained_resources': [{'kind': item['kind'], 'owner': item['owner'],
  3632	                                    'code': item['code'],
  3633	                                    'handle_repr': repr(item['handle'])}
  3634	                                   for item in self._retained_resources],
  3635	            'api_retained': self._api_retained(),
  3636	            'cleanup_deadline': self._cleanup_deadline_value,
  3637	            'failures': list(self.failures),
  3638	            # The claims that this package does not make.
  3639	            'native_execution_proven': False,
  3640	            'native_cleanup_proven': False,
  3641	            'gpu_released_proven': False,
  3642	            'port_released_proven': False,
  3643	            'sampler_proven': False,
  3644	            'parent_crash_recovery_proven': False,
  3645	            'windows_stdin_eof_proven': False,
  3646	            'admission_produced': False,
  3647	            'stage_pass_claim': False,
  3648	            'product_gate0': False,
  3649	        }
  3650	
  3651	
  3652	# -- the bounded private pipe ----------------------------------------------
  3653	
  3654	def _require_deadline(deadline, code='BROKER_DEADLINE_INVALID'):
  3655	    """Check one absolute deadline.
  3656	
  3657	    A deadline is a finite number of seconds of the monotonic clock of
  3658	    the host.  A boolean, a string, an infinite value and a value that
  3659	    is not a number at all are refused: a deadline that was understood
  3660	    as something else would silently unbound the round.
  3661	    """
  3662	    if deadline is None:
  3663	        return None
  3664	    if isinstance(deadline, bool) or not isinstance(deadline, (int, float)):
  3665	        raise BrokerError(code)
  3666	    value = float(deadline)
  3667	    if not math.isfinite(value):
  3668	        raise BrokerError(code)
  3669	    return value
  3670	
  3671	
  3672	class _PosixSyscalls:
  3673	    """The POSIX system calls of the private pipe.
  3674	
  3675	    The table is a narrow and explicit interface.  It is the only place
  3676	    where the private channel touches the operating system, and it can
  3677	    be replaced by a synthetic table in the offline validation: the
  3678	    production logic of the channel is then driven by a table that
  3679	    records the calls instead of by the kernel.
  3680	    """
  3681	
  3682	    def __init__(self):
  3683	        self._pollers = {}
  3684	
  3685	    def fileno(self, file_object):
  3686	        return file_object.fileno()
  3687	
  3688	    def set_blocking(self, descriptor, blocking):
  3689	        os.set_blocking(descriptor, blocking)
  3690	
  3691	    def _poller(self, descriptor):
  3692	        poller = self._pollers.get(descriptor)
  3693	        if poller is None:
  3694	            poller = select.poll()
  3695	            poller.register(descriptor,
  3696	                            select.POLLIN | select.POLLOUT
  3697	                            | select.POLLERR | select.POLLHUP)
  3698	            self._pollers[descriptor] = poller
  3699	        return poller
  3700	
  3701	    def poll(self, descriptor, mask, timeout):
  3702	        """Whether the descriptor is ready for the requested operation."""
  3703	        ready = self._poller(descriptor).poll(max(0.0, timeout) * 1000)
  3704	        for number, event in ready:
  3705	            if event & select.POLLNVAL:
  3706	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_DESCRIPTOR_INVALID')
  3707	            if mask == 'read' and event & (select.POLLIN | select.POLLHUP
  3708	                                           | select.POLLERR):
  3709	                return True
  3710	            if mask == 'write' and event & (select.POLLOUT | select.POLLHUP
  3711	                                            | select.POLLERR):
  3712	                return True
  3713	        return False
  3714	
  3715	    def read(self, descriptor, size):
  3716	        return os.read(descriptor, size)
  3717	
  3718	    def write(self, descriptor, data):
  3719	        return os.write(descriptor, data)
  3720	
  3721	    def close(self, file_object):
  3722	        file_object.close()
  3723	
  3724	
  3725	class _PosixPipeEndpoint:
  3726	    """One end of a private pipe on a POSIX host.
  3727	
  3728	    The endpoint owns the original descriptor of the pipe and it closes
  3729	    it itself.  The descriptor is set to non blocking exactly once, by
  3730	    the owner that created it, and the read and the write poll it in
  3731	    slices of at most 50 milliseconds.  Every slice checks the cancel
  3732	    flag and the absolute deadline, so that a peer that never reads can
  3733	    never hold the supervisor of the round.
  3734	
  3735	    The endpoint never changes the blocking mode of a descriptor that it
  3736	    does not own and it never closes a descriptor that it does not own.
  3737	    It never relies on the lock of a buffered object either: the close
  3738	    of a buffered object may wait for a reader that holds its lock, and
  3739	    the close of this endpoint is the close of the owner of the
  3740	    descriptor.
  3741	    """
  3742	
  3743	    def __init__(self, file_object, *, name, syscalls=None, clock=None):
  3744	        self._file = file_object
  3745	        self._name = name
  3746	        self._syscalls = syscalls or _PosixSyscalls()
  3747	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  3748	        self._descriptor = self._syscalls.fileno(file_object)
  3749	        self._non_blocking = False
  3750	        self._closed = False
  3751	        # The absolute deadline that is bound to this endpoint.  It may
  3752	        # only shorten the deadline that is already bound and it is read
  3753	        # again on every slice: a deadline that was bound after a read
  3754	        # started still tightens that read.
  3755	        self._deadline = None
  3756	        self.facts = {'name': name, 'owner': 'POSIX_ENDPOINT',
  3757	                      'descriptor': self._descriptor}
  3758	
  3759	    def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
  3760	        """Bind an absolute deadline to this endpoint.
  3761	
  3762	        The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
  3763	        bound.  It is a property of the endpoint and not a local
  3764	        argument of one call: a call that passed ``None`` before the
  3765	        binding still observes the bound deadline.
  3766	        """
  3767	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  3768	        if value is None:
  3769	            return self._deadline
  3770	        if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
  3771	            return self._deadline
  3772	        self._deadline = value
  3773	        return value
  3774	
  3775	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  3776	        """The deadline that this call really uses."""
  3777	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  3778	        if self._deadline is None:
  3779	            return value
  3780	        if value is None:
  3781	            return self._deadline
  3782	        return min(value, self._deadline)
  3783	
  3784	    def prepare(self):
  3785	        """Set the descriptor to non blocking, once, by its owner."""
  3786	        if self._non_blocking or self._closed:
  3787	            return
  3788	        self._syscalls.set_blocking(self._descriptor, False)
  3789	        self._non_blocking = True
  3790	        self.facts['non_blocking'] = True
  3791	
  3792	    def _check(self, deadline, cancel):
  3793	        if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
  3794	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name)
  3795	        if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
  3796	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
  3797	
  3798	    def read(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  3799	        """Read one block of the pipe, bounded by the deadline."""
  3800	        self.prepare()
  3801	        while True:
  3802	            self._check(self._effective_deadline(deadline), cancel)
  3803	            if not self._syscalls.poll(self._descriptor, 'read',
  3804	                                       POLL_SLICE_SECONDS):
  3805	                continue
  3806	            try:
  3807	                data = self._syscalls.read(self._descriptor, PIPE_READ_BYTES)
  3808	            except BlockingIOError:
  3809	                # The pipe was not ready after all: the slice is repeated.
  3810	                continue
  3811	            except OSError as error:
  3812	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_READ_FAILED',
  3813	                                  '%s:%s' % (self._name, type(error).__name__))
  3814	            if not data:
  3815	                return b''
  3816	            return data
  3817	
  3818	    def write(self, data, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  3819	        """Write the whole block, bounded by the deadline."""
  3820	        self.prepare()
  3821	        view = memoryview(bytes(data))
  3822	        total = 0
  3823	        while total < len(view):
  3824	            self._check(self._effective_deadline(deadline), cancel)
  3825	            if not self._syscalls.poll(self._descriptor, 'write',
  3826	                                       POLL_SLICE_SECONDS):
  3827	                continue
  3828	            try:
  3829	                count = self._syscalls.write(self._descriptor, view[total:])
  3830	            except BlockingIOError:
  3831	                # The pipe was full after all: the slice is repeated.
  3832	                continue
  3833	            except OSError as error:
  3834	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_WRITE_FAILED',
  3835	                                  '%s:%s' % (self._name, type(error).__name__))
  3836	            if count <= 0:
  3837	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_WRITE_FAILED', self._name)
  3838	            total += count
  3839	        return total
  3840	
  3841	    def close(self):
  3842	        """Close the original descriptor of this endpoint, exactly once.
  3843	
  3844	        The endpoint is closed **only** when the close really happened.
  3845	        A close that failed keeps the descriptor and its owner: the
  3846	        endpoint may try it again and it is never marked as closed
  3847	        irreversibly.
  3848	
  3849	        A close of an endpoint that is already closed keeps the fact
  3850	        that it is closed: the second close is idempotent and it never
  3851	        turns a proven close into a missing one.
  3852	        """
  3853	        if self._closed:
  3854	            self.facts['closed'] = True
  3855	            self.facts['already_closed'] = True
  3856	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = 0
  3857	            self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = True
  3858	            return self.facts
  3859	        try:
  3860	            self._syscalls.close(self._file)
  3861	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3862	            self.facts['closed'] = False
  3863	            self.facts['error_type'] = type(error).__name__
  3864	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = 0
  3865	            self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = True
  3866	            return self.facts
  3867	        self._closed = True
  3868	        self.facts['closed'] = True
  3869	        # This host has no worker thread for an end of the pipe: the
  3870	        # blocking calls are made by the reader of the channel itself.
  3871	        # There is therefore no original worker handle that could be
  3872	        # outstanding, and the fact says so.
  3873	        self.facts['retained_workers'] = 0
  3874	        self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = True
  3875	        return self.facts
  3876	
  3877	
  3878	class _WindowsPipeWorker(threading.Thread):
  3879	    """One worker of one end of a private pipe on a Windows host.
  3880	
  3881	    The worker owns one blocking operation at a time.  It takes the
  3882	    request from its queue, it performs the blocking call on the
  3883	    original handle of the pipe and it reports the real completion of
  3884	    the call.  The thread is created once for the endpoint and it is
  3885	    never created for a frame.
  3886	
  3887	    The handle of the thread is the handle of the thread itself and
  3888	    never a self reported identifier: ``CancelSynchronousIo`` needs the
  3889	    original handle of the thread that performs the operation.
  3890	
  3891	    The lifecycle of the worker is **explicit**.  The states are
  3892	    ``IDLE``, ``QUEUED``, ``IN_IO``, ``CANCELLING``, ``DONE`` and
  3893	    ``STOPPED``:
  3894	
  3895	    * ``IDLE``      the worker waits for a request;
  3896	    * ``QUEUED``    a request waits for the worker;
  3897	    * ``IN_IO``     the worker is inside the blocking call;
  3898	    * ``CANCELLING`` a cancellation was asked for;
  3899	    * ``DONE``      the request of the worker completed;
  3900	    * ``STOPPED``   the thread of the worker really ended.
  3901	
  3902	    ``DONE`` is not ``STOPPED``: the completion of a request says
  3903	    nothing about the thread of the worker, and the handle of the
  3904	    thread may only be closed after the thread really ended.
  3905	
  3906	    The worker is the **only** owner of the handle of its own thread:
  3907	    it duplicated that handle and it closes it when its thread really
  3908	    ended.  A worker that did not stop keeps its handle and its owner:
  3909	    the handle is never closed under a live worker and it is never left
  3910	    to the garbage collector.
  3911	    """
  3912	
  3913	    STATES = ('IDLE', 'QUEUED', 'IN_IO', 'CANCELLING', 'DONE', 'STOPPED')
  3914	
  3915	    def __init__(self, api, handle, *, kind, action, name, clock=None,
  3916	                 context=None):
  3917	        super().__init__(daemon=True, name=name)
  3918	        self.api = api
  3919	        self.handle = handle
  3920	        self.kind = kind
  3921	        self.action = action
  3922	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  3923	        self._requests = queue.Queue(maxsize=1)
  3924	        # The event of the in-flight operation.  It is set while the
  3925	        # worker is inside the call and it is cleared again when the
  3926	        # call ended: an event that is never cleared would report a
  3927	        # permanent in-flight state.
  3928	        self.inside = threading.Event()
  3929	        self.done = threading.Event()
  3930	        self.stopped = threading.Event()
  3931	        self.ready = threading.Event()
  3932	        self.result = None
  3933	        self.error = None
  3934	        # The **original** error object of the last request.  The code
  3935	        # of the error is a name; the object is the failure.  It is kept
  3936	        # so that the endpoint may raise the very object that the
  3937	        # primitive raised instead of a copy of its name.
  3938	        self._error_object = None
  3939	        self.thread_handle = None
  3940	        self.thread_handle_released = False
  3941	        self.joined = False
  3942	        self.state = 'IDLE'
  3943	        # The number of the requests that were submitted and the number
  3944	        # of the requests that completed.  The two numbers are the real
  3945	        # completion of a request: the event alone could report the
  3946	        # completion of an older request.
  3947	        self._condition = threading.Condition()
  3948	        self._submitted = 0
  3949	        self._completed = 0
  3950	        # The generations of the requests that were handed over, in the
  3951	        # order in which they were handed over.  The worker takes the
  3952	        # request and its generation together: a worker that would read
  3953	        # the number of the submissions would name the generation of a
  3954	        # later request.
  3955	        self._generations = collections.deque()
  3956	        # The **outcome of every generation**.  The outcome carries the
  3957	        # result, the error, the **original error object**, the source
  3958	        # of the failure and the order of the completion.  A result
  3959	        # that was really published is never replaced by a later one and
  3960	        # a cancellation never rewrites the outcome of a generation.
  3961	        self._outcomes = {}
  3962	        # The **admission guard of every generation**: the cancel flag
  3963	        # and the absolute deadline that the request of the generation
  3964	        # carries.  The guard is the admission of the worker itself: a
  3965	        # request whose cancel was published or whose deadline arrived
  3966	        # **before** the worker entered the operation performs **no**
  3967	        # primitive call at all.  The guard travels with the request
  3968	        # and it is never a deadline of the worker.
  3969	        self._guards = {}
  3970	        # The explicit notification that a **failure** of a generation
  3971	        # was really published.  It is set under the lock of the worker
  3972	        # and it is set **after** the outcome was recorded: an owner
  3973	        # that waits for it knows that the failure of the round is
  3974	        # published and that a later cancellation may not replace it.
  3975	        self.outcome_published = threading.Event()
  3976	        # The order of the events of this worker.  Every completion and
  3977	        # every cancellation takes a number of it, under the lock of the
  3978	        # worker: the number tells whether a failure was published
  3979	        # **before** the cancellation of the round or after it.
  3980	        self._sequence = 0
  3981	        self._cancel_sequence = None
  3982	        # The ONE context of the round.  The worker reads the absolute
  3983	        # deadline and the published cleanup deadline of the round from
  3984	        # it and it never keeps a budget of its own.
  3985	        self._context = context
  3986	        # The flag of the cancellation.  It stops the worker from
  3987	        # accepting a new task before the cancellation is requested.
  3988	        self._cancelled = False
  3989	        # The name of the cancel flag is not ``_stop``: the base class of
  3990	        # the thread uses that name for its own method and a shadow of it
  3991	        # would break the join of the thread.
  3992	        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
  3993	        # The pause of the retry of a cancellation.  It is its **own**
  3994	        # event and it is never set: the retry of a cancellation may not
  3995	        # wait on the stop event of the worker, because the worker set
  3996	        # that event itself and the wait would return at once.
  3997	        self._pause = threading.Event()
  3998	
  3999	    # -- the lifecycle ----------------------------------------------------
  4000	    def run(self):
  4001	        try:
  4002	            self.thread_handle = self.api.duplicate_current_thread_handle()
  4003	        except BrokerError as error:
  4004	            self.error = error.code
  4005	            self.done.set()
  4006	            self.ready.set()
  4007	            self.state = 'DONE'
  4008	            self.stopped.set()
  4009	            return
  4010	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4011	            self.error = type(error).__name__
  4012	            self.done.set()
  4013	            self.ready.set()
  4014	            self.state = 'DONE'
  4015	            self.stopped.set()
  4016	            return
  4017	        self.ready.set()
  4018	        try:
  4019	            while not self._stop_event.is_set():
  4020	                try:
  4021	                    request = self._requests.get(timeout=0.1)
  4022	                except queue.Empty:
  4023	                    continue
  4024	                with self._condition:
  4025	                    generation = (self._generations.popleft()
  4026	                                  if self._generations else self._submitted)
  4027	                self.error = None
  4028	                self.result = None
  4029	                self._error_object = None
  4030	                # The check of the cancellation and the publication of
  4031	                # the intent to enter the operation are **one** atomic
  4032	                # step under the lock of the worker.  A cancellation
  4033	                # that arrives between the check and the operation would
  4034	                # otherwise be answered by a worker that performs the
  4035	                # operation anyway: the cancellation and the operation
  4036	                # would be two uncoordinated facts.
  4037	                #
  4038	                # The **admission guard of the generation** is asked in
  4039	                # the same atomic step: the cancel flag and the
  4040	                # deadline of the request travel with the request and
  4041	                # the worker never enters the operation of a request
  4042	                # whose admission was already refused.
  4043	                with self._condition:
  4044	                    cancelled = self._cancelled
  4045	                    if not cancelled:
  4046	                        guard = self._guards.get(generation)
  4047	                        if guard is not None:
  4048	                            guard_cancel, guard_deadline = guard
  4049	                            # The **current** original context of the
  4050	                            # round is consulted at the moment of the
  4051	                            # decision of the dispatch.  A cancellation
  4052	                            # or an expiry that was published **before**
  4053	                            # this decision refuses the admission and
  4054	                            # the primitive is never called at all: the
  4055	                            # captured request of the call is never the
  4056	                            # authority of the decision.
  4057	                            context = self._context
  4058	                            if context is not None:
  4059	                                if context.cancel.is_set():
  4060	                                    cancelled = True
  4061	                                    self.error = 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED'
  4062	                                    self._error_object = _cancel_source_error(
  4063	                                        BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED',
  4064	                                                    self.kind), context.cancel)
  4065	                                else:
  4066	                                    # The effective minimum is the one
  4067	                                    # of the context: the bound of the
  4068	                                    # call, the **current** absolute
  4069	                                    # deadline of the round and the
  4070	                                    # **published** cleanup deadline.
  4071	                                    current = self._effective_deadline(
  4072	                                        guard_deadline)
  4073	                                    if (current is not None
  4074	                                            and self._clock() >= current):
  4075	                                        cancelled = True
  4076	                                        self.error = 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT'
  4077	                                        self._error_object = BrokerError(
  4078	                                            'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self.kind)
  4079	                            if (not cancelled and guard_cancel is not None
  4080	                                    and guard_cancel.is_set()):
  4081	                                cancelled = True
  4082	                                self.error = 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED'
  4083	                                # The error of the refusal carries the
  4084	                                # owner of the cancel flag, exactly like
  4085	                                # the refusal of the endpoint: a
  4086	                                # cancellation that the close of the
  4087	                                # channel initiated is a named fact of
  4088	                                # that close and it is never the error
  4089	                                # of the reader.
  4090	                                self._error_object = _cancel_source_error(
  4091	                                    BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED',
  4092	                                                self.kind), guard_cancel)
  4093	                            elif (not cancelled and guard_deadline is not None
  4094	                                  and self._clock() >= guard_deadline):
  4095	                                cancelled = True
  4096	                                self.error = 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT'
  4097	                                self._error_object = BrokerError(
  4098	                                    'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self.kind)
  4099	                    if not cancelled:
  4100	                        self.state = 'IN_IO'
  4101	                        self.inside.set()
  4102	                if cancelled:
  4103	                    # The cancellation reached the worker before the
  4104	                    # operation started: the operation is not performed
  4105	                    # at all and the request is answered as finished.
  4106	                    self._complete(generation)
  4107	                    continue
  4108	                try:
  4109	                    self.result = self.action(request)
  4110	                except BrokerError as error:
  4111	                    self.error = error.code
  4112	                    self._error_object = error
  4113	                except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4114	                    self.error = type(error).__name__
  4115	                    self._error_object = error
  4116	                finally:
  4117	                    self.inside.clear()
  4118	                    self._complete(generation)
  4119	        finally:
  4120	            self.state = 'STOPPED'
  4121	            self.stopped.set()
  4122	            # The worker is the only owner of the handle of its own
  4123	            # thread and it closes it here, when its thread really ended.
  4124	            self.release_thread_handle()
  4125	
  4126	    def _complete(self, generation=None):
  4127	        """Report the real completion of the current request.
  4128	
  4129	        The outcome of the generation is **recorded** here, under the
  4130	        lock of the worker: the result, the code of the error, the
  4131	        **original error object** and the order of the completion.  The
  4132	        record is the history of the request and it is never replaced
  4133	        by a later one.
  4134	
  4135	        The order of the completion is the order of the events of the
  4136	        worker: it tells whether a failure was published **before** the
  4137	        cancellation of the round or after it.  A failure that was
  4138	        published before the cancellation is a fault of the round and it
  4139	        stays the first error; a failure that was published after the
  4140	        cancellation is the consequence of the cancellation.
  4141	        """
  4142	        with self._condition:
  4143	            self._completed += 1
  4144	            self._sequence += 1
  4145	            if generation is not None:
  4146	                record = {
  4147	                    'generation': generation,
  4148	                    'result': self.result,
  4149	                    'error': self.error,
  4150	                    'error_object': self._error_object,
  4151	                    'source': 'WORKER',
  4152	                    'sequence': self._sequence,
  4153	                    'cancel_sequence': self._cancel_sequence,
  4154	                }
  4155	                self._outcomes[generation] = record
  4156	                if record['error'] is not None:
  4157	                    # The failure of this generation is published: a
  4158	                    # later cancellation of the round may not replace
  4159	                    # it.
  4160	                    self.outcome_published.set()
  4161	            self._condition.notify_all()
  4162	        self.done.set()
  4163	        self.state = 'DONE'
  4164	
  4165	    def outcome(self, generation):
  4166	        """The recorded outcome of one generation of this worker.
  4167	
  4168	        The outcome is the **original** outcome of the request: the
  4169	        result, the error and the error object of the request that
  4170	        really ran.  It is ``None`` when the request did not complete.
  4171	        """
  4172	        with self._condition:
  4173	            record = self._outcomes.get(generation)
  4174	            return dict(record) if record is not None else None
  4175	
  4176	    def published_before_cancel(self, generation):
  4177	        """Whether the outcome of this generation was published first.
  4178	
  4179	        The comparison of the two orders is the only proof that a
  4180	        failure of the round happened **before** the cancellation of the
  4181	        round.  A cancellation that was requested first may not rewrite
  4182	        the failure of a request that was still running, and a failure
  4183	        that was published first may never be replaced by a
  4184	        cancellation.
  4185	
  4186	        A worker that was never cancelled proves the order by itself:
  4187	        the failure was published before any cancellation of it.
  4188	        """
  4189	        with self._condition:
  4190	            record = self._outcomes.get(generation)
  4191	            if record is None or record.get('error') is None:
  4192	                return False
  4193	            if self._cancel_sequence is None:
  4194	                return True
  4195	            return record['sequence'] < self._cancel_sequence
  4196	
  4197	    def _request_cancellation(self):
  4198	        """Take the order of the cancellation of this worker.
  4199	
  4200	        The order is taken under the lock of the worker, so that the
  4201	        comparison with the order of a completion is a real order and
  4202	        not a guess of the scheduler.
  4203	        """
  4204	        with self._condition:
  4205	            self._cancelled = True
  4206	            if self._cancel_sequence is None:
  4207	                self._sequence += 1
  4208	                self._cancel_sequence = self._sequence
  4209	            self.state = 'CANCELLING'
  4210	            self._condition.notify_all()
  4211	        self._stop_event.set()
  4212	
  4213	    def wait_ready(self, seconds):
  4214	        """Wait for the worker to have acquired the handle of its thread."""
  4215	        return self.ready.wait(max(0.0, seconds))
  4216	
  4217	    def submit(self, request, cancel=None, deadline=None):
  4218	        """Hand one request to the worker.
  4219	
  4220	        The hand over is bounded: the queue of the worker holds one
  4221	        request and a request that does not fit is refused with a named
  4222	        error.  A worker that is dead and a worker whose thread handle
  4223	        could not be created refuse the request as well.
  4224	
  4225	        The hand over and the cancellation share **one** lock: a
  4226	        request that is submitted after the cancellation of the worker
  4227	        is refused and it never enters the queue.  The cancellation and
  4228	        the submission are therefore coordinated by the same generation
  4229	        of the worker and a request of an older generation is never
  4230	        taken for the completion of this one.
  4231	
  4232	        The **admission guard** of the request - the cancel flag and the
  4233	        absolute deadline of the call - travels with the request: the
  4234	        worker asks it before it enters the operation and a request
  4235	        whose admission was refused performs **no** primitive call.
  4236	
  4237	        The completion of a request is the number of the completed
  4238	        requests and not the event alone: the completion of an older
  4239	        request is never the completion of this one.
  4240	        """
  4241	        if not self.is_alive():
  4242	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_WORKER_NOT_ALIVE', self.kind)
  4243	        if self.thread_handle is None:
  4244	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_WORKER_NOT_READY',
  4245	                               '%s:%s' % (self.kind, self.error))
  4246	        with self._condition:
  4247	            if self._cancelled:
  4248	                # The cancellation of this worker arrived first: the
  4249	                # request is refused and it never enters the queue.
  4250	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_WORKER_CANCELLED', self.kind)
  4251	            self._submitted += 1
  4252	            generation = self._submitted
  4253	            # The generation travels with the request: the worker takes
  4254	            # the request and the generation of **that** request.  The
  4255	            # generation is registered **before** the request is handed
  4256	            # over: a worker that takes the request never finds an
  4257	            # empty list of generations.
  4258	            self._generations.append(generation)
  4259	            self._guards[generation] = (cancel, deadline)
  4260	            try:
  4261	                self._requests.put(request, timeout=POLL_SLICE_SECONDS)
  4262	            except queue.Full:
  4263	                self._submitted -= 1
  4264	                self._guards.pop(generation, None)
  4265	                if self._generations and self._generations[-1] == generation:
  4266	                    self._generations.pop()
  4267	                self._condition.notify_all()
  4268	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_WORKER_QUEUE_FULL', self.kind)
  4269	            self.done.clear()
  4270	            self.state = 'QUEUED'
  4271	            return generation
  4272	
  4273	    def wait_done(self, generation, timeout):
  4274	        """Wait for the completion of one request, bounded."""
  4275	        with self._condition:
  4276	            return self._condition.wait_for(
  4277	                lambda: self._completed >= generation, max(0.0, timeout))
  4278	
  4279	    def request_cancel(self, deadline=None):
  4280	        """Ask for the cancellation of the operation of this worker.
  4281	
  4282	        The request is not a completion.  The caller has to wait for the
  4283	        real completion of the worker and to join it.
  4284	
  4285	        The cancellation stops the worker from accepting a new task
  4286	        **first** and it drives the worker to its end: the worker that
  4287	        is inside an operation is asked to leave it and the worker that
  4288	        waits for a request is asked to stop waiting.  A cancellation
  4289	        that reports ``NOT_FOUND`` is repeated, bounded by the shared
  4290	        deadline, until the worker reports that **this** generation is
  4291	        done or that the thread really stopped: a cancellation that was
  4292	        sent once and dropped is not a cancellation.
  4293	
  4294	        The retry never waits on the stop event of the worker: the
  4295	        worker set that event itself, a moment before, and a wait that
  4296	        returns at once would end the retry after a single
  4297	        ``NOT_FOUND``.  The retry waits on its own pause instead, so
  4298	        that the request of the operating system is really repeated.
  4299	
  4300	        The completion of an **older** generation never releases the
  4301	        retry: the real completion of the request of this generation is
  4302	        the number of the completed requests.
  4303	
  4304	        The deadline of the retry is **recomputed on every round** of
  4305	        the loop: a deadline that was published after the retry started
  4306	        still shortens it, and the pause of the retry is cropped to the
  4307	        time that is really left.  A retry that used the number of the
  4308	        deadline that it found when it started would wait longer than
  4309	        the round allows.
  4310	        """
  4311	        self._request_cancellation()
  4312	        if self.thread_handle is None:
  4313	            return 'NO_HANDLE'
  4314	        # The bound of the retry is fixed **once**: a retry that used a
  4315	        # deadline that moved with every round would never expire.
  4316	        bound = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  4317	        if bound is None:
  4318	            # The retry of a cancellation is **always** bounded: a
  4319	            # worker that never ends may not hold the caller for ever.
  4320	            # The bound of the package is used only when neither the
  4321	            # caller nor the context of the round brought a deadline.
  4322	            bound = self._clock() + CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS
  4323	        while True:
  4324	            try:
  4325	                state = self.api.cancel_synchronous_io(self.thread_handle)
  4326	            except BrokerError as error:
  4327	                self.error = error.code
  4328	                return 'FAILED'
  4329	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4330	                self.error = type(error).__name__
  4331	                return 'FAILED'
  4332	            if state != 'NOT_FOUND':
  4333	                return state
  4334	            # The operation was not found: it had not started yet or it
  4335	            # had just finished.  The worker is asked again until the
  4336	            # request of this generation completed or until the thread
  4337	            # of the worker really stopped.
  4338	            with self._condition:
  4339	                completed = self._completed >= self._submitted
  4340	            if completed or self.stopped.is_set():
  4341	                return 'NOT_FOUND'
  4342	            # The deadline of this round of the retry is read **again**
  4343	            # from the call and from the context of the round: a
  4344	            # deadline that was published after the retry started still
  4345	            # shortens the retry that is running.
  4346	            effective = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  4347	            if effective is None or bound < effective:
  4348	                effective = bound
  4349	            if self._clock() >= effective:
  4350	                return 'NOT_FOUND'
  4351	            # The pause of the retry is its own event: the stop event of
  4352	            # the worker was set by this very method and it may never be
  4353	            # used as the sleep of the retry.  The pause is cropped to
  4354	            # the time that is really left.
  4355	            pause = POLL_SLICE_SECONDS
  4356	            if effective is not None:
  4357	                pause = max(0.0, min(pause, effective - self._clock()))
  4358	            if pause <= 0:
  4359	                return 'NOT_FOUND'
  4360	            self._pause.wait(pause)
  4361	
  4362	    def _effective_deadline(self, call=None):
  4363	        """The ONE deadline that a wait of this worker really uses.
  4364	
  4365	        The deadline of the call, the absolute deadline of the round and
  4366	        the **published** cleanup deadline of the round are one value:
  4367	        the earliest of them.  The value is recomputed on every call.
  4368	        """
  4369	        value = _require_deadline(call)
  4370	        context = self._context
  4371	        if context is not None:
  4372	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
  4373	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
  4374	                value = absolute
  4375	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  4376	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
  4377	                value = cleanup
  4378	        return value
  4379	
  4380	    def stop(self):
  4381	        """Ask the worker to end its loop."""
  4382	        self._stop_event.set()
  4383	
  4384	    def stop_accepting(self):
  4385	        """Stop the worker from accepting a new task.
  4386	
  4387	        The flag is written under the **same** lock that the worker of
  4388	        the endpoint reads it under: the cancellation and the intent to
  4389	        enter the operation are one atomic step.
  4390	
  4391	        The **order** of the cancellation is taken here as well: a
  4392	        failure that was published before this call is a fault of the
  4393	        round and a cancellation may never replace it.
  4394	        """
  4395	        self._request_cancellation()
  4396	
  4397	    def release_thread_handle(self):
  4398	        """Close the handle of the thread of this worker.
  4399	
  4400	        The worker is the only owner of that handle.  It is closed here
  4401	        and only here, and only after the thread of the worker really
  4402	        ended.  A worker that did not stop keeps its handle.
  4403	        """
  4404	        if self.thread_handle is None:
  4405	            return True
  4406	        handle = self.thread_handle
  4407	        try:
  4408	            self.api.close_handle(handle)
  4409	        except BrokerError as error:
  4410	            self.error = error.code
  4411	            return False
  4412	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4413	            self.error = type(error).__name__
  4414	            return False
  4415	        self.thread_handle = None
  4416	        self.thread_handle_released = True
  4417	        return True
  4418	
  4419	    def join_bounded(self, seconds):
  4420	        """Join the worker and report whether it really stopped.
  4421	
  4422	        A worker whose thread was never started cannot be joined: the
  4423	        join of such a thread is an error of the caller and not a fact
  4424	        of the round.  The join reports that the worker did not stop
  4425	        and it never pretends that the thread ended.
  4426	        """
  4427	        try:
  4428	            self.join(max(0.0, seconds))
  4429	        except RuntimeError:
  4430	            # The thread was never started: it is not a thread of the
  4431	            # round that ended, it is a thread that never came up.
  4432	            self.joined = False
  4433	            return False
  4434	        self.joined = not self.is_alive()
  4435	        return self.joined
  4436	
  4437	
  4438	class _WindowsPipeEndpoint:
  4439	    """One end of a private pipe on a Windows host.
  4440	
  4441	    The read and the write of the endpoint each own exactly one worker
  4442	    thread and there is never more than one of them: the number of the
  4443	    threads of a channel is fixed and it never grows with the number of
  4444	    the frames.
  4445	
  4446	    The supervisor never performs the blocking call itself.  It waits
  4447	    for the completion of the worker in bounded slices, it checks the
  4448	    cancel flag and the deadline on every slice and it cancels the
  4449	    worker through the original handle of its thread when the deadline
  4450	    arrives.  The worker that is inside the call is the one that may
  4451	    block; the supervisor never does.
  4452	
  4453	    The cancellation of a synchronous operation is a request and not a
  4454	    completion: ``CancelSynchronousIo`` may report that the operation
  4455	    was not found because it had not started yet or because it had just
  4456	    finished.  The worker therefore reports the real state of its
  4457	    operation, the supervisor joins it with a bounded wait, and a worker
  4458	    that did not stop keeps its owner reference and makes the round
  4459	    unknown.
  4460	
  4461	    A write is a **complete** write: the endpoint writes the whole
  4462	    block, in as many partial writes as Windows needs, and it reports
  4463	    the number of the bytes that really went into the pipe.  A partial
  4464	    write is never reported as a success.
  4465	    """
  4466	
  4467	    #: The bound absolute deadline of this endpoint.  It is a class
  4468	    #: attribute so that an endpoint that was built without its
  4469	    #: constructor still has a real deadline and never a missing one.
  4470	    _deadline = None
  4471	    #: The cancel flag and the deadline of the write that is in flight.
  4472	    #: They are class attributes for the same reason as the deadline
  4473	    #: above: an endpoint that was built without its constructor still
  4474	    #: has them and never a missing one.
  4475	    _write_cancel = None
  4476	    _write_deadline = None
  4477	    #: The context of the round.  It is a class attribute for the same
  4478	    #: reason: an endpoint that was built without its constructor still
  4479	    #: reads the absolute deadline of the round from it and it never
  4480	    #: treats a missing context as an unlimited budget.
  4481	    _context = None
  4482	    #: The lock of the **admission** of the endpoint.  It is a class
  4483	    #: attribute for the same reason: an endpoint that was built without
  4484	    #: its constructor still has a **real** admission and it never has a
  4485	    #: permissive one.  The lock is created **per instance** on its
  4486	    #: first use and it is never shared between two endpoints.
  4487	    _admission_lock = None
  4488	
  4489	    def __init__(self, file_object, *, name, api=None, clock=None,
  4490	                 handle=None, context=None):
  4491	        self._file = file_object
  4492	        self._name = name
  4493	        self._api = api
  4494	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  4495	        self._closed = False
  4496	        self._reader = None
  4497	        self._writer = None
  4498	        self._retained = []
  4499	        self._deadline = None
  4500	        self._write_cancel = None
  4501	        self._write_deadline = None
  4502	        # The lock of the **admission** of the endpoint.  The check of
