  4503	        # the cancel flag, of the deadline of the round and the entry of
  4504	        # the request into the worker are one atomic step under it.
  4505	        self._admission_lock = threading.RLock()
  4506	        # The ONE context of the round.  The endpoint reads the absolute
  4507	        # deadline of the round from it and it never keeps a budget of
  4508	        # its own.
  4509	        self._context = context
  4510	        if handle is None:
  4511	            import msvcrt
  4512	            handle = msvcrt.get_osfhandle(file_object.fileno())
  4513	        self._handle = handle
  4514	        self.facts = {'name': name, 'owner': 'WINDOWS_ENDPOINT'}
  4515	
  4516	    def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
  4517	        """Bind an absolute deadline to this endpoint.
  4518	
  4519	        The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
  4520	        bound and it is read again on every slice.
  4521	        """
  4522	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  4523	        if value is None:
  4524	            return self._deadline
  4525	        if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
  4526	            return self._deadline
  4527	        self._deadline = value
  4528	        return value
  4529	
  4530	    def bind_context(self, context):
  4531	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to this endpoint."""
  4532	        if context is not None:
  4533	            self._context = context
  4534	        return self._context
  4535	
  4536	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  4537	        """The deadline that this call really uses.
  4538	
  4539	        The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
  4540	        endpoint, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
  4541	        cleanup deadline of the round are **one** value: the earliest of
  4542	        the four.  The endpoint never keeps a deadline of its own and it
  4543	        never treats a missing context as an unlimited budget.
  4544	
  4545	        The published cleanup deadline is part of it: a round that
  4546	        entered its cleanup publishes one deadline and the endpoint
  4547	        that reads the context afterwards is bound by it.  An endpoint
  4548	        that still waited with the long lease of the round would
  4549	        outlive the only cleanup budget of the round.
  4550	        """
  4551	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  4552	        bound = self._deadline
  4553	        if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
  4554	            value = bound
  4555	        context = self._context
  4556	        if context is not None:
  4557	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
  4558	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
  4559	                value = absolute
  4560	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  4561	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
  4562	                value = cleanup
  4563	        return value
  4564	
  4565	    def _admission_lock_for(self):
  4566	        """The lock of the admission of **this** endpoint.
  4567	
  4568	        The lock is created **per instance** and it is created once.
  4569	        An endpoint that was built without its constructor - a fixture
  4570	        of the offline validation - therefore still has a **real**
  4571	        admission: the check of the cancel flag and of the deadline is
  4572	        never skipped and it is never permissive.  The creation of the
  4573	        lock is guarded by one lock of the module, so that two threads
  4574	        that arrive at the same time never build two locks for the same
  4575	        endpoint.
  4576	        """
  4577	        lock = self.__dict__.get('_admission_lock')
  4578	        if lock is not None:
  4579	            return lock
  4580	        with _ADMISSION_LOCK_INIT:
  4581	            lock = self.__dict__.get('_admission_lock')
  4582	            if lock is None:
  4583	                lock = threading.RLock()
  4584	                self.__dict__['_admission_lock'] = lock
  4585	        return lock
  4586	
  4587	    def _admission(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  4588	        """The admission of one generation of this endpoint.
  4589	
  4590	        The admission is asked **before** every side effect: before the
  4591	        worker of the endpoint is started, before a request is
  4592	        submitted and before the primitive is called.  A call that
  4593	        entered when the round was already expired or already cancelled
  4594	        is refused there: it never starts a worker, it never submits a
  4595	        request and it never calls the primitive.
  4596	
  4597	        The check and the admission of the generation are **one atomic
  4598	        step** under the lock of the endpoint: a cancellation that
  4599	        arrives between the check and the submit may not be answered by
  4600	        a request that was submitted anyway.
  4601	        """
  4602	        with self._admission_lock_for():
  4603	            return self._admission_locked(deadline, cancel)
  4604	
  4605	    def _admission_locked(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  4606	        """The admission of one generation, under the lock of the endpoint.
  4607	
  4608	        The caller holds the lock of the admission: the check of the
  4609	        cancel flag, of the deadline of the round and of the context of
  4610	        the round and the decision of the admission are **one** atomic
  4611	        step.  The **final** admission of a generation is asked here,
  4612	        immediately before the submission of the request: a
  4613	        cancellation or an expiry that was published **before** that
  4614	        admission causes **zero** primitive calls.
  4615	        """
  4616	        effective = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  4617	        if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
  4618	            # The cancellation of the round arrived first: the
  4619	            # request is refused and no side effect happens.
  4620	            raise _cancel_source_error(
  4621	                BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name), cancel)
  4622	        context = self._context
  4623	        if context is not None and context.cancel.is_set():
  4624	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name)
  4625	        if effective is not None and self._clock() >= effective:
  4626	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
  4627	        return effective
  4628	
  4629	    def _remaining(self, deadline=None):
  4630	        """The seconds that are left until the deadline of this endpoint."""
  4631	        value = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  4632	        if value is None:
  4633	            return CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS
  4634	        return max(0.0, value - self._clock())
  4635	
  4636	    def _wait_slice(self, effective=None):
  4637	        """The seconds that the next wait of this endpoint may ask for.
  4638	
  4639	        The wait is the ordinary poll slice of the package, cropped to
  4640	        the time that is really left until the **current** effective
  4641	        deadline of the round.  A wait never asks for more than the
  4642	        round allows and it never asks for a positive floor beyond it:
  4643	        an expired round is refused before the wait and a round that is
  4644	        about to expire waits only for the time that is left.
  4645	        """
  4646	        value = POLL_SLICE_SECONDS
  4647	        if effective is None:
  4648	            effective = self._effective_deadline()
  4649	        if effective is not None:
  4650	            value = max(0.0, min(value, effective - self._clock()))
  4651	        return value
  4652	
  4653	    def _start(self, kind, action, deadline=None):
  4654	        """Create and start one worker of this endpoint.
  4655	
  4656	        The worker is registered with the endpoint **before** it is
  4657	        started.  An endpoint that owns a worker only after the start
  4658	        returned has no owner at all for a worker whose start did not
  4659	        return, and the close of the endpoint could close the file
  4660	        under a worker that is about to read from it.
  4661	
  4662	        The wait for the handle of the thread of the worker is bounded
  4663	        by the **current** remaining budget of the round.  It never
  4664	        opens a budget of its own and it never waits a fixed number of
  4665	        seconds.
  4666	        """
  4667	        worker = _WindowsPipeWorker(self._api, self._handle, kind=kind,
  4668	                                    action=action, clock=self._clock,
  4669	                                    context=self._context,
  4670	                                    name='lvw-native-custody-pipe-' + kind)
  4671	        # The pre-registration: the endpoint is the owner of the worker
  4672	        # before the thread of the worker exists.
  4673	        if kind == 'read':
  4674	            self._reader = worker
  4675	        else:
  4676	            self._writer = worker
  4677	        try:
  4678	            worker.start()
  4679	        except BaseException:
  4680	            # The start of the worker did not return.  The worker keeps
  4681	            # its owner and it may still come up: it is never forgotten
  4682	            # and it is never closed under.
  4683	            self._retained.append(worker)
  4684	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
  4685	            self.facts['worker_alive'] = True
  4686	            raise
  4687	        # The worker acquires the handle of its own thread before it may
  4688	        # be used.  The wait for it is bounded by the remaining budget of
  4689	        # the round.
  4690	        worker.wait_ready(self._remaining(deadline))
  4691	        return worker
  4692	
  4693	    def _await(self, worker, request, deadline, cancel):
  4694	        """Wait for the completion of one worker, bounded and cancellable.
  4695	
  4696	        The **final** admission of the generation happens here: it is
  4697	        asked after the worker is ready and it is asked **immediately
  4698	        before** the submission of the request, under the lock of the
  4699	        admission.  That is the **linearization point** of the request:
  4700	        a cancellation or an expiry that was published **before** it
  4701	        causes **zero** primitive calls and a cancellation that arrives
  4702	        **after** it follows the bounded cancellation below.  No claim
  4703	        is made that an operating system call that already began may be
  4704	        undone atomically.
  4705	
  4706	        The cancel flag and the deadline of the call travel with the
  4707	        request: the worker asks them itself before it enters the
  4708	        operation and a request whose admission was refused never calls
  4709	        the primitive.
  4710	
  4711	        The deadline that this wait really uses is **recomputed on every
  4712	        slice**: a deadline that was bound after the wait started still
  4713	        tightens the wait that is running.  A wait that carries no
  4714	        deadline yet observes the deadline as soon as it is bound, and
  4715	        the cancel flag ends it in the meantime.
  4716	
  4717	        The **result of the generation** wins over the cancellation and
  4718	        over a new timeout.  A fault that the worker really published
  4719	        before the cancellation of the round is the first error of the
  4720	        round: the cancellation of a later close may not replace it and
  4721	        it may not turn it into a benign cancellation.  The order of the
  4722	        two facts is the order of the events of the worker and it is
  4723	        never a guess of the scheduler.
  4724	        """
  4725	        with self._admission_lock_for():
  4726	            # The final admission of the generation and the submission
  4727	            # of the request are **one** atomic step.
  4728	            effective = self._admission_locked(deadline, cancel)
  4729	            generation = worker.submit(request, cancel=cancel,
  4730	                                       deadline=effective)
  4731	        while True:
  4732	            effective = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  4733	            if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
  4734	                self._raise_cancellation(worker, generation, effective, cancel)
  4735	            context = self._context
  4736	            if context is not None and context.cancel.is_set():
  4737	                # The **original** context of the round is noticed here
  4738	                # as well: the flag of the call and the flag of the
  4739	                # channel are not the only latches of the round.
  4740	                self._raise_cancellation(worker, generation, effective,
  4741	                                        context.cancel)
  4742	            if effective is not None and self._clock() >= effective:
  4743	                # Before a **new** timeout is decided, the published
  4744	                # original error of the **same** generation is
  4745	                # inspected: it remains the primary error of the round
  4746	                # and the timeout is a **parallel** fact.
  4747	                outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4748	                if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4749	                    raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome)
  4750	                try:
  4751	                    self._cancel(worker, effective)
  4752	                except BrokerError as error:
  4753	                    # The coordination failed: the published original
  4754	                    # error of the generation is inspected **again**
  4755	                    # before the failure of the coordination is
  4756	                    # decided, and the failure of the coordination
  4757	                    # stays a parallel fact.
  4758	                    outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4759	                    if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4760	                        raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome, error)
  4761	                    raise
  4762	                # Again after the coordination: the outcome of the
  4763	                # generation is immutable and it is never rewritten by
  4764	                # the timeout.
  4765	                outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4766	                if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4767	                    raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome)
  4768	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
  4769	            if worker.wait_done(generation, self._wait_slice(effective)):
  4770	                break
  4771	        outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4772	        if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4773	            raise self._outcome_error(outcome)
  4774	        if worker.error:
  4775	            raise BrokerError(worker.error, self._name)
  4776	        return worker.result
  4777	
  4778	    @staticmethod
  4779	    def _parallel_timeout(outcome, parallel=None):
  4780	        """The published failure of a generation with the timeout beside it.
  4781	
  4782	        The **original** error object is the error of the round: a
  4783	        timeout that arrives after the failure was really published is
  4784	        a **parallel** fact and it never replaces the failure.
  4785	        """
  4786	        error = _WindowsPipeEndpoint._outcome_error(outcome)
  4787	        try:
  4788	            error.lvw_parallel_timeout = {
  4789	                'code': 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT',
  4790	                'generation': outcome.get('generation'),
  4791	                'sequence': outcome.get('sequence'),
  4792	                'cancel_sequence': outcome.get('cancel_sequence'),
  4793	                'coordination_error': (type(parallel).__name__
  4794	                                       if parallel is not None else None),
  4795	            }
  4796	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4797	            pass
  4798	        return error
  4799	
  4800	    @staticmethod
  4801	    def _outcome_error(outcome):
  4802	        """The error of one published outcome of a worker.
  4803	
  4804	        The **original** error object is raised when the worker kept
  4805	        it: the object is the failure and the code is only its name.  A
  4806	        worker that kept only a code raises the stable code of the
  4807	        broker.
  4808	        """
  4809	        original = outcome.get('error_object')
  4810	        if original is not None:
  4811	            return original
  4812	        return BrokerError(outcome.get('error'), 'WORKER_OUTCOME')
  4813	
  4814	    def _raise_cancellation(self, worker, generation, effective, cancel):
  4815	        """Raise the cancellation of this generation of the worker.
  4816	
  4817	        The outcome of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
  4818	        cancelled and it is asked again after the worker really stopped.
  4819	        A failure of this generation is the **first error** of the
  4820	        round: a cancellation that arrives afterwards may never replace
  4821	        it and it may never turn it into a benign cancellation.  The
  4822	        cancellation stays a **parallel** fact and it is named next to
  4823	        the failure.
  4824	
  4825	        A **failed** coordination - a cancellation that could not be
  4826	        confirmed or a join that did not end - is a parallel fact as
  4827	        well: it never replaces the published failure of the
  4828	        generation and it never hides it.
  4829	
  4830	        The error of the cancellation carries the owner of the flag: the
  4831	        origin of the cancellation is named at the place where the error
  4832	        is really raised and never by a caller of it.
  4833	        """
  4834	        outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4835	        if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4836	            raise self._parallel_fault(outcome, cancel)
  4837	        coordination_error = None
  4838	        try:
  4839	            self._cancel(worker, effective)
  4840	        except BrokerError as error:
  4841	            coordination_error = error
  4842	        outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4843	        if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4844	            raise self._parallel_fault(outcome, cancel, coordination_error)
  4845	        if coordination_error is not None:
  4846	            raise coordination_error
  4847	        raise _cancel_source_error(
  4848	            BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name), cancel)
  4849	
  4850	    @staticmethod
  4851	    def _parallel_fault(outcome, cancel, coordination_error=None):
  4852	        """The failure of a generation with the cancellation beside it.
  4853	
  4854	        The failure is the error of the round.  The cancellation that
  4855	        was requested at the same moment is a **parallel** fact: it is
  4856	        named next to the failure and it never replaces it.  A
  4857	        coordination that failed is a parallel fact as well.
  4858	        """
  4859	        error = _WindowsPipeEndpoint._outcome_error(outcome)
  4860	        if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
  4861	            source = None
  4862	            owner = getattr(cancel, 'owner', None)
  4863	            if callable(owner):
  4864	                try:
  4865	                    source = getattr(owner(), 'owner', None)
  4866	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4867	                    source = None
  4868	            try:
  4869	                error.lvw_parallel_cancellation = {
  4870	                    'code': 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED',
  4871	                    'source': source,
  4872	                    'generation': outcome.get('generation'),
  4873	                    'sequence': outcome.get('sequence'),
  4874	                    'cancel_sequence': outcome.get('cancel_sequence'),
  4875	                    'coordination_error': (type(coordination_error).__name__
  4876	                                           if coordination_error is not None
  4877	                                           else None),
  4878	                }
  4879	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4880	                pass
  4881	        return error
  4882	
  4883	    def _cancel(self, worker, deadline=None):
  4884	        """Cancel one worker and prove that it really stopped."""
  4885	        state = worker.request_cancel(self._effective_deadline(deadline))
  4886	        if not worker.join_bounded(self._remaining(deadline)):
  4887	            # The worker did not stop: it keeps its owner reference and
  4888	            # the round becomes unknown.  It is never terminated blindly
  4889	            # and it is never waited for without a bound.
  4890	            self._retained.append(worker)
  4891	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
  4892	            self.facts['worker_alive'] = True
  4893	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_NOT_CONFIRMED',
  4894	                              '%s:%s' % (self._name, state))
  4895	        if not worker.joined:
  4896	            self._retained.append(worker)
  4897	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
  4898	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_NOT_CONFIRMED', self._name)
  4899	
  4900	    def read(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  4901	        """Read one block of the pipe, bounded by the deadline.
  4902	
  4903	        The admission of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
  4904	        started and before the request is submitted: a read that enters
  4905	        when the round is already expired or already cancelled performs
  4906	        **no** primitive call at all.
  4907	        """
  4908	        effective = self._admission(deadline, cancel)
  4909	        if self._reader is None:
  4910	            self._start('read', self._read_block, effective)
  4911	        return self._await(self._reader, None, deadline, cancel)
  4912	
  4913	    def write(self, data, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  4914	        """Write the whole block, bounded by the deadline.
  4915	
  4916	        The admission of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
  4917	        started and before the request is submitted: a write that enters
  4918	        when the round is already expired or already cancelled performs
  4919	        **no** primitive call at all.
  4920	        """
  4921	        effective = self._admission(deadline, cancel)
  4922	        if self._writer is None:
  4923	            self._start('write', self._write_block, effective)
  4924	        # The cancel flag and the deadline of this write are published
  4925	        # for the partial write: every segment of a partial write checks
  4926	        # the cancel flag and the deadline of the round.  They are not
  4927	        # cleared when the write ends: a segment that is still in flight
  4928	        # inside the worker has to observe the cancel of this write.
  4929	        self._write_cancel = cancel
  4930	        self._write_deadline = effective
  4931	        return self._await(self._writer, bytes(data), deadline, cancel)
  4932	
  4933	    def _read_block(self, request):
  4934	        return self._api.read_stream(self._handle, PIPE_READ_BYTES)
  4935	
  4936	    def _current_write_deadline(self):
  4937	        """The deadline that the next segment of a write really uses.
  4938	
  4939	        The value is read **again** for every segment of a partial
  4940	        write.  A copy of the deadline that was taken when the write
  4941	        started would let the write continue past a deadline that the
  4942	        round published in the meantime, and the shared context of the
  4943	        round is the only authority of the deadline.
  4944	
  4945	        The **published cleanup deadline** is part of it: a write that
  4946	        is still running when the round entered its cleanup is bounded by
  4947	        the cleanup budget of the round and not by the long lease.
  4948	        """
  4949	        value = self._write_deadline
  4950	        bound = self._deadline
  4951	        if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
  4952	            value = bound
  4953	        context = self._context
  4954	        if context is not None:
  4955	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
  4956	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
  4957	                value = absolute
  4958	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  4959	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
  4960	                value = cleanup
  4961	        return value
  4962	
  4963	    def _write_block(self, request):
  4964	        """Write the whole block, report the number of the written bytes.
  4965	
  4966	        Windows may write less than the block that it was given.  The
  4967	        endpoint repeats the write until the whole block was written or
  4968	        until the write fails with a named error.  **Every segment** of
  4969	        the partial write checks the cancel flag and the deadline of the
  4970	        round: a write that is not bounded is not a write.
  4971	        """
  4972	        data = bytes(request)
  4973	        total = 0
  4974	        while total < len(data):
  4975	            cancel = self._write_cancel
  4976	            if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
  4977	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name)
  4978	            deadline = self._current_write_deadline()
  4979	            if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
  4980	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
  4981	            count = self._api.write_stream(self._handle, data[total:])
  4982	            if count is None or count <= 0:
  4983	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_WRITE_FAILED',
  4984	                                  '%s:written=%d' % (self._name, total))
  4985	            total += count
  4986	        return total
  4987	
  4988	    def close(self):
  4989	        """Close the original handle of this endpoint, exactly once.
  4990	
  4991	        The close stops the workers from accepting a new task first, it
  4992	        asks for the cancellation of the operation that is in flight and
  4993	        it joins the workers with a bounded wait.  A worker that did not
  4994	        stop keeps its owner reference: the file is never closed under a
  4995	        worker that is still inside its operation.
  4996	
  4997	        The close is **committed** only when it really happened.  A
  4998	        close that failed keeps the file and its owner: the endpoint
  4999	        may try it again inside the deadline of the round and it is
  5000	        never marked as closed irreversibly.  A close of an endpoint
  5001	        that is already closed keeps the fact that it is closed: the
  5002	        second close is idempotent and it never turns a proven close
  5003	        into a missing one.
  5004	
  5005	        The state of the workers is **recomputed** on every close: a
  5006	        worker that really ended and whose handle of its thread was
  5007	        released is not retained any more, and a worker whose handle of
  5008	        its thread could not be closed is retained again and it may be
  5009	        released by a later close.  A closed file never locks the
  5010	        recovery of the handle of a thread.
  5011	        """
  5012	        deadline = self._effective_deadline()
  5013	        alive = False
  5014	        retained = []
  5015	        for worker in (self._reader, self._writer):
  5016	            if worker is None:
  5017	                continue
  5018	            worker.stop_accepting()
  5019	            worker.request_cancel(deadline)
  5020	            if not worker.join_bounded(self._remaining(deadline)):
  5021	                # The worker did not stop: it keeps its owner and the
  5022	                # file is never closed under it.
  5023	                alive = True
  5024	                retained.append(worker)
  5025	                continue
  5026	            # The thread of the worker really ended: the original handle
  5027	            # of the thread may be released now.  A handle that could
  5028	            # not be closed keeps its owner and a later close may try it
  5029	            # again.
  5030	            if not worker.release_thread_handle():
  5031	                retained.append(worker)
  5032	        # The state of the workers is **recomputed** on every close: a
  5033	        # worker that really ended and whose handle of its thread was
  5034	        # released is not retained any more.  A closed file never locks
  5035	        # the recovery of the handle of a thread.
  5036	        self._retained = retained
  5037	        self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
  5038	        self.facts['worker_alive'] = alive
  5039	        self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = all(
  5040	            worker.thread_handle_released
  5041	            for worker in (self._reader, self._writer) if worker is not None)
  5042	        if alive:
  5043	            # A live worker keeps the file: the file is never closed
  5044	            # under a worker that is still inside its operation.
  5045	            self.facts['closed'] = False
  5046	            return self.facts
  5047	        if self._closed:
  5048	            self.facts['closed'] = True
  5049	            self.facts['already_closed'] = True
  5050	            return self.facts
  5051	        try:
  5052	            self._file.close()
  5053	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5054	            self.facts['closed'] = False
  5055	            self.facts['error_type'] = type(error).__name__
  5056	            return self.facts
  5057	        self._closed = True
  5058	        self.facts['closed'] = True
  5059	        return self.facts
  5060	
  5061	
  5062	def _make_endpoint(file_object, name, *, api=None, clock=None, context=None):
  5063	    """The endpoint of one end of the private pipe of this host.
  5064	
  5065	    The Windows host gets the supervised and cancellable endpoint and
  5066	    every other host gets the non blocking endpoint of the original
  5067	    descriptor.  The choice is made by the host and never by a string
  5068	    that a caller could set: a platform that is not Windows never loads
  5069	    the Windows table.
  5070	
  5071	    The Windows endpoint needs the primitive table of the host, because
  5072	    the cancellation of a synchronous operation needs the original
  5073	    handle of the thread that performs it.  The table is loaded only on
  5074	    Windows.
  5075	
  5076	    The ONE context of the round is handed to the endpoint: the
  5077	    endpoint reads the absolute deadline of the round from it and it
  5078	    never keeps a budget of its own.  The context is bound to the
  5079	    **primitive table** as well when the endpoint owns the table: a
  5080	    table that does not know the context of the round would open a
  5081	    budget of its own.
  5082	    """
  5083	    if os.name == 'nt':
  5084	        if api is None:
  5085	            api = WindowsApi()
  5086	        binder = getattr(api, 'bind_context', None)
  5087	        if callable(binder) and context is not None:
  5088	            try:
  5089	                binder(context)
  5090	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5091	                pass
  5092	        return _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, api=api, clock=clock,
  5093	                                    context=context)
  5094	    return _PosixPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, clock=clock)
  5095	
  5096	
  5097	# -- the private identity of a cancellation of the round --------------------
  5098	
  5099	class _CloseSource:
  5100	    """The private identity of one close of one channel.
  5101	
  5102	    The identity is an **object**: two closes of the same channel never
  5103	    share it and the owner of a cancellation is compared by identity
  5104	    and never by the name of a code.  The sequence is the order of the
  5105	    closes of the channel and it is part of the evidence.
  5106	    """
  5107	
  5108	    def __init__(self, sequence):
  5109	        self.sequence = sequence
  5110	        self.owner = 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'
  5111	
  5112	
  5113	class _ChannelCancel:
  5114	    """The cancel flag of one channel, with the owner that set it.
  5115	
  5116	    The flag names the **owner** that set it: a cancellation that the
  5117	    close of this channel initiated is provable and a cancellation that
  5118	    another owner initiated is not.  The owner is registered in the
  5119	    same atomic step as the setting of the flag and it is registered
  5120	    **before** the flag is set, so that a reader can never see the flag
  5121	    set without its owner.
  5122	
  5123	    The **first** owner wins: a flag that another owner already set is
  5124	    never re-labelled by a later one.  A cancellation that this channel
  5125	    did not initiate is therefore never taken for a cancellation of the
  5126	    close of this channel.
  5127	    """
  5128	
  5129	    def __init__(self):
  5130	        self._event = threading.Event()
  5131	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
  5132	        self._owner = None
  5133	
  5134	    def is_set(self):
  5135	        return self._event.is_set()
  5136	
  5137	    def set(self, owner=None):
  5138	        """Set the flag and name the owner that set it.
  5139	
  5140	        The owner is written under the lock of the flag and it is
  5141	        written **before** the flag is set: the registration of the
  5142	        owner is never later than the cancellation itself.
  5143	        """
  5144	        with self._lock:
  5145	            if not self._event.is_set():
  5146	                if owner is not None:
  5147	                    self._owner = owner
  5148	                self._event.set()
  5149	
  5150	    def owner(self):
  5151	        """The owner that set the flag, or None when it named none."""
  5152	        with self._lock:
  5153	            return self._owner
  5154	
  5155	    def wait(self, timeout=None):
  5156	        return self._event.wait(timeout)
  5157	
  5158	
  5159	def _cancel_source_error(error, cancel):
  5160	    """Stamp the owner of a cancel flag on the error of a cancellation.
  5161	
  5162	    The owner is read from the flag **at the origin of the error**: the
  5163	    error itself carries the identity of the owner that really set the
  5164	    flag and it never carries a claim of a caller.  A flag that names
  5165	    no owner leaves the error untouched: an unknown source is never
  5166	    taken for a known one.
  5167	    """
  5168	    owner = getattr(cancel, 'owner', None)
  5169	    if not callable(owner):
  5170	        return error
  5171	    try:
  5172	        source = owner()
  5173	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5174	        return error
  5175	    if source is None:
  5176	        return error
  5177	    try:
  5178	        error.lvw_cancel_source = source
  5179	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5180	        pass
  5181	    return error
  5182	
  5183	
  5184	# -- the private control channel -------------------------------------------
  5185	
  5186	class StreamChannel:
  5187	    """The private control channel of the broker.
  5188	
  5189	    The channel is the broker's own standard input and output.  It is
  5190	    bounded three times: one frame is at most 32 KiB, the channel carries
  5191	    at most 1024 frames and the text of the children is bounded by the
  5192	    core.
  5193	
  5194	    The read side is drained by one background thread, so the broker never
  5195	    blocks on the read.  A parent that stops talking therefore cannot hold
  5196	    the deadline of the round: the deadline is checked by the broker
  5197	    itself.
  5198	
  5199	    The channel owns the original handles of its two ends.  A read and a
  5200	    write are bounded by an absolute deadline and by a cancel flag, so a
  5201	    peer that never reads can never hold the supervisor of the round.
  5202	    """
  5203	
  5204	    def __init__(self, reader, writer, *, max_frames=MAX_FRAMES,
  5205	                 max_frame_bytes=MAX_FRAME_BYTES, name='channel',
  5206	                 read_endpoint=None, write_endpoint=None, clock=None, api=None,
  5207	                 context=None, custody=None):
  5208	        self._queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=max_frames)
  5209	        self._max_frames = max_frames
  5210	        self._max_frame_bytes = max_frame_bytes
  5211	        self._count = 0
  5212	        self._overflow = False
  5213	        self._overflow_facts = {}
  5214	        self._eof = False
  5215	        self._error = None
  5216	        self._buffer = b''
  5217	        self._deadline = None
  5218	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  5219	        # The cancel flag of the channel names the owner that set it:
  5220	        # only a cancellation that the close of **this** channel
  5221	        # initiated is provable and only a provable one is a normal
  5222	        # fact of the close.
  5223	        self._cancel = _ChannelCancel()
  5224	        # The private state of the close of this channel: the identity
  5225	        # of the close that is running now and the named fact of the
  5226	        # cancellation that this close really initiated.
  5227	        self._close_source = None
  5228	        self._close_sequence = 0
  5229	        self._close_cancellation = None
  5230	        # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
  5231	        # failure of the reader: it is a parallel fact and it never
  5232	        # replaces the failure.
  5233	        self._parallel_cancellation = None
  5234	        self.reader_alive = None
  5235	        self.reader_finished = None
  5236	        # The ONE context of the round.  The channel and its endpoints
  5237	        # read the absolute deadline of the round from it and they never
  5238	        # keep a budget of their own.
  5239	        self._context = context
  5240	        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._drain, daemon=True,
  5241	                                        name='lvw-native-custody-channel')
  5242	        # The channel registers itself with the custody of the launcher
  5243	        # **before** it builds anything: a channel whose construction
  5244	        # failed half way - an endpoint that could not be built, a
  5245	        # reader thread that did not start - may not leave an endpoint
  5246	        # and a thread without an owner.  The outer owner of the launch
  5247	        # finds the partial channel through this registration and it
  5248	        # never closes the files of the process by itself.
  5249	        self._custody = custody
  5250	        if custody is not None:
  5251	            custody.retain('channel', self, 'constructing')
  5252	        try:
  5253	            self._reader = read_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
  5254	                reader, name + '-read', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
  5255	            # The parameter of the constructor is ``write_endpoint`` and
  5256	            # the attribute of the channel is ``_writer``.  The two names
  5257	            # are never mixed: a name that does not exist raises a
  5258	            # ``NameError`` and every construction of a channel of this
  5259	            # package fails with it.
  5260	            self._writer = write_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
  5261	                writer, name + '-write', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
  5262	            self._thread.start()
  5263	        except BaseException:
  5264	            # The construction of the channel did not return: the
  5265	            # channel keeps its owner in the custody of the launcher and
  5266	            # it is never forgotten.
  5267	            raise
  5268	
  5269	    def bind_context(self, context):
  5270	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to the channel.
  5271	
  5272	        The context is bound to the channel **and** to its two
  5273	        endpoints: every layer of the round reads the same absolute
  5274	        deadline and none of them keeps a deadline of its own.
  5275	        """
  5276	        if context is not None:
  5277	            self._context = context
  5278	            for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
  5279	                binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_context', None)
  5280	                if callable(binder):
  5281	                    binder(context)
  5282	        return self._context
  5283	
  5284	    def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
  5285	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the channel.
  5286	
  5287	        The deadline is bound to the channel **and** to its two
  5288	        endpoints.  A binding that happens after a read started still
  5289	        tightens that read: the deadline is a property of the endpoint
  5290	        and not a local argument of one call.
  5291	        """
  5292	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  5293	        if value is None:
  5294	            return self._deadline
  5295	        if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
  5296	            return self._deadline
  5297	        self._deadline = value
  5298	        for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
  5299	            binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_deadline', None)
  5300	            if callable(binder):
  5301	                try:
  5302	                    binder(value)
  5303	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5304	                    pass
  5305	        return value
  5306	
  5307	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  5308	        """The ONE deadline that a call of this channel really uses.
  5309	
  5310	        The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
  5311	        channel, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
  5312	        cleanup deadline of the round are one value: the earliest of the
  5313	        four.
  5314	
  5315	        The published cleanup deadline is part of it.  A round that
  5316	        entered its cleanup publishes one deadline, and the transport of
  5317	        the round - the write of the terminal reply and the close of the
  5318	        channel - is bound by it: a transport that still waited with the
  5319	        long lease of the round would outlive the only cleanup budget
  5320	        of the round.
  5321	        """
  5322	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  5323	        bound = self._deadline
  5324	        if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
  5325	            value = bound
  5326	        context = self._context
  5327	        if context is not None:
  5328	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
  5329	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
  5330	                value = absolute
  5331	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  5332	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
  5333	                value = cleanup
  5334	        return value
  5335	
  5336	    def _remaining(self, deadline=None):
  5337	        """The seconds that are left until the deadline of the channel."""
  5338	        value = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  5339	        if value is None:
  5340	            return None
  5341	        return max(0.0, value - (self._clock or time.monotonic)())
  5342	
  5343	    def _drain(self):
  5344	        try:
  5345	            while True:
  5346	                block = self._reader.read(self._deadline, self._cancel)
  5347	                if not block:
  5348	                    break
  5349	                buffer = self._buffer + block
  5350	                self._buffer = b''
  5351	                while True:
  5352	                    index = buffer.find(b'\n')
  5353	                    if index < 0:
  5354	                        break
  5355	                    raw = buffer[:index + 1]
  5356	                    buffer = buffer[index + 1:]
  5357	                    if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
  5358	                        self._record_overflow('FRAME_TOO_LARGE',
  5359	                                              {'frame_bytes': len(raw),
  5360	                                               'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
  5361	                        return
  5362	                    self._count += 1
  5363	                    if self._count > self._max_frames:
  5364	                        self._record_overflow('TOO_MANY_FRAMES',
  5365	                                              {'frames': self._count,
  5366	                                               'limit': self._max_frames})
  5367	                        return
  5368	                    while True:
  5369	                        self._check_wait()
  5370	                        try:
  5371	                            self._queue.put(raw, timeout=self._put_wait())
  5372	                            break
  5373	                        except queue.Full:
  5374	                            if self._eof:
  5375	                                return
  5376	                            continue
  5377	                # The remainder of the block has no newline.  The buffer
  5378	                # of the channel is bounded: it is checked on **every**
  5379	                # append and an oversize buffer is refused at once.  One
  5380	                # byte beyond the limit is kept, so that the overflow is
  5381	                # detected and not silently trimmed.
  5382	                if len(buffer) > self._max_frame_bytes:
  5383	                    self._buffer = buffer[:self._max_frame_bytes + 1]
  5384	                    self._record_overflow('BUFFER_TOO_LARGE',
  5385	                                          {'buffer_bytes': len(self._buffer),
  5386	                                           'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
  5387	                    return
  5388	                self._buffer = buffer
  5389	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5390	            self._record_reader_error(error)
  5391	        finally:
  5392	            self._eof = True
  5393	            if self._buffer and self._error is None:
  5394	                # The stream ended in the middle of a frame.  An
  5395	                # incomplete frame is a named failure of the channel and
  5396	                # it is never dropped silently.
  5397	                self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_PARTIAL_FRAME'
  5398	
  5399	    def _record_overflow(self, reason, facts):
  5400	        """Record the overflow of the channel at the place where it happened.
  5401	
  5402	        The overflow is a **named** failure of the channel and it is
  5403	        recorded **here**, where it really happened.  A flag that only
  5404	        the internals of the channel know would leave the close of the
  5405	        channel with no error at all: the reader of a channel that
  5406	        overflowed would look like a reader that ended normally and the
  5407	        round would release its resources on a fact that never happened.
  5408	
  5409	        The error of the channel is **sticky**: the first named failure
  5410	        of the reader stays the error of the channel and the overflow
  5411	        never replaces it.
  5412	        """
  5413	        self._overflow = True
  5414	        self._overflow_facts = dict(facts, reason=reason)
  5415	        if self._error is None:
  5416	            self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW'
  5417	
  5418	    def _record_reader_error(self, error):
  5419	        """Record the error of the reader of this channel.
  5420	
  5421	        A cancellation that **this** channel initiated with **this**
  5422	        close is not a failure of the reader: it is a named fact of the
  5423	        close.  It is kept with its code, with its source and with its
  5424	        order for the evidence, and it never replaces the error of the
  5425	        reader and it never clears a historical error of the reader.
  5426	
  5427	        Every other error keeps the original error of the reader and the
  5428	        round stays unknown: an ordinary failure of the read, a timeout,
  5429	        an overflow, a partial frame, an error that was already there
  5430	        before the close and a cancellation whose owner is unknown or
  5431	        is not the close of this channel.
  5432	        """
  5433	        source = getattr(error, 'lvw_cancel_source', None)
  5434	        if source is not None and source is self._close_source:
  5435	            # This cancellation was initiated by the close of this
  5436	            # channel: it is a named fact of the close and it is not a
  5437	            # failure of the reader.  The historical error of the reader
  5438	            # is never cleared and it is never replaced by this fact.
  5439	            self._close_cancellation = {
  5440	                'code': getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__,
  5441	                'source': getattr(source, 'owner', 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'),
  5442	                'sequence': getattr(source, 'sequence', None),
  5443	                'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  5444	            }
  5445	            return
  5446	        parallel = getattr(error, 'lvw_parallel_cancellation', None)
  5447	        if parallel is not None:
  5448	            # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
  5449	            # failure of the reader is a **parallel** fact: it is named
  5450	            # next to the failure and it never replaces it.
  5451	            self._parallel_cancellation = dict(parallel)
  5452	        self._error = getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__
  5453	
  5454	    def _check_wait(self):
  5455	        """The cancel flag and the deadline of the wait of the reader."""
  5456	        if self._cancel.is_set():
  5457	            # The error carries the owner of the flag: the reader of
  5458	            # the channel and the close of the channel agree on the
  5459	            # origin of the cancellation and the evidence can prove it.
  5460	            raise _cancel_source_error(BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_CANCELLED'),
  5461	                                       self._cancel)
  5462	        remaining = self._remaining()
  5463	        if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
  5464	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_TIMEOUT')
  5465	
  5466	    def _put_wait(self):
  5467	        """The seconds that the next put of the reader may ask for.
  5468	
  5469	        The put is the bounded wait of the package, cropped to the
  5470	        time that is really left until the **current** effective
  5471	        deadline of the round.  There is no positive floor beyond it:
  5472	        an expired round is refused by the check that precedes the put
  5473	        and the put itself never waits longer than the round allows.
  5474	        """
  5475	        remaining = self._remaining()
  5476	        if remaining is None:
  5477	            return CHANNEL_QUEUE_PUT_SECONDS
  5478	        return max(0.0, min(CHANNEL_QUEUE_PUT_SECONDS, remaining))
  5479	
  5480	    def read(self, timeout):
  5481	        """One raw frame, ``None`` when nothing arrived, ``b''`` at the end."""
  5482	        if self._overflow:
  5483	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW')
  5484	        try:
  5485	            return self._queue.get(timeout=max(0.0, timeout))
  5486	        except queue.Empty:
  5487	            if self._error:
  5488	                raise BrokerError(self._error)
  5489	            if self._eof:
  5490	                return b''
  5491	            return None
  5492	
  5493	    def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
  5494	        """Write one frame, bounded by the deadline of the round.
  5495	
  5496	        The number of the bytes that really went into the pipe is
  5497	        checked: a partial write is never reported as a success.
  5498	        """
  5499	        if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
  5500	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_FRAME_TOO_LARGE')
  5501	        target = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  5502	        written = self._writer.write(raw, target, self._cancel)
  5503	        if written is not None and written != len(raw):
  5504	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_WRITE_INCOMPLETE',
  5505	                              'written=%s:expected=%d' % (written, len(raw)))
  5506	        return written
  5507	
  5508	    def close(self):
  5509	        """Close the channel and report whether the reader really stopped.
  5510	
  5511	        The reader thread is joined with a bounded wait.  A reader that
  5512	        did not stop is reported as such: a daemon thread is not a
  5513	        finished thread, so the fact that the reader is still alive is
  5514	        kept visible instead of being hidden behind the close.  The read
  5515	        end is closed by its owner only when the reader really stopped.
  5516	
  5517	        The facts of the close carry the error of the reader, the number
  5518	        of the workers that the endpoints retained and the fact that the
  5519	        original handles of those workers were released: a reader that
  5520	        ended with an error is not a successful end.
  5521	
  5522	        The close may be **repeated**: a close that did not confirm its
  5523	        ends may try them again, and a close of an endpoint that is
  5524	        already closed keeps the fact that it is closed.
  5525	
  5526	        The error of the reader of the channel is **sticky** and it is
  5527	        never cleared by a later close of the channel: a close that
  5528	        returned is not a release and the error of the reader stays.
  5529	
  5530	        The close **initiates** the cancellation of the reader and it
  5531	        names itself as the owner of that cancellation before the flag
  5532	        is set.  A cancellation that this close really initiated is a
  5533	        named fact of the close and it is not a failure of the reader; a
  5534	        cancellation of another owner, a failure of the read that
  5535	        happened before the close and every other error of the reader
  5536	        keep the round unknown.
  5537	        """
  5538	        facts = {}
  5539	        # The identity of this close is published **before** the
  5540	        # cancellation is initiated and the flag registers the owner in
  5541	        # the same atomic step as the setting of the flag.
  5542	        self._close_sequence += 1
  5543	        source = _CloseSource(self._close_sequence)
  5544	        self._close_source = source
  5545	        self._cancel.set(source)
  5546	        try:
  5547	            write_facts = self._writer.close()
  5548	            facts['write_end_closed'] = bool(write_facts.get('closed'))
  5549	            facts['write_retained_workers'] = int(
  5550	                write_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
  5551	            facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5552	                write_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5553	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5554	            facts['write_end_closed'] = False
  5555	            facts['write_retained_workers'] = 0
  5556	            facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
  5557	        remaining = self._remaining()
  5558	        join_seconds = CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS if remaining is None else remaining
  5559	        start_unconfirmed = False
  5560	        try:
  5561	            self._thread.join(join_seconds)
  5562	        except RuntimeError:
  5563	            # The join could not prove the end of the reader: the start
  5564	            # of the thread of the reader was **interrupted** or it was
  5565	            # never confirmed.  A start that did not return may still
  5566	            # come up and a late reader may still read from the read
  5567	            # end of the channel.  The state of the reader therefore
  5568	            # stays **unknown**: the read end is never closed underneath
  5569	            # a possibly late target and the round keeps the fact.
  5570	            self.reader_alive = True
  5571	            self.reader_finished = False
  5572	            start_unconfirmed = True
  5573	        else:
  5574	            self.reader_alive = self._thread.is_alive()
  5575	            self.reader_finished = not self.reader_alive
  5576	        facts['reader_finished'] = self.reader_finished
  5577	        facts['reader_error'] = self._error
  5578	        # The start of the reader that was never confirmed is a named
  5579	        # fact of the close: the state of the reader is unknown and it
  5580	        # is never read as a finished reader.
  5581	        facts['reader_start_unconfirmed'] = start_unconfirmed
  5582	        # The facts of the overflow are published with the close: the
  5583	        # overflow is a named failure of the channel and it is never
  5584	        # only an internal flag.
  5585	        facts['overflow'] = bool(self._overflow)
  5586	        facts['overflow_facts'] = dict(self._overflow_facts)
  5587	        # The parallel fact of the cancellation of the close: it never
  5588	        # replaces the error of the reader.
  5589	        facts['reader_parallel_cancellation'] = (
  5590	            dict(self._parallel_cancellation)
  5591	            if self._parallel_cancellation else None)
  5592	        # The named fact of the cancellation that this close really
  5593	        # initiated.  It is **not** the error of the reader and it does
  5594	        # not prove that the reader, the thread, the worker or the
  5595	        # handle of the round were released: the strict facts of the
  5596	        # close above are the ones that prove it.
  5597	        facts['close_cancellation'] = (dict(self._close_cancellation)
  5598	                                       if self._close_cancellation else None)
  5599	        if self.reader_alive:
  5600	            # The reader is still inside the pipe: the read end is not
  5601	            # closed under it.  It stays owned by the channel and the
  5602	            # round keeps the fact.  The facts of the reader endpoint are
  5603	            # the real ones and they are never replaced by a zero.
  5604	            endpoint_facts = getattr(self._reader, 'facts', None)
  5605	            endpoint_facts = endpoint_facts if isinstance(endpoint_facts, dict) else {}
  5606	            facts['read_end_closed'] = False
  5607	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
  5608	                endpoint_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
  5609	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5610	                endpoint_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5611	            facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5612	                facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
  5613	                and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5614	            facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
  5615	                                                  + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
  5616	            return facts
  5617	        try:
  5618	            read_facts = self._reader.close()
  5619	            facts['read_end_closed'] = bool(read_facts.get('closed'))
  5620	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
  5621	                read_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
  5622	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5623	                read_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5624	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5625	            facts['read_end_closed'] = False
  5626	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = 0
  5627	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
  5628	        facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5629	            facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
  5630	            and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5631	        facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
  5632	                                              + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
  5633	        return facts
  5634	
  5635	    @property
  5636	    def eof(self):
  5637	        return self._eof
  5638	
  5639	    @property
  5640	    def overflow(self):
  5641	        return self._overflow
  5642	
  5643	    @property
  5644	    def frames(self):
  5645	        return self._count
  5646	
  5647	
  5648	# -- the bounded command loop ----------------------------------------------
  5649	
  5650	class _StartupOwner:
  5651	    """The bounded owner of one startup step of the round.
  5652	
  5653	    A startup step may block: the creation of a job, the creation of a
  5654	    process and the assignment of a process may all block inside the
  5655	    operating system.  Such a step may not run unbounded inside the one
  5656	    supervision thread of the round.
  5657	
  5658	    The owner therefore runs the step in its own thread and hands the
  5659	    result over through a **bounded** channel of one slot.  The
  5660	    supervisor waits for the result until the absolute deadline of the
  5661	    round.  A step that did not finish in time keeps the round unknown,
  5662	    keeps the thread and its owner, and a result that arrives late may
  5663	    only clean up: it may never continue the startup of the round.
  5664	    """
  5665	
  5666	    def __init__(self, *, name, clock, deadline, context=None):
  5667	        self.name = name
  5668	        self._clock = clock
  5669	        self._deadline = deadline
  5670	        # The ONE context of the round.  The owner reads the **current**
  5671	        # absolute deadline and the **published** cleanup deadline of
  5672	        # the round from it and it never keeps a copy of a deadline of
  5673	        # its own.
  5674	        self._context = context
  5675	        self._results = queue.Queue(maxsize=STARTUP_INFLIGHT_SLOTS)
  5676	        self._thread = None
  5677	        self._error = None
  5678	        self._value = None
  5679	        self._finished = threading.Event()
  5680	        self._expired = False
  5681	        # Whether the start of the thread of this owner **returned**.
  5682	        # A start that did not return may still come up: the owner keeps
  5683	        # its thread and the round keeps the fact.  The flag is never
  5684	        # the authority of the question: the facts of the thread itself
  5685	        # are.
  5686	        self._started = False
  5687	
  5688	    def start(self, action):
  5689	        """Run one startup step in the thread of this owner.
  5690	
  5691	        The start of the thread is **one** step and it is recorded: a
  5692	        start that did not return keeps its owner and the thread of the
  5693	        step may still come up.  The owner is never released because
  5694	        the start failed.
  5695	
  5696	        A start that raised **after** the thread really came up leaves
  5697	        the flag of the owner unset, and the facts of the thread are
  5698	        consulted instead: the thread of the step is a thread of the
  5699	        round and the owner may join it.
  5700	        """
  5701	        self._thread = threading.Thread(
  5702	            target=self._run, args=(action,), daemon=True,
  5703	            name='lvw-native-custody-startup-' + self.name)
  5704	        try:
  5705	            self._thread.start()
  5706	        except BaseException:
  5707	            # The start did not return: the thread may really have come
  5708	            # up and the facts of the thread decide.
  5709	            self._started = self._thread_facts_started()
  5710	            raise
  5711	        self._started = True
  5712	        return self
  5713	
  5714	    def _thread_facts_started(self):
  5715	        """Whether the original facts of the thread prove that it started.
  5716	
  5717	        The private flag of the owner is **not** the authority: it is
  5718	        written after the start returned and a start that raised after
  5719	        the thread really came up leaves it unset.  The identity of the
  5720	        thread and its liveness are the facts that decide whether a
  5721	        real join may be attempted.
  5722	        """
  5723	        thread = self._thread
  5724	        if thread is None:
  5725	            return False
  5726	        try:
  5727	            if getattr(thread, 'ident', None) is not None:
  5728	                return True
  5729	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5730	            return False
  5731	        try:
  5732	            return bool(thread.is_alive())
  5733	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5734	            return False
  5735	
  5736	    def _remaining(self):
  5737	        """The seconds that are left until the current bound of the step.
  5738	
  5739	        The bound is the **current** effective deadline of the original
  5740	        context of the round - its absolute deadline and the published
  5741	        cleanup deadline - together with the deadline that the owner
  5742	        received.  The value is recomputed on every call: a deadline
  5743	        that was published while the step was running shortens the wait
  5744	        that is running.
  5745	        """
  5746	        candidates = []
  5747	        context = self._context
  5748	        if context is not None:
  5749	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
  5750	            if absolute is not None:
  5751	                candidates.append(absolute)
  5752	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  5753	            if cleanup is not None:
  5754	                candidates.append(cleanup)
  5755	        if self._deadline is not None:
  5756	            candidates.append(self._deadline)
  5757	        if not candidates:
  5758	            return None
  5759	        return max(0.0, min(candidates) - self._clock())
  5760	
  5761	    def _cancelled(self):
  5762	        """Whether the original context of the round asked for the stop."""
  5763	        context = self._context
  5764	        return context is not None and context.cancel.is_set()
  5765	
  5766	    def _run(self, action):
  5767	        try:
  5768	            value = action()
  5769	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5770	            self._error = error
  5771	            outcome = ('error', error)
  5772	        else:
  5773	            self._value = value
  5774	            outcome = ('value', value)
  5775	        # The **attempt** of the step ended.  The event of the attempt is
  5776	        # published here and it is never a proof that the thread of the
  5777	        # step ended: the thread still has to publish its result and to
  5778	        # leave.
  5779	        self._finished.set()
  5780	        self._put(outcome)
  5781	
  5782	    def _put(self, item):
  5783	        try:
  5784	            self._results.put_nowait(item)
  5785	        except queue.Full:
  5786	            # The bounded channel is full: the result is dropped and the
  5787	            # owner keeps the error.  The channel never grows.
  5788	            pass
  5789	
  5790	    def await_result(self):
  5791	        """Wait for the result of the step, bounded by the deadline.
  5792	
  5793	        The result that is already published is **never** replaced by a
  5794	        refusal of the deadline: the original failure of the step is
  5795	        the error that leaves.
  5796	
  5797	        The wait of the next slice is cropped to the time that is
  5798	        really left until the **current** effective deadline of the
  5799	        original context of the round and there is no positive floor
  5800	        beyond it: an expired round refuses at once.
  5801	        """
  5802	        while True:
  5803	            try:
  5804	                kind, value = self._results.get_nowait()
  5805	            except queue.Empty:
  5806	                kind = None
  5807	                value = None
  5808	            if kind is not None:
  5809	                if kind == 'error':
  5810	                    raise value
  5811	                return value
  5812	            remaining = self._remaining()
  5813	            if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
  5814	                self._expired = True
  5815	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', self.name)
  5816	            if self._cancelled():
  5817	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', self.name)
  5818	            wait = POLL_SLICE_SECONDS
  5819	            if remaining is not None:
  5820	                wait = max(0.0, min(wait, remaining))
  5821	            try:
  5822	                kind, value = self._results.get(timeout=wait)
  5823	            except queue.Empty:
  5824	                continue
  5825	            if kind == 'error':
  5826	                raise value
  5827	            return value
  5828	
  5829	    def settle(self, seconds):
  5830	        """Wait, bounded, for the **thread** of this owner to end.
  5831	
  5832	        The wait is bounded by the shared cleanup deadline of the round
  5833	        and it never adds a settle budget of its own.
  5834	
  5835	        The end of the **attempt** of the step and the end of the
  5836	        **thread** that performs it are two different facts.  The event
  5837	        of the attempt is published before the result and before the
  5838	        thread leaves, and it is therefore never a proof that the
  5839	        thread ended.  The settle asks the thread itself: a thread that
  5840	        is still running keeps its owner and the round stays unknown.
  5841	
  5842	        A **start that did not return** is unknown only when the facts
  5843	        of the thread do not prove that it came up.  A thread that
  5844	        really started is joined for real, even when the start raised
  5845	        afterwards: the owner of the step coordinates the thread that it
  5846	        really has and it never releases it without a proof.
  5847	        """
  5848	        if self._thread is None:
  5849	            return True
  5850	        if not self._started and not self._thread_facts_started():
  5851	            # The start of the thread did not return and the thread
  5852	            # really did not come up: it may still come up and the owner
  5853	            # is never released.
  5854	            return False
  5855	        try:
  5856	            self._thread.join(max(0.0, seconds))
  5857	        except RuntimeError:
  5858	            # The thread was never started: it is not a thread of the
  5859	            # round that ended.
  5860	            return False
  5861	        return not self._thread.is_alive()
  5862	
  5863	    @property
  5864	    def expired(self):
  5865	        """Whether the step of this owner missed the deadline."""
  5866	        return self._expired
  5867	
  5868	    @property
  5869	    def in_flight(self):
  5870	        """Whether the step of this owner is still running."""
  5871	        return self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive()
  5872	
  5873	
  5874	class BrokerLoop:
  5875	    """The bounded command loop of the broker.
  5876	
  5877	    The loop owns the absolute deadline of the round.  It is fixed once,
  5878	    from the lease of the plan, and it is never moved: neither a status
  5879	    request nor a heartbeat may extend it.
  5880	
  5881	    The round is stopped when the parent asks for it, when the channel
  5882	    reaches its end, when a bound of the channel or of the text of the
  5883	    children is exceeded or when the absolute deadline arrives.  A stop
  5884	    that was not requested by the parent keeps the evidence of the round
  5885	    unknown: the round did not end in a proven release.
  5886	
  5887	    The startup of the round is supervised.  The arm and the launch are
  5888	    **bounded startup steps** with their own owner: a step that blocks
  5889	    may not hold the supervision of the round and a step that returns
  5890	    after the absolute deadline may only clean up.
  5891	    """
  5892	
  5893	    def __init__(self, *, core, transport, plan, nonce, clock=None, wall=None,
  5894	                 max_frames=MAX_FRAMES):
  5895	        self.core = core
  5896	        self.transport = transport
  5897	        self.plan = plan
  5898	        self.nonce = nonce
  5899	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  5900	        self._wall = wall or time.time
  5901	        self._max_frames = max_frames
  5902	        self._sequence = 0
  5903	        self._last_sequence = None
  5904	        self._frames = 0
  5905	        self._sent_frames = 0
  5906	        self._deadline_wall = None
  5907	        self._deadline_mono = None
  5908	        self._last_frame_wall = None
  5909	        self._last_alive_wall = None
  5910	        self._stopped = False
  5911	        # The ONE context of the round.  The loop holds the very same
  5912	        # object as the core: the deadlines, the cancel and the unknown
  5913	        # of the round are one state and not one number per layer.
  5914	        self.context = core.context
  5915	        # The owner of the startup step that is in flight.  A step that
  5916	        # did not end keeps the round unknown and it keeps its owner.
  5917	        self._startup_owner = None
  5918	        self._startup_in_flight = None
  5919	
  5920	    # -- the deadline ------------------------------------------------------
  5921	    def fix_deadline(self):
  5922	        """Fix the absolute deadline of the round and return it.
  5923	
  5924	        The deadline is fixed **before** the transport of the round
  5925	        exists: the reader of the channel and the workers of the
  5926	        endpoints never start without a deadline and the child has no
  5927	        unbound window.
  5928	        """
  5929	        return self._fix_deadline()
  5930	
  5931	    def bind_transport(self, transport):
  5932	        """Bind the transport of the round to this loop.
  5933	
  5934	        The transport is bound after the deadline of the round was
  5935	        fixed: the transport of the round is never created without the
  5936	        deadline that bounds it.
  5937	        """
  5938	        self.transport = transport
  5939	        return transport
  5940	
  5941	    def _fix_deadline(self):
  5942	        """Fix the absolute deadline of the round, once, before anything.
  5943	
  5944	        The deadline is fixed before the job of the round is created,
  5945	        before the first reply is written and before the first role is
  5946	        launched.  It is the earlier of the lease of the plan and the
  5947	        runtime of the plan and it is never moved afterwards.
  5948	        """
  5949	        if self._deadline_mono is not None:
  5950	            return self._deadline_mono
  5951	        wall_now = self._wall()
  5952	        self._deadline_wall = min(self.plan.lease_expires_at,
  5953	                                  wall_now + self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
  5954	        self._deadline_mono = self._clock() + max(0.0, self._deadline_wall - wall_now)
  5955	        return self._deadline_mono
  5956	
  5957	    def remaining_seconds(self):
  5958	        if self._deadline_mono is None:
  5959	            return float(self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
  5960	        return max(0.0, self._deadline_mono - self._clock())
  5961	
  5962	    # -- the replies -------------------------------------------------------
  5963	    def _check_frame_budget(self, reserved=RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES):
  5964	        """The frame budget of the channel, in both directions.
  5965	
  5966	        The budget of the round is shared by the frames that the broker
  5967	        sends and by the frames that it receives: the round of six hours
  5968	        with its heartbeat stays inside the budget of the channel.  The
  5969	        frames of the terminal exchange are reserved and the budget is
  5970	        never widened and never renewed.
  5971	
  5972	        The check is the **total** of both directions.  A single
  5973	        direction is never a substitute for the total: a channel that
  5974	        received a thousand frames and sent none is not inside the
  5975	        budget when the budget is a thousand and twenty four frames.
  5976	        """
  5977	        if self._frames + self._sent_frames + reserved + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
  5978	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED',
  5979	                              'sent=%d:received=%d' % (self._sent_frames,
  5980	                                                       self._frames))
  5981	
  5982	    def _reply(self, kind, state, detail, *, terminal=False):
  5983	        self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
  5984	        self._sent_frames += 1
  5985	        self._sequence += 1
  5986	        frame = {'schema': CHANNEL_SCHEMA, 'kind': kind, 'run_id': self.plan.run_id,
  5987	                 'candidate_sha': self.plan.candidate_sha,
  5988	                 'authorization_sha256': self.plan.authorization_sha256,
  5989	                 'nonce': self.nonce, 'sequence': self._sequence,
  5990	                 'state': state, 'detail': detail}
  5991	        raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
  5992	                         separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
  5993	        if len(raw) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
  5994	            frame['detail'] = {'truncated': True}
  5995	            raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
  5996	                             separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
  5997	        # The deadline of the round is already bound to the transport, so
  5998	        # the write of the frame is bounded by it.  The transport is
  5999	        # never called with an argument that it does not know.
  6000	        self.transport.write(raw + b'\n')
  6001	
  6002	    # -- the supervised startup --------------------------------------------
  6003	    def _guarded_startup(self, action, stage):
  6004	        """One startup step, guarded by the shared deadline.
  6005	
  6006	        The shared budget is checked **before** the step and **after**
