  6007	        it returned.  A step that starts after the deadline of the round
  6008	        arrived never runs at all, and a step that finished after the
  6009	        deadline may only clean up: it may never continue the startup of
  6010	        the round and it may never write a successful reply.
  6011	
  6012	        The supervision publishes the cancel of the round when the
  6013	        deadline arrived: the core and every later step of the startup
  6014	        see it and they stop.
  6015	        """
  6016	        def guarded():
  6017	            self._check_startup_budget(stage)
  6018	            action()
  6019	            self._check_startup_budget(stage)
  6020	            return None
  6021	        return guarded
  6022	
  6023	    def _check_startup_budget(self, stage):
  6024	        """Check the shared budget of the startup of the round."""
  6025	        try:
  6026	            self.core.check_startup_budget(stage)
  6027	        except BrokerError:
  6028	            # The supervision publishes the cancel of the round: the
  6029	            # core and every later step of the startup see it.
  6030	            self.context.request_cancel()
  6031	            self._abort_started_round()
  6032	            raise
  6033	
  6034	    def _abort_started_round(self):
  6035	        """Release what the round acquired, bounded by the shared budget."""
  6036	        try:
  6037	            self.core.stop('STARTUP_AFTER_DEADLINE', force_unknown=True)
  6038	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6039	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'startup_abort',
  6040	                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  6041	
  6042	    def _run_startup(self, stage, action):
  6043	        """Run one startup step under the absolute deadline of the round.
  6044	
  6045	        **Every** exit of this method waits for the **real** exit of the
  6046	        thread of the step: the success, the timeout, the ordinary
  6047	        failure, the cancellation and the ``SystemExit``.  The attempt
  6048	        of the step and the thread that performs it are two different
  6049	        facts and the owner of the step is released only after the real
  6050	        exit of its thread.
  6051	
  6052	        A thread that did not exit keeps its owner: it stays in the
  6053	        owners that are in flight and the round keeps the fact.  It is
  6054	        never released because the attempt ended and it is never given a
  6055	        new budget.
  6056	        """
  6057	        owner = _StartupOwner(name=stage, clock=self._clock,
  6058	                              deadline=self._deadline_mono,
  6059	                              context=self.context)
  6060	        self._startup_owner = owner
  6061	        self.context.register_inflight(owner)
  6062	        try:
  6063	            # The start of the step is **inside** the ownership of the
  6064	            # owner and inside the ``finally`` below: a start that did
  6065	            # not return is coordinated like every other exit and the
  6066	            # owner is never left in the flight without a decision.
  6067	            owner.start(self._guarded_startup(action, stage))
  6068	            value = owner.await_result()
  6069	        except BrokerError as error:
  6070	            if error.code not in ('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED',
  6071	                                  'BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED'):
  6072	                # An ordinary failure of the step: the thread of the
  6073	                # step is still coordinated in the finally below and the
  6074	                # original failure is the one that leaves this method.
  6075	                raise
  6076	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage, 'code': error.code})
  6077	            # The step did not finish before the deadline.  The owner
  6078	            # keeps its thread: a late result may only clean up.
  6079	            raise
  6080	        finally:
  6081	            # The budget of the settle is **recomputed after the await**
  6082	            # and immediately before the join: a deadline that was
  6083	            # published while the step was running still shortens the
  6084	            # wait and a settle budget that was copied before the await
  6085	            # is never used.  There is no positive floor beyond the
  6086	            # time that is really left.
  6087	            settle_budget = self._startup_settle_seconds()
  6088	            # The real exit of the thread is asked on **every** exit.
  6089	            try:
  6090	                settled = owner.settle(settle_budget)
  6091	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6092	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
  6093	                                           'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  6094	                settled = False
  6095	            if not settled:
  6096	                self._startup_in_flight = owner
  6097	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
  6098	                                           'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
  6099	            else:
  6100	                self.context.release_inflight(owner)
  6101	        return value
  6102	
  6103	    def _startup_settle_seconds(self):
  6104	        """The bounded wait for the real exit of a startup thread.
  6105	
  6106	        The wait is bounded by the **remaining** of the absolute
  6107	        deadline of the round and by the settle bound of the package.
  6108	        It never publishes the cleanup deadline of the round - the
  6109	        cleanup of a round that did not fail keeps its whole budget -
  6110	        and it never opens a budget of its own.
  6111	
  6112	        The remaining is recomputed on every call and it is never
  6113	        rounded **up**: a settle budget that is larger than the time
  6114	        that is really left would wait past the deadline of the round.
  6115	        The published cleanup deadline of the round shortens it when the
  6116	        round already entered its cleanup.
  6117	        """
  6118	        remaining = self.context.remaining_absolute()
  6119	        cleanup = self.context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  6120	        if cleanup is not None:
  6121	            left = max(0.0, cleanup - self._clock())
  6122	            remaining = left if remaining is None else min(remaining, left)
  6123	        if remaining is None:
  6124	            return STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS
  6125	        return max(0.0, min(remaining, STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS))
  6126	
  6127	    # -- the run -----------------------------------------------------------
  6128	    def run(self):
  6129	        """Run the round and always release what the round owns.
  6130	
  6131	        The loop has a ``finally`` that does not depend on the diagnosis
  6132	        of the round and not on the success of a write: a round that
  6133	        could not write its first reply still releases the job and the
  6134	        handles that it created.
  6135	        """
  6136	        self._fix_deadline()
  6137	        # The core and the loop read the same clock: the deadlines of
  6138	        # the round and the ONE cleanup deadline are in one domain.
  6139	        binder_clock = getattr(self.core, 'bind_clock', None)
  6140	        if callable(binder_clock):
  6141	            binder_clock(self._clock)
  6142	        self.core.bind_absolute_deadline(self._deadline_mono)
  6143	        binder = getattr(self.transport, 'bind_deadline', None)
  6144	        if callable(binder):
  6145	            # The transport of the channel is bound to the deadline of
  6146	            # the round before the job of the round exists: a read or a
  6147	            # write of the channel can never outlive the round.
  6148	            binder(self._deadline_mono)
  6149	        try:
  6150	            try:
  6151	                self._run_startup('ARM', self.core.arm)
  6152	            except BrokerError as error:
  6153	                self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
  6154	                            terminal=True)
  6155	                self._write_receipt('ARM_FAILED')
  6156	                return 3
  6157	            self._reply('READY', self.core.state, {'broker': self.core.broker_identity})
  6158	            try:
  6159	                self._run_startup('LAUNCH', self.core.launch)
  6160	            except BrokerError as error:
  6161	                self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
  6162	                            terminal=True)
  6163	                self._write_receipt('LAUNCH_FAILED')
  6164	                return 3
  6165	            self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
  6166	            exit_code = self._loop()
  6167	            self._write_receipt(self.core.stop_reason)
  6168	            return exit_code
  6169	        finally:
  6170	            self._ensure_stopped()
  6171	
  6172	    def _ensure_stopped(self):
  6173	        """Release the resources of the round, whatever happened before.
  6174	
  6175	        The release never raises: a failure of the release is kept by the
  6176	        core and it never replaces the first failure of the round.
  6177	        """
  6178	        if self._stopped:
  6179	            return
  6180	        self._stopped = True
  6181	        if self._startup_in_flight is not None:
  6182	            # A startup step is still running: the core belongs to the
  6183	            # in-flight owner and the round keeps the fact.  The cleanup
  6184	            # of the core would race with the step that is still inside
  6185	            # the operating system, so it is not started here.
  6186	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
  6187	                                       'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
  6188	            self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
  6189	            return
  6190	        try:
  6191	            self.core.stop(self.core.stop_reason or 'LOOP_EXIT', force_unknown=True)
  6192	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6193	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
  6194	                                        'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  6195	            self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
  6196	
  6197	    def _loop(self):
  6198	        self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
  6199	        while True:
  6200	            now = self._clock()
  6201	            if now >= self._deadline_mono:
  6202	                return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
  6203	            if self.core.overflowed():
  6204	                return self._stop('BOUND_EXCEEDED', 4)
  6205	            # The read of the transport is bounded by the time that is
  6206	            # really left until the absolute deadline of the round.
  6207	            # There is **no positive floor** beyond it: a round whose
  6208	            # deadline arrived asks for nothing and the loop decides
  6209	            # the lease that arrived.
  6210	            remaining = self._deadline_mono - now
  6211	            if remaining <= 0:
  6212	                return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
  6213	            timeout = min(POLL_SECONDS, remaining)
  6214	            try:
  6215	                raw = self.transport.read(timeout)
  6216	            except BrokerError:
  6217	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_ERROR', 4)
  6218	            if raw is None:
  6219	                # A missing heartbeat is a secondary signal.  The absolute
  6220	                # deadline stays the authority of the round.
  6221	                if self._wall() - self._last_frame_wall > HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:
  6222	                    return self._stop('HEARTBEAT_MISSING', 4)
  6223	                continue
  6224	            if raw == b'':
  6225	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_EOF', 4)
  6226	            # The hard cap of the channel is checked before the frame is
  6227	            # decoded: a frame that does not fit at all is refused
  6228	            # without being accepted.
  6229	            if self._frames + self._sent_frames + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
  6230	                return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
  6231	            try:
  6232	                frame = decode_command(raw, run_id=self.plan.run_id,
  6233	                                       candidate_sha=self.plan.candidate_sha,
  6234	                                       authorization_sha256=self.plan.authorization_sha256,
  6235	                                       nonce=self.nonce, last_sequence=self._last_sequence)
  6236	            except CustodyError as error:
  6237	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'channel', 'code': error.code})
  6238	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_REFUSED', 4)
  6239	            # The reservation of the terminal exchange is **consumed** by
  6240	            # the terminal frame itself.  A STOP that arrives near the
  6241	            # limit of the budget is a legal frame: it is never refused
  6242	            # because of the reservation that was kept for it.  A frame
  6243	            # that is not terminal never borrows the reservation and it
  6244	            # fails closed when the total does not fit.
  6245	            terminal = frame['command'] == 'STOP'
  6246	            try:
  6247	                self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
  6248	            except BrokerError:
  6249	                return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
  6250	            self._frames += 1
  6251	            if self._frames > self._max_frames:
  6252	                return self._stop('FRAME_COUNT_EXCEEDED', 4)
  6253	            # Only a frame that was decoded and that carries the identity
  6254	            # of the round refreshes the heartbeat of the round.  A frame
  6255	            # that was refused never does.
  6256	            self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
  6257	            self._last_alive_wall = self._last_frame_wall
  6258	            self._last_sequence = frame['sequence']
  6259	            if frame['command'] == 'STATUS':
  6260	                self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
  6261	                continue
  6262	            return self._stop('STOP_COMMAND', 0)
  6263	
  6264	    def _stop(self, reason, exit_code):
  6265	        unknown = reason != 'STOP_COMMAND'
  6266	        self.core.stop(reason, force_unknown=unknown)
  6267	        self._reply('STOPPED', self.core.state, self.core.summary(), terminal=True)
  6268	        return exit_code
  6269	
  6270	    def _write_receipt(self, reason):
  6271	        """Write the terminal receipt of the round into the run root.
  6272	
  6273	        The receipt is the evidence that survives a parent that died.  It
  6274	        is written once, it is bounded and it is never replaced by a
  6275	        successful claim.  A failure of the write never replaces the
  6276	        first failure of the round.
  6277	        """
  6278	        evidence = self.core.evidence()
  6279	        evidence['reason'] = reason
  6280	        evidence['channel'] = {'frames_received': self._frames,
  6281	                               'last_sequence_received': self._last_sequence,
  6282	                               'frame_limit': self._max_frames}
  6283	        evidence['deadline_wall'] = self._deadline_wall
  6284	        evidence['deadline_extended'] = False
  6285	        raw = json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
  6286	                         indent=2).encode('utf-8')
  6287	        if len(raw) > MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES:
  6288	            raw = json.dumps({'kind': 'lvw.s3-native-broker-receipt/1',
  6289	                              'reason': reason, 'truncated': True,
  6290	                              'stage_pass_claim': False}).encode('utf-8')
  6291	        path = Path(self.core.run_root) / 'temp' / 'native' / 'custody-terminal.json'
  6292	        try:
  6293	            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  6294	            path.write_bytes(raw)
  6295	        except OSError:
  6296	            return False
  6297	        return True
  6298	
  6299	
  6300	# -- the fixed entry of the broker -----------------------------------------
  6301	
  6302	def build_broker_command(*, plan_path, plan_sha256, run_root, nonce, python=None):
  6303	    """The fixed command of the broker.
  6304	
  6305	    The command starts exactly this broker with one sealed plan document.
  6306	    There is no dynamic import of a caller supplied module and no arbitrary
  6307	    argument vector: the only arguments are the plan, its digest, the run
  6308	    root of the round and the nonce of the session.
  6309	    """
  6310	    script = Path(__file__).resolve()
  6311	    if not script.is_file():
  6312	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_SCRIPT_MISSING')
  6313	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', str(plan_sha256)):
  6314	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_INVALID')
  6315	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(nonce)):
  6316	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_NONCE_INVALID')
  6317	    executable = python or sys.executable
  6318	    return [executable, '-I', '-B', str(script),
  6319	            '--plan', str(plan_path), '--plan-sha256', str(plan_sha256),
  6320	            '--run-root', str(run_root), '--nonce', str(nonce)]
  6321	
  6322	
  6323	class BrokerProcess:
  6324	    """The original handle of the broker process of one round.
  6325	
  6326	    The parent keeps the process handle, the pipes of the private channel
  6327	    and the identity of the executable.  The replies of the broker are
  6328	    read through one bounded background reader, so the parent never blocks
  6329	    on the channel and the deadline of the round is never held up by a
  6330	    silent broker.
  6331	
  6332	    The handle is **deadline aware**: it says so with its own marker and
  6333	    it takes the absolute deadline of every write.  A handle that is not
  6334	    deadline aware is never assumed to be deadline aware by the parent.
  6335	    """
  6336	
  6337	    #: The marker of a handle that understands an absolute deadline.
  6338	    channel_deadline_aware = True
  6339	
  6340	    def __init__(self, process, executable, executable_sha256, channel,
  6341	                 clock=None, context=None):
  6342	        self._process = process
  6343	        self._channel = channel
  6344	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  6345	        # The ONE context of the launch.  The close of the broker reads
  6346	        # the absolute deadline and the published cleanup deadline of
  6347	        # the round from it and it never opens a budget of its own.
  6348	        self._context = context
  6349	        self.pid = process.pid
  6350	        self.executable = executable
  6351	        self.executable_sha256 = executable_sha256
  6352	        # Whether the reader of the channel really stopped when the channel
  6353	        # was closed.  It is ``None`` until the close happened and it stays
  6354	        # ``False`` when the reader was still alive after the bounded join.
  6355	        self.reader_finished = None
  6356	        # The absolute deadline of the round, handed over by the parent.
  6357	        # The parent may only shorten it.
  6358	        self.write_deadline = None
  6359	        self.close_facts = None
  6360	
  6361	    def bind_channel_deadline(self, deadline):
  6362	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the private channel.
  6363	
  6364	        The deadline is a finite number of seconds of the monotonic clock
  6365	        and it may only shorten the deadline that is already bound: a
  6366	        later deadline is refused.
  6367	        """
  6368	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  6369	        if value is None:
  6370	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_INVALID')
  6371	        if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
  6372	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
  6373	        self.write_deadline = value
  6374	        binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
  6375	        if callable(binder):
  6376	            binder(value)
  6377	        return value
  6378	
  6379	    def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
  6380	        """Write one frame of the parent, bounded by the deadline.
  6381	
  6382	        The deadline is explicit and it is checked: a write that is not
  6383	        bounded by an absolute deadline would let a broker that never
  6384	        reads hold the supervisor of the round forever.
  6385	        """
  6386	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  6387	        if value is None:
  6388	            value = self.write_deadline
  6389	        if value is None:
  6390	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_MISSING')
  6391	        if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
  6392	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
  6393	        return self._channel.write(raw, value)
  6394	
  6395	    def read(self, timeout):
  6396	        return self._channel.read(timeout)
  6397	
  6398	    def close(self, deadline=None):
  6399	        """Close the original handle of the broker.
  6400	
  6401	        The close happens even when the exchange of the stop failed: the
  6402	        resource of this round is released either way.
  6403	
  6404	        The facts of the close are returned and they are named: a handle
  6405	        that was not closed, a reader that did not stop and a process that
  6406	        did not exit are facts of the round and they are never hidden
  6407	        behind a success.  The release of the original process handle is
  6408	        explicit and it is never left to the garbage collector.
  6409	
  6410	        The waits of the close are bounded by **one** absolute deadline:
  6411	        the deadline that the parent bound before it closed the handle.
  6412	        The remaining time is recomputed before every wait, so that a
  6413	        wait that already consumed part of the budget never gets the
  6414	        whole budget again and no layer of the cleanup adds a budget of
  6415	        its own.
  6416	
  6417	        The close may be **repeated** inside the same deadline: an item
  6418	        that was confirmed is never closed a second time and an item
  6419	        that was not confirmed is coordinated again.  The attempt that
  6420	        ended is not a release: only the facts of the close prove it.
  6421	
  6422	        The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
  6423	        round and not a fact of the last attempt: a repeated close keeps
  6424	        the first error that the reader really had, it publishes the
  6425	        source of that error and it never turns a real error into a
  6426	        ``None``.  The resource facts of the close may converge while
  6427	        the error history of the reader stays: a close that returned is
  6428	        not a release and a retry is not an amnesty.
  6429	
  6430	        The **endpoint is the only owner** of the files of the channel.
  6431	        The broker process therefore never closes the standard input and
  6432	        the standard output of the process by itself: it closes the
  6433	        channel, and the channel closes the files that it owns.
  6434	        """
  6435	        value = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
  6436	        budget = max(0.0, value - self._clock())
  6437	        facts = {'channel_closed': False,
  6438	                 'channel_reader_finished': None,
  6439	                 'channel_reader_error': None,
  6440	                 'channel_reader_error_source': None,
  6441	                 'channel_reader_secondary_errors': [],
  6442	                 'channel_overflow': False,
  6443	                 'channel_overflow_facts': {},
  6444	                 'close_cancellation': None,
  6445	                 'channel_retained_workers': 0,
  6446	                 'write_end_closed': False,
  6447	                 'read_end_closed': False,
  6448	                 'stdin_closed': False,
  6449	                 'stdout_closed': False,
  6450	                 'process_exited': False,
  6451	                 'exit_code': None,
  6452	                 'terminated': False,
  6453	                 'process_handle_released': False,
  6454	                 'process_handle_release_method': None,
  6455	                 'worker_thread_handle_released': False,
  6456	                 'cleanup_budget_seconds': budget,
  6457	                 'cleanup_deadline': value}
  6458	        # The facts of a previous attempt are kept: an item that was
  6459	        # confirmed is never closed a second time and its fact stays
  6460	        # true.
  6461	        previous = self.close_facts if isinstance(self.close_facts, dict) else {}
  6462	        for key, confirmed in previous.items():
  6463	            if key in ('cleanup_budget_seconds', 'cleanup_deadline'):
  6464	                continue
  6465	            if (confirmed is True
  6466	                    or (key == 'exit_code' and confirmed is not None)
  6467	                    or key == 'process_handle_release_method'
  6468	                    or key == 'close_cancellation'):
  6469	                facts[key] = confirmed
  6470	        # The channel and its endpoints use the very same absolute
  6471	        # deadline: they never start a wait of their own.
  6472	        binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
  6473	        if callable(binder):
  6474	            try:
  6475	                binder(value)
  6476	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6477	                pass
  6478	        if not facts['channel_closed']:
  6479	            channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
  6480	        elif not self._channel_complete(facts):
  6481	            # A previous attempt already called the close of the
  6482	            # channel, but it did not confirm **every** item of it: the
  6483	            # read end, the worker of an endpoint and the original
  6484	            # handle of its thread may still be open.  The items that
  6485	            # were not confirmed are coordinated again, inside the very
  6486	            # same deadline.  A close that returned is not a release.
  6487	            channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
  6488	        else:
  6489	            channel_facts = None
  6490	        if channel_facts is not None:
  6491	            facts.update(channel_facts)
  6492	        # The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
  6493	        # round: a repeated close never turns a real error of the reader
  6494	        # into a ``None``.  The first error is the error of the round and
  6495	        # a later error is kept beside it as a secondary one.
  6496	        self._keep_reader_error_history(facts, previous)
  6497	        # The files of the channel belong to the endpoints of the
  6498	        # channel.  The fact of their close is the fact of the channel.
  6499	        facts['stdin_closed'] = facts['write_end_closed']
  6500	        facts['stdout_closed'] = facts['read_end_closed']
  6501	        if not facts['process_exited']:
  6502	            try:
  6503	                # The bound of the cleanup is read **again** here: the
  6504	                # close of the channel may have published a shorter
  6505	                # cleanup deadline of the round and the wait of the
  6506	                # process receives the time that is really left.
  6507	                current = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
  6508	                self._process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, current - self._clock()))
  6509	                facts['process_exited'] = True
  6510	                facts['exit_code'] = self._process.returncode
  6511	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6512	                # Only the broker that this parent holds is terminated, and
  6513	                # its exit is confirmed afterwards with a bounded wait.
  6514	                try:
  6515	                    self._process.terminate()
  6516	                    facts['terminated'] = True
  6517	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6518	                    pass
  6519	                try:
  6520	                    # The bound is read **again** for the retry as well:
  6521	                    # a deadline that was published while the first wait
  6522	                    # ran shortens the retry too.
  6523	                    current = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
  6524	                    self._process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, current - self._clock()))
  6525	                    facts['process_exited'] = True
  6526	                    facts['exit_code'] = self._process.returncode
  6527	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6528	                    pass
  6529	        if not facts['process_handle_released']:
  6530	            facts.update(self._release_process_handle(facts['process_exited']))
  6531	        self.close_facts = facts
  6532	        self.reader_finished = facts['channel_reader_finished']
  6533	        return facts
  6534	
  6535	    @staticmethod
  6536	    def _keep_reader_error_history(facts, previous):
  6537	        """Keep the history of the error of the reader of the channel.
  6538	
  6539	        The **first** error that the reader of the channel really had is
  6540	        the error of the round: every later close keeps it and it is
  6541	        never turned into a ``None`` by a repeated close.  The resource
  6542	        facts of the close may converge, but a real error of the reader
  6543	        is never lost by the retry: the copy of the facts of a previous
  6544	        attempt keeps the facts that are ``True`` and it does **not**
  6545	        keep a code, so the error is restored here from the history
  6546	        itself.
  6547	
  6548	        A later error is a **secondary** error: it is kept beside the
  6549	        first one and it never replaces it.  The source of the error is
  6550	        published with it: ``FIRST_CLOSE`` when the close that is running
  6551	        now is the one that really observed the error and
  6552	        ``REPEATED_CLOSE`` when this attempt carries the error of an
  6553	        earlier one.  The fact of the error itself is the history and it
  6554	        is never replaced by a boolean.
  6555	
  6556	        The named fact of the cancellation of the close is independent
  6557	        from this history and it is never merged with it.
  6558	        """
  6559	        previous = previous if isinstance(previous, dict) else {}
  6560	        first = previous.get('channel_reader_error')
  6561	        current = facts.get('channel_reader_error')
  6562	        secondary = list(previous.get('channel_reader_secondary_errors') or [])
  6563	        if first:
  6564	            if current and current != first:
  6565	                # A second error of the reader is kept beside the first
  6566	                # one: the first error stays the error of the round.
  6567	                if current not in secondary:
  6568	                    secondary.append(current)
  6569	            facts['channel_reader_secondary_errors'] = secondary
  6570	            facts['channel_reader_error'] = first
  6571	            facts['channel_reader_error_source'] = 'REPEATED_CLOSE'
  6572	            return facts
  6573	        if current:
  6574	            facts['channel_reader_error_source'] = 'FIRST_CLOSE'
  6575	        return facts
  6576	
  6577	    @staticmethod
  6578	    def _channel_complete(facts):
  6579	        """Whether the close of the channel confirmed every item of it.
  6580	
  6581	        The items are the two ends of the channel, the reader of the
  6582	        channel, the workers of the endpoints and the original handles
  6583	        of their threads.  A fact that is missing is a missing fact and
  6584	        not a success.
  6585	        """
  6586	        if facts.get('channel_reader_finished') is not True:
  6587	            return False
  6588	        if facts.get('read_end_closed') is not True:
  6589	            return False
  6590	        if facts.get('write_end_closed') is not True:
  6591	            return False
  6592	        if int(facts.get('channel_retained_workers') or 0) != 0:
  6593	            return False
  6594	        if facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released') is not True:
  6595	            return False
  6596	        return True
  6597	
  6598	    def _close_channel(self, facts):
  6599	        """Close the channel of the round and report its facts.
  6600	
  6601	        The method returns the facts of the close of the channel and it
  6602	        never raises: the failure of the close of the channel is a fact
  6603	        of the round and it is named in the facts.
  6604	        """
  6605	        try:
  6606	            channel_facts = self._channel.close()
  6607	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6608	            facts['channel_closed'] = False
  6609	            return None
  6610	        facts['channel_closed'] = True
  6611	        facts['channel_reader_finished'] = getattr(self._channel,
  6612	                                                    'reader_finished', None)
  6613	        if not isinstance(channel_facts, dict):
  6614	            return None
  6615	        facts['write_end_closed'] = bool(channel_facts.get('write_end_closed'))
  6616	        facts['read_end_closed'] = bool(channel_facts.get('read_end_closed'))
  6617	        facts['channel_reader_error'] = channel_facts.get('reader_error')
  6618	        # The overflow of the channel is a **named** failure of the
  6619	        # round and it travels with the facts of the close: a channel
  6620	        # that overflowed is never a channel that ended normally.
  6621	        facts['channel_overflow'] = bool(channel_facts.get('overflow'))
  6622	        facts['channel_overflow_facts'] = dict(
  6623	            channel_facts.get('overflow_facts') or {})
  6624	        facts['channel_retained_workers'] = int(
  6625	            (channel_facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
  6626	            + (channel_facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
  6627	        facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  6628	            channel_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
  6629	        return channel_facts
  6630	
  6631	    def _cleanup_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  6632	        """The ONE absolute deadline of the cleanup of the broker.
  6633	
  6634	        The deadline is the one that the parent bound before it closed
  6635	        the handle.  It may only be shortened, never extended, and every
  6636	        wait of the cleanup recomputes the time that is left.
  6637	
  6638	        The **context** of the launch is read as well: a cleanup that
  6639	        runs after the round published its cleanup deadline uses that
  6640	        deadline and it never falls back to a fresh budget of its own.
  6641	        """
  6642	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  6643	        if value is None:
  6644	            value = self.write_deadline
  6645	        context = self._context
  6646	        if context is not None:
  6647	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
  6648	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
  6649	                if candidate is None:
  6650	                    continue
  6651	                if value is None or candidate < value:
  6652	                    value = candidate
  6653	        if value is None:
  6654	            value = self._clock() + CLEANUP_BUDGET_SECONDS
  6655	        elif self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
  6656	            value = self.write_deadline
  6657	        return value
  6658	
  6659	    def _release_process_handle(self, exited=False):
  6660	        """Release the original process handle, explicitly and by platform.
  6661	
  6662	        The release is recorded and it is never left to the garbage
  6663	        collector: on Windows the original handle of the process is closed
  6664	        through its own handle object and on POSIX the child process has
  6665	        no separate handle that the parent owns.
  6666	
  6667	        The original handle of the process is closed **only** when the
  6668	        exit of the process was really confirmed.  A process whose exit
  6669	        was not confirmed may still be alive and its original handle is
  6670	        the only handle that the parent owns for it: the handle stays
  6671	        with its owner and a later close may release it.  A close that
  6672	        succeeds is never a proof that the process ended.
  6673	        """
  6674	        if not exited:
  6675	            return {'process_handle_released': False,
  6676	                    'process_handle_release_method': 'EXIT_NOT_CONFIRMED'}
  6677	        if os.name == 'nt':
  6678	            handle = getattr(self._process, '_handle', None)
  6679	            close = getattr(handle, 'Close', None)
  6680	            if close is None:
  6681	                return {'process_handle_released': False,
  6682	                        'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_NO_EXPLICIT_HANDLE'}
  6683	            try:
  6684	                close()
  6685	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6686	                return {'process_handle_released': False,
  6687	                        'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED'}
  6688	            return {'process_handle_released': True,
  6689	                    'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_HANDLE_CLOSE'}
  6690	        return {'process_handle_released': True,
  6691	                'process_handle_release_method': 'POSIX_PROCESS_EXIT'}
  6692	
  6693	
  6694	class _LaunchAggregate:
  6695	    """The thin persistent owner of **one** launch.
  6696	
  6697	    Every launch of this package has exactly one aggregate and it is
  6698	    created **before** the first side effect of the launch.  It holds
  6699	    the original references of the launch: the process, the channel,
  6700	    the partial endpoints, the workers of the endpoints and the context
  6701	    of the launch.
  6702	
  6703	    The aggregate is the entry that the outer owner of a failed launch
  6704	    uses.  A construction that did not return, a channel whose reader
  6705	    thread did not start and a channel that could not be handed over
  6706	    are found **here** and never through a local variable that is
  6707	    ``None`` at that moment.
  6708	
  6709	    The release of the aggregate is ordered by the **dependency**: the
  6710	    channel owns the files of the process and the worker of an endpoint
  6711	    owns the handle of its thread, so the channel is coordinated first
  6712	    and the process is coordinated after it.  A process whose channel
  6713	    took the files is never closed by the process helper: a file that
  6714	    is closed under a live worker is a file that a worker reads from.
  6715	    """
  6716	
  6717	    def __init__(self, *, launch_id=None, custody=None, clock=None,
  6718	                 context=None):
  6719	        self.launch_id = launch_id or 'launch'
  6720	        self.custody = custody
  6721	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  6722	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
  6723	        self.process = None
  6724	        self.channel = None
  6725	        self.endpoints = []
  6726	        self.workers = []
  6727	        # The **original raw streams** of the launch that could not be
  6728	        # closed.  A raw stream is a file of the process of the round:
  6729	        # the launch that created it is its owner and it is retried by
  6730	        # that owner alone.  A stream that is not in this list was
  6731	        # closed with a confirmed close.
  6732	        self.raw_streams = []
  6733	        self.context = context
  6734	        self.facts = {}
  6735	        # Whether the exit of the process of this launch was already
  6736	        # **confirmed**.  A retry of the same owner coordinates only the
  6737	        # parts that were not confirmed yet: it never terminates and
  6738	        # never waits a second time.
  6739	        self.process_exit_confirmed = False
  6740	
  6741	    # -- the bindings of the launch ---------------------------------------
  6742	    def bind_process(self, process):
  6743	        """Bind the original process of this launch."""
  6744	        with self._lock:
  6745	            self.process = process
  6746	        return process
  6747	
  6748	    def bind_channel(self, channel):
  6749	        """Bind the channel of this launch."""
  6750	        with self._lock:
  6751	            if channel is not None:
  6752	                self.channel = channel
  6753	        return channel
  6754	
  6755	    def bind_endpoint(self, endpoint):
  6756	        """Bind one endpoint of this launch."""
  6757	        with self._lock:
  6758	            self.endpoints.append(endpoint)
  6759	        return endpoint
  6760	
  6761	    def bind_worker(self, worker):
  6762	        """Bind one worker of this launch."""
  6763	        with self._lock:
  6764	            self.workers.append(worker)
  6765	        return worker
  6766	
  6767	    def bind_context(self, context):
  6768	        """Bind the ONE context of this launch."""
  6769	        with self._lock:
  6770	            if context is not None:
  6771	                self.context = context
  6772	        return self.context
  6773	
  6774	    # -- the custody protocol of the launcher -----------------------------
  6775	    def retain(self, kind, resource, detail=None):
  6776	        """Take one original resource of this launch.
  6777	
  6778	        The protocol is the protocol of the table of the launcher: the
  6779	        channel of a launch registers itself **before** it builds
  6780	        anything, so that a partial channel is always reachable.
  6781	        """
  6782	        with self._lock:
  6783	            if kind == 'channel' and resource is not None:
  6784	                self.channel = resource
  6785	            elif kind == 'endpoint':
  6786	                self.endpoints.append(resource)
  6787	            elif kind == 'worker':
  6788	                self.workers.append(resource)
  6789	        return resource
  6790	
  6791	    def forget(self, resource):
  6792	        """Leave the launch: the resource was handed over or released."""
  6793	        with self._lock:
  6794	            if self.channel is resource:
  6795	                self.channel = None
  6796	            self.endpoints = [item for item in self.endpoints
  6797	                              if item is not resource]
  6798	            self.workers = [item for item in self.workers
  6799	                            if item is not resource]
  6800	        return None
  6801	
  6802	    def items(self):
  6803	        """The original resources that this launch still owns."""
  6804	        with self._lock:
  6805	            return {'process': self.process, 'channel': self.channel,
  6806	                    'endpoints': list(self.endpoints),
  6807	                    'workers': list(self.workers),
  6808	                    'raw_streams': list(self.raw_streams)}
  6809	
  6810	    # -- the ownership of the original raw streams -------------------------
  6811	    def retain_raw_stream(self, name, stream):
  6812	        """Take the ownership of one original raw stream of this launch.
  6813	
  6814	        A raw stream is a file of the process of the round.  It enters
  6815	        the ownership of **this** launch when its close could not be
  6816	        confirmed: the launch is then the only owner that may retry it
  6817	        and the table of the launcher never releases it behind the back
  6818	        of the launch.
  6819	        """
  6820	        with self._lock:
  6821	            for item in self.raw_streams:
  6822	                if item['stream'] is stream:
  6823	                    return item
  6824	            item = {'name': name, 'stream': stream}
  6825	            self.raw_streams.append(item)
  6826	            return item
  6827	
  6828	    def forget_raw_stream(self, stream):
  6829	        """Leave the ownership of one raw stream: its close was confirmed."""
  6830	        with self._lock:
  6831	            self.raw_streams = [item for item in self.raw_streams
  6832	                                if item['stream'] is not stream]
  6833	        return None
  6834	
  6835	    def pending_raw_streams(self):
  6836	        """The original raw streams that this launch still owns."""
  6837	        with self._lock:
  6838	            return list(self.raw_streams)
  6839	
  6840	    # -- the coordinated release ------------------------------------------
  6841	    def _release_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  6842	        """The absolute deadline that the release of this launch uses.
  6843	
  6844	        The deadline of the caller and the deadlines of the **original**
  6845	        context of this launch are one value: the earliest of them.  The
  6846	        context is read **again** at the moment of the release and it is
  6847	        never copied into a number of the launch: a layer that copied
  6848	        the number of a deadline would have a deadline of its own.
  6849	        """
  6850	        try:
  6851	            value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  6852	        except BrokerError:
  6853	            value = None
  6854	        context = self.context
  6855	        if context is not None:
  6856	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
  6857	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
  6858	                if candidate is None:
  6859	                    continue
  6860	                if value is None or candidate < value:
  6861	                    value = candidate
  6862	        return value
  6863	
  6864	    def release(self, deadline=None):
  6865	        """Coordinate the resources of this launch, in the dependency order.
  6866	
  6867	        The channel is coordinated **first** and the process is
  6868	        coordinated after it.  A close that returned is not a release:
  6869	        the channel is released only when **every** fact of it is
  6870	        closed - the reader, the two ends, the workers and the original
  6871	        handles of their threads.  An incomplete close keeps the
  6872	        channel with its owner and it is named.
  6873	
  6874	        The original handle of the process is released only after the
  6875	        exit of the process was really confirmed.
  6876	
  6877	        The release never raises before every safe step was executed:
  6878	        the original cancellation of the launch is raised at the end and
  6879	        the ordinary failures are named beside it.
  6880	        """
  6881	        released = []
  6882	        retained = []
  6883	        failures = {}
  6884	        cancellations = []
  6885	        # The original raw streams that this launch already owns and
  6886	        # whose close was not confirmed yet: they are retried by **this**
  6887	        # owner and by no other one.
  6888	        pending_raw = self.pending_raw_streams()
  6889	        channel = self.channel
  6890	        if channel is not None:
  6891	            facts = {}
  6892	            try:
  6893	                facts = channel.close()
  6894	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  6895	                cancellations.append(error)
  6896	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6897	                failures['channel'] = type(error).__name__
  6898	            if not isinstance(facts, dict):
  6899	                facts = {}
  6900	            complete = (bool(facts.get('reader_finished'))
  6901	                        and bool(facts.get('read_end_closed'))
  6902	                        and bool(facts.get('write_end_closed'))
  6903	                        and int(facts.get('channel_retained_workers') or 0) == 0
  6904	                        and bool(facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released')))
  6905	            if complete:
  6906	                released.append('channel')
  6907	                self.forget(channel)
  6908	                if self.custody is not None:
  6909	                    self.custody.forget(channel)
  6910	            else:
  6911	                # A close that returned is not a release: the channel
  6912	                # keeps its owner and its original reference.
  6913	                failures['channel'] = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE'
  6914	                retained.append('channel')
  6915	                if self.custody is not None:
  6916	                    self.custody.retain('channel', channel,
  6917	                                         'release_incomplete', launch=self)
  6918	        process = self.process
  6919	        if process is not None:
  6920	            # The files of the process belong to the channel when the
  6921	            # channel took them: the process helper never closes them.
  6922	            close_pipes = channel is None
  6923	            outcome = {'failures': {}, 'exited': False, 'retained_streams': []}
  6924	            try:
  6925	                # The deadline of the launch is read **again** after the
  6926	                # channel was coordinated: a cleanup deadline that was
  6927	                # published in the meantime shortens the wait of the
  6928	                # process and it is never a number that was copied
  6929	                # once.
  6930	                outcome = _release_launch_process(
  6931	                    process, deadline=self._release_deadline(deadline),
  6932	                    close_pipes=close_pipes,
  6933	                    exited=self.process_exit_confirmed,
  6934	                    context=self.context)
  6935	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  6936	                cancellations.append(error)
  6937	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6938	                outcome['failures']['process'] = type(error).__name__
  6939	            failures.update(outcome['failures'])
  6940	            # A raw stream that could not be closed stays a reachable
  6941	            # owned resource: it keeps its **original** reference and it
  6942	            # is never replaced by a summary of the failure.  This
  6943	            # launch is the owner of it and it retries it.
  6944	            for name, stream in outcome.get('retained_streams') or []:
  6945	                retained.append(name)
  6946	                self.retain_raw_stream(name, stream)
  6947	                if self.custody is not None:
  6948	                    self.custody.retain(name, stream, 'close_failed',
  6949	                                        launch=self)
  6950	            if outcome['exited']:
  6951	                self.process_exit_confirmed = True
  6952	            # The original process is forgotten **only** when its exit
  6953	            # was confirmed and the original handle of the process was
  6954	            # released.  A close of the handle that failed keeps the
  6955	            # process with its owner and the same owner may retry it.
  6956	            handle_released = 'handle' not in outcome['failures']
  6957	            if outcome['exited'] and handle_released:
  6958	                released.append('process')
  6959	                with self._lock:
  6960	                    self.process = None
  6961	                if self.custody is not None:
  6962	                    self.custody.forget(process)
  6963	            else:
  6964	                retained.append('process')
  6965	                if self.custody is not None:
  6966	                    self.custody.retain('process', process, 'release_failed',
  6967	                                         launch=self)
  6968	        # The retry of the original raw streams that this launch owns.
  6969	        # The retry happens **after** the channel and the process were
  6970	        # coordinated: a raw file of a process whose channel took the
  6971	        # files is never closed by the launch.
  6972	        self._release_raw_streams(pending_raw, released, retained, failures,
  6973	                                  cancellations, deadline)
  6974	        self.facts = {'released': list(released), 'retained': list(retained),
  6975	                      'failures': dict(failures)}
  6976	        if cancellations:
  6977	            # The original cancellation is raised after every safe step
  6978	            # of the release was executed and the ordinary failures stay
  6979	            # named next to it.
  6980	            raise cancellations[0]
  6981	        return self.facts
  6982	
  6983	    def _release_raw_streams(self, items, released, retained, failures,
  6984	                             cancellations, deadline):
  6985	        """Retry the original raw streams that this launch still owns.
  6986	
  6987	        A raw stream is a file of the process of the round.  It is owned
  6988	        by the launch that created it and it is retried **only** by that
  6989	        owner: the table of the launcher never closes it and it never
  6990	        becomes a detached entry that nobody may reach.
  6991	
  6992	        The retry happens under the **current** bound of the release:
  6993	        the deadline of the caller and the deadlines of the original
  6994	        context of the launch are read again and an expired bound never
  6995	        starts a new close.
  6996	
  6997	        A stream that is owned by a channel - a live channel or a
  6998	        partial one - is never closed by the launch: the files of the
  6999	        process belong to the channel and a file that is closed under a
  7000	        live worker is a file that a worker reads from.
  7001	
  7002	        A stream whose close is confirmed is forgotten **before** it
  7003	        leaves the table of the launcher: the confirmed close is
  7004	        recorded and the table never resurrects the stream.
  7005	        """
  7006	        if not items:
  7007	            return
  7008	        if self.channel is not None:
  7009	            # The channel owns the files of the process: the raw
  7010	            # streams are never closed under a live or a partial
  7011	            # channel endpoint.
  7012	            for item in items:
  7013	                retained.append(item['name'])
  7014	                failures['raw_stream'] = 'BROKER_RAW_STREAM_OWNED_BY_CHANNEL'
  7015	            return
  7016	        value = self._release_deadline(deadline)
  7017	        for item in items:
  7018	            stream = item['stream']
  7019	            if value is not None and self._clock() >= value:
  7020	                # An expired bound never starts a new close: the stream
  7021	                # keeps its owner and the round keeps the fact.
  7022	                failures['raw_stream'] = 'BROKER_RAW_STREAM_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED'
  7023	                retained.append(item['name'])
  7024	                continue
  7025	            try:
  7026	                stream.close()
  7027	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  7028	                # The original cancellation survives: it is raised after
  7029	                # every safe step of the release and the stream keeps
  7030	                # its owner.
  7031	                cancellations.append(error)
  7032	                failures['raw_stream'] = 'CANCELLED'
  7033	                retained.append(item['name'])
  7034	                continue
  7035	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7036	                failures['raw_stream'] = type(error).__name__
  7037	                retained.append(item['name'])
  7038	                continue
  7039	            # The close was confirmed: the stream is forgotten by its
  7040	            # owner and by the table of the launcher.
  7041	            self.forget_raw_stream(stream)
  7042	            if self.custody is not None:
  7043	                self.custody.forget(stream)
  7044	            released.append(item['name'])
  7045	
  7046	
  7047	class _LauncherCustody:
  7048	    """The persistent owner of the original resources of a failed launch.
  7049	
  7050	    The launcher owns the original process of the round until the
  7051	    channel of the round exists.  A channel that could not be built
  7052	    would leave the launcher with a running process and with pipes that
  7053	    nobody owns.
  7054	
  7055	    Every original resource of such a launch enters this table **with
  7056	    its original reference** and it stays there until a confirmed
  7057	    release: the round never loses the ownership of a resource that it
  7058	    created, and a pid is never used to find a process again.
  7059	
  7060	    The table is the recovery entry point of the launcher.  It is
  7061	    explicit and it is finite: a caller that wants the resources of a
  7062	    failed launch released asks for it.
  7063	
  7064	    The resources of a launch are coordinated by the **aggregate** of
  7065	    that launch and not by the order of this table: the channel of a
  7066	    launch is closed before the process of that launch, because the
  7067	    channel owns the files of the process.  A resource that belongs to
  7068	    a launch is never released a second time by this table.
  7069	    """
  7070	
  7071	    def __init__(self):
  7072	        self._items = []
  7073	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
  7074	        # The ONE context of the round, when the table is bound to it.
  7075	        # A recovery that runs without a deadline of its own reads the
  7076	        # absolute deadline and the published cleanup deadline of the
  7077	        # round from it and it never opens a fresh budget.
  7078	        self._context = None
  7079	
  7080	    def bind_context(self, context):
  7081	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to this table."""
  7082	        if context is not None:
  7083	            self._context = context
  7084	        return self._context
  7085	
  7086	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  7087	        """The ONE deadline that a release of this table really uses."""
  7088	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  7089	        context = self._context
  7090	        if context is not None:
  7091	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
  7092	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
  7093	                if candidate is None:
  7094	                    continue
  7095	                if value is None or candidate < value:
  7096	                    value = candidate
  7097	        return value
  7098	
  7099	    def retain(self, kind, resource, detail=None, launch=None):
  7100	        """Take one original resource into the table of the launcher.
  7101	
  7102	        The table never names the same resource twice: a resource that
  7103	        is already with its owner is not entered a second time and the
  7104	        table converges.
  7105	
  7106	        The ``launch`` is the aggregate that owns the resource.  A
  7107	        resource that belongs to a launch is coordinated by that
  7108	        launch and it is never released a second time by this table.
  7109	        """
  7110	        with self._lock:
  7111	            for item in self._items:
  7112	                if item['kind'] == kind and item['resource'] is resource:
  7113	                    return resource
  7114	            self._items.append({'kind': kind, 'resource': resource,
  7115	                                'detail': detail, 'launch': launch})
  7116	        return resource
  7117	
  7118	    def forget(self, resource):
  7119	        """Leave the table: the resource was handed over to its owner."""
  7120	        with self._lock:
  7121	            remaining = [item for item in self._items
  7122	                         if item['resource'] is not resource]
  7123	            self._items = remaining
  7124	
  7125	    def items(self):
  7126	        """The original resources that the launcher still owns."""
  7127	        with self._lock:
  7128	            return list(self._items)
  7129	
  7130	    def release(self, deadline=None, context=None):
  7131	        """Try to release every original resource of the table.
  7132	
  7133	        The release of every resource is independent of the others: a
  7134	        resource that could not be released stays in the table with its
  7135	        original reference and it is named in the report.
  7136	
  7137	        The **launches** are coordinated first: every aggregate closes
  7138	        the channel of its launch before the process of that launch.
  7139	        The resources that belong to no launch are released afterwards
  7140	        and a resource of a launch is never released twice.
  7141	
  7142	        The wait of every resource uses the **same** absolute deadline.
  7143	        A recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never waits a
  7144	        fixed number of seconds for a process that may still be alive.
  7145	        """
  7146	        released = []
  7147	        retained = []
  7148	        cancellations = []
  7149	        with self._lock:
  7150	            items = list(self._items)
  7151	        keep = []
  7152	        for item in items:
  7153	            if item['kind'] == 'aggregate':
  7154	                launch = item['resource']
  7155	                try:
  7156	                    # The deadline of the table is read **again** for
  7157	                    # every launch: a cleanup deadline that was
  7158	                    # published in the meantime shortens the release of
  7159	                    # the launch.
  7160	                    report = launch.release(
  7161	                        deadline=self._effective_deadline(deadline))
  7162	                except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  7163	                    cancellations.append(error)
  7164	                    report = {'released': [], 'retained': ['launch']}
  7165	                except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7166	                    report = {'released': [], 'retained': ['launch']}
  7167	                released.extend(report.get('released') or [])
  7168	                names = list(report.get('retained') or [])
  7169	                retained.extend(names)
  7170	                if names:
  7171	                    keep.append(item)
  7172	                continue
  7173	            if item.get('launch') is not None:
  7174	                # The launch of this resource coordinates it: the table
  7175	                # never releases it a second time.  A resource that the
  7176	                # launch released **while** this release was running is
  7177	                # not named by the table any more: the stale snapshot of
  7178	                # the table never resurrects it and never names it as
  7179	                # retained.
  7180	                with self._lock:
  7181	                    owned = any(current['resource'] is item['resource']
  7182	                                for current in self._items)
  7183	                if not owned:
  7184	                    continue
  7185	                keep.append(item)
  7186	                retained.append(item['kind'])
  7187	                continue
  7188	            resource = item['resource']
  7189	            try:
  7190	                if item['kind'] == 'process':
  7191	                    outcome = _release_launch_process(
  7192	                        resource, deadline=self._effective_deadline(deadline),
  7193	                        close_pipes=True, context=self._context)
  7194	                    if outcome['failures']:
  7195	                        keep.append(item)
  7196	                        retained.append(item['kind'])
  7197	                        # A raw stream that could not be closed stays a
  7198	                        # reachable owned resource of the launcher.
  7199	                        for name, stream in outcome.get('retained_streams') or []:
  7200	                            self.retain(name, stream, 'close_failed')
  7201	                        continue
  7202	                else:
  7203	                    close = getattr(resource, 'close', None)
  7204	                    if callable(close):
  7205	                        close()
  7206	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7207	                keep.append(item)
  7208	                retained.append(item['kind'])
  7209	                continue
  7210	            released.append(item['kind'])
  7211	        with self._lock:
  7212	            # The table is rebuilt from the entries that are **still**
  7213	            # in it: an entry that was forgotten while the release ran -
  7214	            # a process that the aggregate of its launch released - is
  7215	            # never resurrected as a detached entry.  The entries that
  7216	            # were added while the release ran are new resources of the
  7217	            # round and they are kept.
  7218	            kept = {id(item['resource']) for item in keep}
  7219	            snapshot = {id(item['resource']) for item in items}
  7220	            self._items = [item for item in self._items
  7221	                           if id(item['resource']) not in snapshot
  7222	                           or id(item['resource']) in kept]
  7223	        if cancellations:
  7224	            raise cancellations[0]
  7225	        return {'released': list(released), 'retained': list(retained)}
  7226	
  7227	
  7228	#: The one table of the original resources of the launches of this
  7229	#: package.  It is empty after a launch that was handed over and it
  7230	#: names the resources of a launch that was not.
  7231	_LAUNCHER_CUSTODY = _LauncherCustody()
  7232	
  7233	
  7234	def release_retained_launches(deadline=None, *, context=None):
  7235	    """The recovery entry point of the launcher of this package.
  7236	
  7237	    The caller asks for the original resources of the launches that were
  7238	    not handed over to be released, and the report names the resources
  7239	    that could not be released.
  7240	
  7241	    The wait of every resource uses the **same** absolute deadline of the
  7242	    round: a recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never
  7243	    waits a fixed number of seconds for a process that may still be
  7244	    alive.
  7245	
  7246	    The deadline of the context is **read again** here: a recovery
  7247	    that runs after the round published its cleanup deadline uses that
  7248	    deadline and it never falls back to a fresh budget of its own.
  7249	    """
  7250	    value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  7251	    if context is not None:
  7252	        _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.bind_context(context)
  7253	    return _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.release(deadline=value)
  7254	
  7255	
  7256	def _launcher_custody_items():
  7257	    """The original resources that the launcher of this package owns.
  7258	
  7259	    The list is a copy and every entry carries the **original** object:
  7260	    a pid is never used to find a process of the round again.
  7261	    """
  7262	    return _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.items()
  7263	
  7264	
  7265	def _release_budget(deadline=None, context=None):
  7266	    """The seconds that are left for the wait of a process of a launch.
  7267	
  7268	    The deadline of the caller and the deadlines of the **original**
  7269	    context of the launch are one value: the earliest of them.  The
  7270	    context is read **again** at the moment of the wait: a cleanup
  7271	    deadline that was published while the channel of the launch was
  7272	    closed shortens the wait of the process.  A recovery never opens a
  7273	    budget of its own and it never waits a fixed number of seconds for
  7274	    a process that may still be alive.
  7275	
  7276	    The bound of the package is used only when neither the caller nor
  7277	    the context of the launch brought a deadline at all.
  7278	    """
  7279	    try:
  7280	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  7281	    except BrokerError:
  7282	        # A deadline that cannot be understood is no deadline: the
  7283	        # bound of the package is used and it is never a budget that
  7284	        # the caller did not ask for.
  7285	        value = None
  7286	    if context is not None:
  7287	        for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
  7288	                          context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
  7289	            if candidate is None:
  7290	                continue
  7291	            if value is None or candidate < value:
  7292	                value = candidate
  7293	    if value is None:
  7294	        return CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS
  7295	    return max(0.0, value - time.monotonic())
  7296	
  7297	
  7298	def _release_launch_process(process, *, deadline=None, close_pipes=True,
  7299	                            exited=None, context=None):
  7300	    """Terminate one process of a launch and report the two facts.
  7301	
  7302	    The release is explicit and it is bounded: the process is
  7303	    terminated, its exit is confirmed and its pipes are closed when
  7304	    nobody else owns them.
  7305	
  7306	    The wait uses the **same** absolute deadline of the round: the
  7307	    deadline is read **again** from the context of the launch at the
  7308	    moment of the wait and a recovery never opens a budget of its own.
  7309	
  7310	    The ``exited`` argument is the exit that an **earlier** attempt of
  7311	    the same owner already confirmed: a retry coordinates only the
  7312	    parts that were not confirmed yet and it never terminates and
  7313	    never waits a second time.
  7314	
  7315	    The original handle of the process is closed **only** when its exit
  7316	    was really confirmed.  A process that may still be alive keeps its
  7317	    handle and its owner: the handle is the only thing that may stop
  7318	    it and it is never closed under it.
  7319	
  7320	    The report names every step that did not succeed and it names the
  7321	    **exit** of the process as a separate fact: a close that succeeded
  7322	    is never a proof that the process ended.  A raw stream that could
  7323	    not be closed is returned with its **original** reference: it stays
  7324	    a reachable owned resource and it is never replaced by a summary.
  7325	    """
  7326	    failures = {}
  7327	    retained_streams = []
  7328	    confirmed = bool(exited)
  7329	    if not confirmed:
  7330	        try:
  7331	            process.terminate()
  7332	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7333	            failures['terminate'] = type(error).__name__
  7334	        budget = _release_budget(deadline, context)
  7335	        try:
  7336	            process.wait(timeout=budget)
  7337	            confirmed = True
  7338	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7339	            failures['wait'] = type(error).__name__
  7340	    if confirmed and os.name == 'nt':
  7341	        # The original handle of the process is closed explicitly and it
  7342	        # is never left to the garbage collector.  A handle that could
  7343	        # not be closed keeps its owner.
  7344	        handle = getattr(process, '_handle', None)
  7345	        closer = getattr(handle, 'Close', None)
  7346	        if callable(closer):
  7347	            try:
  7348	                closer()
  7349	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7350	                failures['handle'] = type(error).__name__
  7351	    if close_pipes:
  7352	        for name in ('stdin', 'stdout', 'stderr'):
  7353	            stream = getattr(process, name, None)
  7354	            if stream is None:
  7355	                continue
  7356	            try:
  7357	                stream.close()
  7358	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7359	                failures[name] = type(error).__name__
  7360	                # The stream keeps its **original** reference: it stays
  7361	                # a reachable owned resource of the round.
  7362	                retained_streams.append((name, stream))
  7363	    return {'failures': failures, 'exited': confirmed,
  7364	            'retained_streams': retained_streams}
  7365	
  7366	
  7367	def _terminate_and_close_process(process, *, deadline=None, close_pipes=True,
  7368	                                 context=None):
  7369	    """Terminate one process of this round and close its pipes.
  7370	
  7371	    The release is explicit and it is bounded: the process is
  7372	    terminated, its exit is confirmed and its pipes are closed.  The
  7373	    method never raises: it is the last step of a failure and it never
  7374	    replaces the failure that is on the way out.
  7375	
  7376	    The wait uses the **same** absolute deadline of the round: a
  7377	    recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never waits a fixed
  7378	    number of seconds for a process that may still be alive.
  7379	
  7380	    The original handle of the process is closed **only** when its exit
  7381	    was really confirmed.  A process that may still be alive keeps its
  7382	    handle and its owner: the handle is the only thing that may stop
  7383	    it and it is never closed under it.
  7384	
  7385	    The report names every step that did not succeed, so that the
  7386	    caller can keep the original reference of a resource that is still
  7387	    open.
  7388	    """
  7389	    return _release_launch_process(process, deadline=deadline,
  7390	                                   close_pipes=close_pipes,
  7391	                                   context=context)['failures']
  7392	
  7393	
  7394	def _adopt_broker_process(process, *, channel_factory, executable,
  7395	                          executable_sha256, deadline=None, aggregate=None,
  7396	                          custody=None):
  7397	    """Take the original process of the broker into the custody of a channel.
  7398	
  7399	    The launcher owns the original process of the round until the
  7400	    channel of the round exists.  A channel that could not be built
  7401	    would leave the launcher with a running process and with pipes that
  7402	    nobody owns.
  7403	
  7404	    The **channel** is the only owner of the files of the process.  A
  7405	    channel that exists and that could not be handed over is closed by
  7406	    the launcher through the channel and never by closing the standard
  7407	    input and the standard output of the process by itself: a file that
  7408	    is closed under a live worker of the channel is a file that a
  7409	    worker is reading from.
  7410	
  7411	    The **aggregate** of the launch is the owner that the outer code
  7412	    uses: a partial channel - a channel whose reader thread did not
  7413	    start - is found through the aggregate and never through a local
  7414	    variable that is ``None`` at that moment.
  7415	
  7416	    A resource that could not be released keeps its **original**
  7417	    reference in the table of the launcher: the session never received
  7418	    the process and the round never loses the ownership of it.
  7419	
  7420	    The handover happens exactly once: the process that the channel
  7421	    received is the process that the parent owns.
  7422	    """
  7423	    table = custody if custody is not None else _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY
  7424	    launch = aggregate if aggregate is not None else _LaunchAggregate(
  7425	        custody=table)
  7426	    launch.bind_process(process)
  7427	    # The launch is registered with the table of the launcher: the
  7428	    # recovery of the table coordinates the resources of the launch in
  7429	    # the dependency order - the channel before the process.
  7430	    table.retain('aggregate', launch, 'launch')
  7431	    table.retain('process', process, 'popened', launch=launch)
  7432	    channel = None
  7433	    try:
  7434	        channel = channel_factory()
  7435	        if channel is not None:
  7436	            launch.bind_channel(channel)
  7437	        broker = BrokerProcess(process, executable, executable_sha256, channel,
  7438	                               context=launch.context)
  7439	    except BaseException as error:
  7440	        # The resources of the launch are coordinated by the aggregate:
  7441	        # the channel first and the process after it.  The resources
  7442	        # that could not be released keep their original reference and
  7443	        # their owner.
  7444	        try:
  7445	            launch.release(deadline=deadline)
  7446	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as cancellation:
  7447	            # The cancellation of the release never replaces the
  7448	            # original failure of the launch: the original object
  7449	            # continues to propagate and the cancellation stays
  7450	            # **reachable** next to it.  The primary failure and the
  7451	            # cancellation of the recovery coexist.
  7452	            try:
  7453	                error.lvw_cancelled = cancellation
  7454	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7455	                pass
  7456	        except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7457	            pass
  7458	        raise
  7459	    # The handover happened: the launcher does not own the process of
  7460	    # the round any more.
  7461	    table.forget(process)
  7462	    table.forget(channel)
  7463	    table.forget(launch)
  7464	    launch.forget(channel)
  7465	    launch.forget(process)
  7466	    return broker
  7467	
  7468	
  7469	def _check_launch_budget(context, stage):
  7470	    """Check the budget of the round before a side effect of a launch.
  7471	
  7472	    The check is asked **before** the first side effect of the launch
  7473	    - the creation of the directory, the writing of the plan and the
  7474	    creation of the process - and it is asked **again** after the
  7475	    preparatory work and before the next effect.  A launch that starts
  7476	    after the deadline of the round arrived never creates a process.
  7477	
  7478	    The **original** context of the round is the authority: the
  7479	    launcher never keeps a copy of the deadline and it never opens a
  7480	    budget of its own.  The authority is the **effective minimum** of
  7481	    the context - the absolute deadline of the round **and** the
  7482	    published cleanup deadline of the round - and not the absolute
