  7483	    deadline alone: a round that published its cleanup deadline and
  7484	    whose cleanup expired is a round that may not create a process any
  7485	    more, even when its absolute lease is still in the future.  The
  7486	    stop of the round, the cancel of the round and the unknown state of
  7487	    the round are honoured here as well.
  7488	    """
  7489	    if context is None:
  7490	        return True
  7491	    if context.cancel.is_set() or context.is_unknown():
  7492	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', stage)
  7493	    if context.stopping.is_set():
  7494	        # The public stop of the round published the stop of the round:
  7495	        # a launch that continues after it would create a process that
  7496	        # nobody may stop any more.
  7497	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', stage)
  7498	    deadline = context.effective_deadline()
  7499	    if deadline is not None and context.clock() >= deadline:
  7500	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', stage)
  7501	    return True
  7502	
  7503	
  7504	def launch_broker(plan, run_root, nonce, *, context=None):
  7505	    """Start the broker process of one round and keep its original handle.
  7506	
  7507	    The launcher is a Windows only operation.  It refuses on every other
  7508	    platform before anything is started: a broker that cannot be started
  7509	    on Windows is never replaced by an unconstrained process and never by
  7510	    a simulation that pretends to be one.
  7511	
  7512	    The nonce and the plan are checked **before** the first directory and
  7513	    the first file of the round exist, so that a refused launch leaves
  7514	    nothing behind.
  7515	
  7516	    The ONE context of the round is held **before** the first side
  7517	    effect of the launch: the creation of the process, the reading of
  7518	    the executable, the channel and its endpoints all read the deadline
  7519	    of the round from that context.  A launcher that waits for the end
  7520	    of the launcher to bind the deadline would leave a window in which
  7521	    the process of the round runs without a budget.
  7522	
  7523	    The **aggregate** of the launch is created before the process and it
  7524	    holds every original resource of the launch: a failure at any point
  7525	    of the launch finds the partial owner of the launch through it.
  7526	    """
  7527	    if os.name != 'nt':
  7528	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLATFORM_UNSUPPORTED')
  7529	    if plan is None:
  7530	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_INVALID')
  7531	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(nonce)):
  7532	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_NONCE_INVALID')
  7533	    # The aggregate of this launch and the deadline of the round are
  7534	    # taken **before** the first side effect of the launch.
  7535	    launch = _LaunchAggregate(custody=_LAUNCHER_CUSTODY, context=context)
  7536	    launch.bind_context(context)
  7537	    # The table of the launcher reads the deadline of the round from
  7538	    # the same context: a recovery of the launcher never opens a fresh
  7539	    # budget of its own.
  7540	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.bind_context(context)
  7541	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.retain('aggregate', launch, 'launch')
  7542	    deadline = None
  7543	    if context is not None:
  7544	        deadline = context.absolute_deadline
  7545	    root = Path(run_root)
  7546	    raw = plan.sealed_bytes()
  7547	    digest = plan.sealed_sha256()
  7548	    directory = root / 'temp' / 'native'
  7549	    # The budget of the round is checked **before** the first side
  7550	    # effect of the launch: a launch that starts after the deadline of
  7551	    # the round arrived creates no directory and no file.
  7552	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_DIRECTORY')
  7553	    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  7554	    plan_path = directory / 'custody-plan.json'
  7555	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_PLAN')
  7556	    with plan_path.open('xb') as stream:
  7557	        stream.write(raw)
  7558	    command = build_broker_command(plan_path=plan_path, plan_sha256=digest,
  7559	                                   run_root=root, nonce=nonce)
  7560	    # The budget is checked **again** after the preparatory work and
  7561	    # before the creation of the process: a plan file that consumed the
  7562	    # whole lease of the round never produces a process.
  7563	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_POPEN')
  7564	    process = subprocess.Popen(command, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
  7565	                               stderr=None, close_fds=True)
  7566	    # The original process of the round is with the launcher from the
  7567	    # moment of its creation.  The window between the creation of the
  7568	    # process and the handover of the channel is not a window without an
  7569	    # owner: a cancellation of the reading of the executable leaves the
  7570	    # process with its owner and it never disappears.
  7571	    launch.bind_process(process)
  7572	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.retain('process', process, 'popened', launch=launch)
  7573	    executable = Path(command[0])
  7574	    try:
  7575	        executable_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
  7576	    except OSError:
  7577	        executable_sha256 = None
  7578	    except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
  7579	        # The cancellation of the reading of the executable is cleaned
  7580	        # up and it continues to propagate.  The process keeps its owner
  7581	        # in the table of the launcher and the recovery of the launcher
  7582	        # is the entry that releases it.
  7583	        raise
  7584	
  7585	    def channel_factory():
  7586	        """Build the channel of this launch with the context of the round.
  7587	
  7588	        The channel registers itself with the **aggregate** of the
  7589	        launch before it builds anything: a channel whose reader
  7590	        thread did not start is found by the outer owner through the
  7591	        aggregate and the files of the process are never closed by the
  7592	        process helper.
  7593	        """
  7594	        channel = StreamChannel(process.stdout, process.stdin,
  7595	                                custody=launch, context=context)
  7596	        if deadline is not None:
  7597	            channel.bind_deadline(deadline)
  7598	        return channel
  7599	
  7600	    return _adopt_broker_process(
  7601	        process,
  7602	        channel_factory=channel_factory,
  7603	        executable=str(executable), executable_sha256=executable_sha256,
  7604	        deadline=deadline, aggregate=launch)
  7605	
  7606	
  7607	def read_sealed_plan(path, digest):
  7608	    """Read one sealed plan document and check its digest."""
  7609	    target = Path(path)
  7610	    if not target.is_file():
  7611	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_MISSING')
  7612	    with target.open('rb') as stream:
  7613	        raw = stream.read(MAX_PLAN_BYTES + 1)
  7614	    if len(raw) > MAX_PLAN_BYTES:
  7615	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_TOO_LARGE')
  7616	    if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != digest:
  7617	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_DRIFT')
  7618	    try:
  7619	        document = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'))
  7620	    except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError) as error:
  7621	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_INVALID') from error
  7622	    return document
  7623	
  7624	
  7625	def main(argv=None):
  7626	    """The production entry of the broker.
  7627	
  7628	    The entry is fixed: one sealed plan, its digest, the run root of the
  7629	    round and the nonce of the session.  The plan is validated again here,
  7630	    because the broker never trusts a document that it did not check.
  7631	    """
  7632	    import argparse
  7633	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
  7634	    parser.add_argument('--plan', required=True)
  7635	    parser.add_argument('--plan-sha256', required=True)
  7636	    parser.add_argument('--run-root', required=True)
  7637	    parser.add_argument('--nonce', required=True)
  7638	    arguments = parser.parse_args(argv)
  7639	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(arguments.nonce)):
  7640	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_NONCE_INVALID\n')
  7641	        sys.stderr.flush()
  7642	        return 2
  7643	    document = read_sealed_plan(arguments.plan, arguments.plan_sha256)
  7644	    try:
  7645	        plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
  7646	    except CustodyError as error:
  7647	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
  7648	        sys.stderr.flush()
  7649	        return 2
  7650	    if plan.sealed_sha256() != arguments.plan_sha256:
  7651	        # The seal of the plan that the broker validated is not the seal
  7652	        # that the parent wrote: the document drifted on its way here.
  7653	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_PLAN_SEAL_DRIFT\n')
  7654	        sys.stderr.flush()
  7655	        return 2
  7656	    try:
  7657	        win32 = WindowsApi()
  7658	    except BrokerError as error:
  7659	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
  7660	        sys.stderr.flush()
  7661	        return 2
  7662	    core = BrokerCore(win32=win32, plan=plan, run_root=arguments.run_root)
  7663	    loop = BrokerLoop(core=core, transport=None, plan=plan,
  7664	                      nonce=arguments.nonce)
  7665	    # The **absolute deadline** of the round is fixed and bound to the
  7666	    # core **before** the transport of the round exists.  The reader of
  7667	    # the channel and the workers of the endpoints therefore never
  7668	    # start with an unbound context: the child has no window in which
  7669	    # a read of the channel runs without a deadline.
  7670	    deadline = loop.fix_deadline()
  7671	    core.bind_absolute_deadline(deadline)
  7672	    # The ONE context of the round is handed to the transport of the
  7673	    # channel and to its endpoints: the channel, the endpoints and the
  7674	    # workers of the endpoints read the same absolute deadline and none
  7675	    # of them keeps a budget of its own.
  7676	    transport = StreamChannel(sys.stdin.buffer, sys.stdout.buffer, api=win32,
  7677	                              context=core.context)
  7678	    transport.bind_deadline(deadline)
  7679	    loop.bind_transport(transport)
  7680	    try:
  7681	        return loop.run()
  7682	    finally:
  7683	        # The round releases its own resources in the loop; the entry only
  7684	        # closes the channel and never swallows a failure of the round.
  7685	        try:
  7686	            transport.close()
  7687	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7688	            pass
  7689	
  7690	
  7691	if __name__ == '__main__':
  7692	    sys.exit(main())
